Conclusion: 在现有训练规模下，仅无湍流时能准确读出电荷；电荷与湍流都作用于散斑颗粒尺寸，可能限制了读出精度。实验验证及向矢量涡旋光束的扩展留待未来工作。

Abstract: Fractional topological charge (TC) enlarges the usable mode set of orbital angular momentum (OAM) links, but reading it reliably after atmospheric turbulence remains difficult. Interferometric and diffractive methods need an undistorted beam and a careful alignment, while most learned approaches treat integer or coarsely spaced charges and do not include turbulence. Here we ask whether the charge of a scalar vortex beam can be regressed directly from a single speckle image, and at which turbulence strength this stops working. We propagate vortex beams over 1500~m through 150 von Kármán phase screens, scatter them with a fixed random diffuser, and record $256\times256$, 16-bit speckle images for TC from 0 to 10 in steps of 0.01 and turbulence strengths $D/r_0$ from 0 to 50; the results reported here use a subset of 20\,000 images, and a data set of $10^{6}$ images is in preparation. A convolutional neural network estimates the TC and, through an auxiliary head, the turbulence category. On charges withheld from training, the mean absolute error is 0.031 without turbulence (with a common intensity scale) and rises to 0.78 at $D/r_0=10$ and 1.85 at $D/r_0=50$, against 2.5 for a constant prediction, and the turbulence category is identified correctly in 78\% of the images. The results show that, at the present training size, the charge is read accurately only without turbulence, and that the charge and the turbulence oth act on the speckle grain size, which may limit the readout. Experimental validation and the extension to vector vortex beams are left for future work.

</details>


### [104] [Tune for Retention, Not Just Gain: SFT Forgets Far Less than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34579)
*Nirmit Joshi,Nathan Srebro,Eran Malach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战"SFT因off-policy而必然导致灾难性遗忘、on-policy学习是持续学习关键"的流行观点，指出仅通过联合调节学习率与批大小、以"增益+保持"为准则选超参，朴素SFT即可在获得新任务提升的同时几乎不遗忘，性能可与on-policy的自蒸馏微调(SDFT)竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难性遗忘是语言模型微调的核心难题。近期工作将SFT的遗忘归因于其off-policy性质，认为on-policy学习才是持续学习的关键。作者质疑这一前提，希望厘清遗忘究竟由任务目标最大化还是优化配置驱动。

Method: 在SFT中系统性调整超参数（降低学习率、减小批大小），以联合考虑新任务增益与旧能力保持的准则选择配置，并与on-policy方法SDFT进行对比；同时对SDFT也施加保持感知的超参选择准则，以公平比较。

Result: 仅追求新任务性能最大化会诱发显著遗忘，但较低学习率与小批大小可在相近增益下实现几乎无遗忘。超参联合选择后，朴素SFT的保持能力远优于此前报告的结论，可与SDFT相竞争；SDFT对超参更鲁棒，但同样需要保持感知准则来避免灾难性遗忘的配置。

Conclusion: on-policy训练并非实现"学习且保持"的必要条件，灾难性遗忘在很大程度上取决于超参数与优化配置。这动摇了现有归因，并提出应当重新审视微调中遗忘的真正驱动因素。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting, the drastic loss of previously acquired capabilities when learning new ones, is a central challenge in language model fine-tuning. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely believed to be prone to such forgetting. Recent works have attributed this to the off-policy nature of SFT, arguing that on-policy learning is key to continual learning. In this paper, we challenge this premise. We show that while maximizing new-task performance can induce catastrophic forgetting, alternative hyperparameter choices (lower learning rate and batch size) can yield similar gains with little to no forgetting. With hyperparameters selected jointly for gain and retention, vanilla SFT retains prior capabilities far better than previously suggested and can be competitive with Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT), an on-policy approach designed to mitigate forgetting. While SDFT can be more robust to hyperparameter choices, it also requires a retention-aware criterion to identify hyperparameter configurations that avoid catastrophic forgetting. Our results challenge the view that on-policy training is what enables learning with retention and raise broader questions about what drives catastrophic forgetting during fine-tuning.

</details>


### [105] [ChemoMAE: Self-Supervised Spectral Representation Learning Reveals Chemical Heterogeneity in Historical Wood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34581)
*Ryuji Yamaguchi,Miyuki Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChemoMAE——一种基于近红外高光谱成像的自监督Transformer，通过掩码去噪学习像素级光谱表征，并结合聚类与Local Label Agreement (LLA)指标，实现跨树种、跨服役历史的古建筑木材降解模式的比较与空间映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史木构建筑中的木材历经数百年多种环境因素作用，且化学组成随解剖区域而异，形成复杂的降解模式。有效保护与修复需理解木材构件的降解机制，但现有认识不清；同时不同树种与服役历史的样本间缺乏统一比较基础。

Method: 对日本多座历史建筑的49个木材样本横切面进行近红外高光谱成像；采用自监督Transformer（ChemoMAE），利用光谱变量间的多重共线性，通过掩码去噪学习像素级光谱表征；对表征聚类生成无需预设化学状态类别的空间图；提出LLA（Local Label Agreement）量化相邻像素标签一致性，以光谱单独评估。

Result: 在验证样本上，ChemoMAE仅用光谱即取得高于基线方法的平均LLA；在全部样本上重新训练并拟合聚类中心后，所得空间图呈现出与木材解剖结构及表面暴露预期效应一致的结构。

Conclusion: 结合木材解剖学、服役历史与既有研究，对区域光谱与空间分布进行交叉比对，提出内部与表面降解的机制性假设；该探索性AI for Science框架为将自监督学到的潜在模式与领域知识关联、研究材料状态形成机制提供了基础。

Abstract: In historical timber buildings, multiple environmental factors act over centuries on wood whose chemical composition varies across anatomical regions, producing complex patterns of degradation. Understanding how timber members degrade is important for the effective conservation and restoration of historical buildings, yet the mechanisms underlying these patterns remain unclear. To investigate these mechanisms, we used self-supervised learning to establish a common basis for comparing specimens from timber members that differ in wood species and service history. We examined transverse sections of 49 wood specimens from several historical buildings in Japan using near-infrared hyperspectral imaging. ChemoMAE, a self-supervised Transformer, learns pixelwise spectral representations through masked denoising that exploits multicollinearity among spectral variables. Clustering these representations yields spatial maps without predefined chemical-state categories. Because anatomy and degradation can produce spatially continuous regions, we formulated Local Label Agreement (LLA) to quantify agreement among labels assigned to neighboring pixels. Using spectra alone, ChemoMAE yielded higher mean LLA than baseline methods on validation specimens. We then trained ChemoMAE anew and fitted cluster centers across all specimens. Spatial structures consistent with wood anatomy and the expected effects of surface exposure emerged in the resulting maps. By cross-referencing regional spectra and spatial distributions across specimens in light of wood anatomy, service histories, and prior studies, we proposed models of internal and surface degradation as mechanistic hypotheses. This exploratory AI for Science framework gives researchers a basis for linking latent patterns learned through self-supervision with domain knowledge when investigating how material states form.

</details>


### [106] [Learning from Crowds with Unsupervised Item Response Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34602)
*Pascal Wullschleger,Majid Zarharan,Donnacha Daly,Marc Pouly,Jennifer Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将众包标注建模为无监督IRT问题，系统评估无监督IRT模型在潜伏特质恢复与标签推断上的表现，并探讨用IRT为分类器训练提供信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包标注中标注者能力与任务难度异质，标准IRT模型天然契合这一结构，但无监督IRT在众包场景下的可靠性及其对下游分类器的价值尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 在六个带已知真值的众包标注数据集上，将无监督逻辑IRT变体与基于真值训练的有监督变体对比，评估标注者/项目特质恢复与标签推断；并提出IRT信息引导的课程式训练方法，与现有众包学习方法在F1与校准度上比较。

Result: 无监督IRT在多数数据集上能可靠恢复能力、难度、区分度等特质；标签推断比多数基线更稳定但未必最优；众包学习方法可提升F1，但无一方法能同时显著改善F1与校准度，F1最优方法往往校准较差。

Conclusion: 无监督IRT是众包标注的稳健工具，但需依数据集权衡；IRT引导的课程方法在校准上更有优势，而F1与校准之间存在权衡，不存在全面占优的众包学习方法。

Abstract: Item Response Theory (IRT) is the study of tests, including the properties of both test items and test takers. Crowdsourced labelling can be cast as an unsupervised IRT problem: annotators play the role of test takers with varying ability, and annotation tasks play the role of items with varying difficulty. We evaluate unsupervised variants of standard logistic IRT models for latent trait recovery and label inference on six datasets with crowdsourced labels and known ground truths. Unsupervised IRT models can recover annotator and item traits (e.g., ability, difficulty and discrimination) reliably across most datasets when compared to supervised variants trained on the ground truth. IRT models seem to be more stable in label inference compared to most baselines, but might not be the best possible choice depending on the dataset. We further propose to use IRT models to inform classifiers during training and compare against existing crowd-learning methods. We find that crowd-learning methods can improve F1 over majority-vote baselines, but no method significantly improves both F1 and calibration. IRT-informed curriculum methods seem to preserve calibration better than other crowd-learning methods, but are not at the frontier regarding F1, and the best crowd-learning methods in terms of F1 seem to be miscalibrated.

</details>


### [107] [Automated Batch-Variable Selection for ComBat-Based Radiomic Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34618)
*Jose Lozano-Montoya,Gemma Urbanos,Carmen Prieto-de-la-Lastra,Emilio Soria-Olivas,Ana Jimenez-Pastor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ParsiComBat，以数据驱动方式自动选择ComBat批次变量，在去除批次效应与保留特征结构间平衡，并在技术变异可忽略时弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ComBat可协调多中心影像组学特征，但批次变量通常由人工选择，缺乏跨采集参数的统一准则。

Method: 按批次效应排序候选采集参数，过滤冗余变量，对顺序式ComBat校正打分；并在三组实验中评估：模拟数据（能否识别已知批次变量、无批次时是否弃权）、CT（NSCLC-Radiomics）与MRI（MAMA-MIA）队列（对比手动选择厂商、贪心式全参数与AutoCombat）、以及内部与外部临床预测。

Result: 模拟中正例92.9%识别真实批次变量，零对照99.2%弃权；CT中校正重建核使kNN批次效应检验均值由0.276降至0.032，接近六变量贪心校正；MRI中校正层厚与厂商使其由0.680降至0.325，略优于手动选择但不及七变量贪心校正；去除技术变异未提升临床判别，各策略（含原始特征）AUC与ParsiComBat均无显著差异。

Conclusion: ParsiComBat以最少采集参数去除技术批次效应，保留特征结构与临床判别力，所选变量可对照采集协议核验，并在无需校正时弃权，支持透明可复现的多中心影像组学。

Abstract: ComBat harmonizes multicentre radiomic features, but batch variables are usually chosen manually, without a standardized criterion across image acquisition parameters. We propose ParsiComBat, which selects acquisition parameters for ComBat, balancing batch-effect removal against feature-structure preservation and abstaining when technical variation is negligible.
  ParsiComBat ranks candidate acquisition parameters by batch effect, filters redundancy, and scores sequential ComBat corrections. We evaluated it in three experiments: in simulations, testing whether it recovered a known batch variable and abstained without one; in CT (NSCLC-Radiomics) and MRI (MAMA-MIA) cohorts, comparing batch-effect removal against manufacturer (manual), every feasible parameter (greedy) and AutoCombat; and in clinical prediction, internally and externally.
  In simulations, ParsiComBat identified the true batch variable in 92.9% of positive trials and abstained in 99.2% of null controls. In CT, correcting for reconstruction kernel reduced mean k-nearest neighbour batch-effect test from 0.276 to 0.032, almost matching greedy correction of six variables. In MRI, correcting for slice thickness and manufacturer reduced it from 0.680 to 0.325, slightly more than manual selection but less than greedy correction of seven variables. Removing technical variation did not improve clinical discrimination: no strategy, including raw features, differed significantly from ParsiComBat in area under the curve.
  ParsiComBat provides a data-driven framework that removes technical batch effects using minimal acquisition parameters, preserving feature structure and clinical discrimination. It selects variables verifiable against the acquisition protocol and abstains when no correction is needed, supporting transparent, reproducible multicentre radiomics.

</details>


### [108] [DecisionReplay: A Corpus and Offline Simulator for Ensembling Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34627)
*Dean Ariel,Seffi Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发布 DecisionReplay：包含 17 个商用决策模型在 35 个基准上的概率向量、token 数、计费成本与实测时延的公开数据集，并配确定性模拟器，可无额外模型调用地快速比较集成、路由与级联方法。核心发现：每基准选单一最优模型难以击败，模型间错误高度相关，路由与认证提前退出可显著降本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较集成（ensemble）、路由器（router）与级联（cascade）需要每个模型在每个样本上的输出及其成本，而现有面向路由构建的存储集合不保留逐选项概率，导致复现与对比成本高、需重复调用模型。

Method: 构建 DecisionReplay 数据集：17 个商业决策模型（对每个答案选项返回概率的端点）在 35 个基准上的概率向量，附每次调用的 token 计数、计费成本与实测时延，全部公开；并提供确定性模拟器，可重放任意选择、组合或级联策略。

Result: 每个基准上精选的单一模型难以超越：全模型池化比其低 2.7 个准确率点；堆叠（stacking）提升 1.0 点但成本为 3.4 倍。模型同时出错现象显著——两模型同时出错时 72% 选择相同的错误选项。最佳路由器以 64% 成本达到该模型精度；认证式提前退出（certified early exit）在答案不变的前提下将堆叠集成成本降低 43%。

Conclusion: DecisionReplay 使检验新方法能否改变上述结论变得廉价而快速，为集成、路由与级联策略提供了可复现、低成本的标准评测基础。

Abstract: Comparing ensembles, routers and cascades requires the output of every model on every item, and its cost. Stored collections built for routing keep no per-option probabilities. We provide a dataset on which ensemble methods, routers and cascades are simulated quickly, without new model calls. DecisionReplay holds the probability vectors of 17 commercial decision models, endpoints that return a probability for every answer option, on 35 benchmarks, with the token counts, billed cost and measured latency of every call, all released. A deterministic simulator replays any policy that selects, combines or cascades them. One well-chosen model per benchmark is hard to beat: pooling all models loses 2.7 accuracy points to it, and stacking gains 1.0 points at 3.4 times its cost. The models are wrong together: two that are both wrong choose the same wrong option in 72% of cases. The best router matches that model at 64% of its cost, and certified early exit cuts the cost of a stacked ensemble by 43% without changing an answer. DecisionReplay makes it cheap and fast to test whether a new method changes these results.

</details>


### [109] [Predicting acute food crises and discovering actionable drivers with causal machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34629)
*Mélissande Machefer,Michele Ronco,Michele Meroni,Jordi Cerdà-Bautista,Francesco Collivignarelli,Chungmann Kim,Nicolas Rost,Alessia Matanó,Esther Rodrigo-Bonet,Philipp Zimmer,Gustau Camps-Valls,Christina Corbane,Felix Rembold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为开放获取的HFIAS数据集补充2007–2024年、月尺度、次国家级、80国的驱动变量，构建抗缺失数据的机器学习流程，预测3/6/9个月后的急性粮食危机；结果优于无技能基线15–50个百分点，并发现预测重要性与因果效应存在系统错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急性粮食不安全由冲突、极端气候、流离失所与经济冲击复合驱动，但现有开放数据缺乏系统化的驱动变量与可跨分类体系（FEWS NET 与 IPC/CH）比较的、对缺失稳健的预测方法；同时预测建模与因果推断对"可干预驱动因素"的结论是否一致尚不清楚。

Method: 在Harmonized Food Insecurity Dataset上加入驱动变量（月度、次国家、80国、2007–2024）；构建对缺失数据稳健的ML流程，分别在3、6、9个月提前期预测危机发生，同时使用FEWS NET与IPC/CH（首次大规模使用IPC/CH）标签；采用嵌套交叉验证评估，以PR-AUC对比无技能基线；使用TreeSHAP解释特征贡献，并与双重稳健因果估计进行比较。

Result: 流程在PR-AUC上较无技能基线提升15–50个百分点；性能与危机流行率相关，而非与所用分类体系相关；TreeSHAP显示复合、地理特异的预测信号剖面，可归纳为三种反复出现的预测原型；预测重要性与因果效应比对表明：急性冲突事件预测信号最弱却是危机分类的主要因果驱动，而农业气候是唯一预测重要性与因果效应一致的维度。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可扩展、跨分类体系的急性粮食不安全预测基线，并揭示"预测性能"与"因果可干预性"之间的错位——单纯优化预测精度可能导致对冲突等高因果效力驱动因素的忽视，为预警系统设计与干预优先级提供方法论警示。

Abstract: Acute food insecurity is driven by a compound of conflict, adverse climate events, displacement, and economic shocks. We enrich the open-access Harmonized Food Insecurity Dataset with driver variables (monthly, subnational, 80 countries, 2007--2024) and develop missing-data-resilient machine learning pipelines forecasting crisis occurrence at 3, 6, and 9-month lead times using both FEWS NET and, for the first time at scale, IPC/CH classifications. Pipelines, evaluated through nested cross-validation, outperform no-skill baselines by 15 to 50 percentage points in PR-AUC, with performance correlated with crisis prevalence rather than the classification system. TreeSHAP-based explainability reveals compound, geography-specific predictive signal profiles, with three recurrent forecasting archetypes. Comparing predictive importance against doubly robust causal estimates of actionable drivers, we find that acute conflict events, having the weakest predictive signals, emerge as primary causal drivers of crisis classification whilst agroclimatology is the sole dimension in which predictive importance and causal effect align.

</details>


### [110] [Distractor-Robust Reinforcement Learning via Variational Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34641)
*Benjamin Freed,Vivaan Singh,Dean Zadok,Roberto Calandra,Jeff Schneider,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VIBES目标，用对抗项替代像素重建，使潜状态具备奖励与动力学充分性并诱导双模拟关系，在Dreamer中即插即用，在Distracting Control Suite与Realistic ManiSkill上达到SOTA，并可推广至高维向量状态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的MBRL目标会让模型把表征容量浪费在与任务无关的干扰因素上，缺乏数据效率与泛化性；作者希望潜状态只保留任务相关信息，且不依赖图像专用的数据增强。

Method: 提出VIBES（Variational Inference for Bisimulation-based Encoded States）：以对抗性目标替换像素重建，强制潜状态足以预测奖励与下一潜状态；理论上证明该目标度量编码器距奖励/动力学充分性的差距，且在可实现精确充分性时全局最优解诱导历史上的双模拟关系；配合固定容量潜瓶颈抑制无关变异；作为Dreamer模型学习组件的直接替代。

Result: 在Distracting Control Suite上取得SOTA；在Realistic ManiSkill的视觉复杂操作任务上有效；在100连杆swimmer机器人高维向量状态任务上同样适用；UMAP嵌入与最近邻探测显示表征对任务相关结构敏感、对干扰不变。

Conclusion: 以双模拟/充分性为核心的对抗式目标可在不依赖图像增强的前提下学到任务相关、抗干扰的潜表征，兼具理论保证与跨模态（像素与向量状态）适用性。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) promises data efficiency and generalization, but typical reconstruction-based objectives encourage models to waste representational capacity on task-irrelevant distractors. We introduce VIBES (Variational Inference for Bisimulation-based Encoded States), a new objective that replaces pixel reconstruction with an adversarial term, which enforces that latent states suffice to predict both rewards and the next latent state. We show that this objective measures how far the encoder is from reward and dynamics sufficiency, and that whenever exact sufficiency is attainable, its global optima induce bisimulation relations on histories. Combined with a fixed-capacity latent bottleneck, this encourages latent states to retain task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant variation. Our method serves as a drop-in replacement for Dreamer's model-learning component, achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Distracting Control Suite, and extends to visually complex manipulation in Realistic ManiSkill. Unlike prior approaches, it does not rely on image-specific augmentations and applies equally well to high-dimensional vector-state tasks, demonstrated on a 100-link swimmer robot. Finally, latent-space analyses (UMAP embeddings and nearest-neighbor probes) confirm that the learned representations are sensitive to task-relevant structure while invariant to distractors.

</details>


### [111] [Do We Still Need Reinforcement Learning When We Can Read the World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34656)
*Mingchao Zhang,Miduo Cui,Kangze He,Yuanjun Li,Dapeng Li,Ce Zhang,Chuanchen Luo,Xue Yan,Mingzhe Xing,Zhumin Chen,Hangyu Mao,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种新范式：让基于 LLM 的自主编码智能体通过阅读可读的世界模型（环境源代码）直接合成控制策略，并借助在线反馈或离线数据迭代精炼，从而在无需任务特定 RL 训练的情况下达到或超过常规 RL 方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽在序列决策中成效显著，但学习有效策略通常需要大量交互经验。基于模型的 RL 通过世界模型缓解这一依赖，但世界模型一般仅用于支持规划或策略优化。作者希望探索另一条路径：既然环境源码本身即是一种可读的世界模型，能否让 LLM 编码智能体直接基于它推理并合成策略，从而减少试错依赖。

Method: 让自主 LLM 编码智能体检查环境源代码，将环境知识转化为可执行的决策规则以直接合成控制策略；随后利用在线反馈或离线数据进行迭代式策略精炼，全过程不进行任务特定的 RL 训练。并通过消融开展对比：与无法访问源码所生成的策略对比，以及考察在困难条件下的表现。

Result: 在多种决策基准上，合成策略的表现匹配甚至优于常规 RL 方法，且所需经验显著更少；分析还揭示了可解释的决策规则及其在反馈驱动精炼中的演化过程；无源码访问的对照与困难条件实验进一步证明了可读世界模型带来的收益。

Conclusion: 直接策略合成可降低对试错的依赖，对"任务特定 RL 训练是否必要"这一前提构成挑战，表明可读世界模型结合 LLM 编码智能体是一种高效、可解释的决策学习替代范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in sequential decision-making, but learning effective policies often requires substantial interaction experience. Model-based reinforcement learning alleviates this dependence by leveraging world models, which are typically used to support planning or policy optimization. In this paper, we investigate an alternative paradigm in which autonomous LLM-based coding agents directly synthesize control policies by reasoning over readable world models. By examining environment source code, these agents translate environmental knowledge into executable decision rules and iteratively refine their policies using online feedback or offline data, without task-specific RL training. Experiments across diverse decision-making benchmarks show that synthesized policies match or outperform conventional RL methods while requiring substantially less experience. Further analyses reveal interpretable decision rules and how they evolve through feedback-driven refinement. Comparisons with policies generated without source code access and experiments under challenging conditions further demonstrate the benefits of readable world models. These findings suggest that direct policy synthesis can reduce reliance on trial and error, challenging the necessity of task-specific RL training.

</details>


### [112] [Learning to Be Truncated: Preparing LLM Checkpoints for SVD Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34667)
*Chenyue Fang,Danila Mishin,Babak Falsafi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLift 通过仅在单一压缩比下对低秩近似进行微调，使预训练模型可被 SVD 直接压缩到多种压缩比而无需二次微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 SVD 的压缩常需后续微调或权重校正来缓解性能下降，且不同压缩比需重复进行特定预算的优化，成本高。

Method: 以原始模型为教师，在单一压缩比下微调低秩近似；将学到的更新合并回原权重，同时保留训练前向中排除的互补分量；对处理后模型的目标矩阵做 SVD，并在不同截断秩下复用该分解。

Result: 在 LLaMA-7B 上，单个准备好模型在三种压缩比下均取得精度提升；增益可推广到不同模型家族、模型规模和 SVD 压缩算子；梯度与早期训练更新的分析提供了单比例更新可惠及其他比例的局部证据。

Conclusion: LLift 可在无需为每个压缩模型单独微调的情况下，跨部署预算改善 SVD 压缩效果。

Abstract: Compression based on singular value decomposition (SVD) often requires subsequent fine-tuning or weight correction to mitigate performance degradation. Budget-specific optimization must be repeated for different compression ratios. We introduce LLift, which prepares a pretrained model for SVD compression across ratios without further fine-tuning. LLift fine-tunes a low-rank approximation at a single ratio using the original model as a teacher. We merge the learned updates into the original weights while preserving the complementary components excluded from the training forward pass. We apply SVD to the prepared model's target matrices and reuse the decompositions at different truncation ranks. On LLaMA-7B, we demonstrate accuracy gains across three compression ratios using a single prepared model. The gains extend across model families, model sizes, and SVD compression operators. We analyze gradients and an early training update to provide local evidence that updates learned at one ratio can benefit compression at other ratios. These results show that LLift can improve compression across deployment budgets without fine-tuning each compressed model separately.

</details>


### [113] [Provably Efficient Concave Utility Reinforcement Learning with Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34678)
*Max Qiushi Lin,Ahmed Magd,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究双线性MDP下的凹效用强化学习（CURL），提出基于模型的乐观凹效用策略优化框架OCUPO，on-policy版本达到Õ(ε^{-4})样本复杂度，off-policy版本借助反向特征回归子程序复用历史轨迹，达到Õ(ε^{-2})样本复杂度，并可扩展到线性MDP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CURL统一了标准RL、模仿学习与最大熵探索等问题，但通常需估计高维状态-动作占用度量。现有方法要么回避探索问题，要么局限于表格情形，要么缺乏计算高效的策略优化；因此需要一种可高效估计占用度量并具备理论保证的方法。

Method: 在有限时域分幕式双线性MDP中，利用转移模型的低维特征表示，通过低维特征期望高效估计占用度量，构建基于模型的乐观凹效用策略优化框架OCUPO。on-policy实例每次迭代用独立轨迹估计凹效用策略梯度；off-policy实例引入反向特征回归子程序，复用历史策略采集的轨迹来估计当前策略的占用度量，假设策略类满足技术条件（如具有充分表达特征的对数线性策略）；框架进一步扩展至线性MDP。

Result: on-policy OCUPO获得Õ(ε^{-4})的样本复杂度；off-policy OCUPO在可控投影误差内达到Õ(ε^{-2})的样本复杂度，且在线性MDP下保持相同复杂度；对ε的依赖与线性函数逼近下的标准RL相匹配。

Conclusion: OCUPO为CURL提供了计算高效且具样本复杂度保证的策略优化方法，显著提升样本效率，且不依赖先前工作所需的积分预言机。

Abstract: Concave utility reinforcement learning (CURL) unifies a diverse range of problems, including standard reinforcement learning (RL), imitation learning, and maximum entropy exploration. Unlike standard RL, CURL generally requires estimating high-dimensional state-action occupancy measures. Consequently, existing methods for CURL either sidestep the exploration problem, are restricted to the tabular setting, or lack computationally efficient policy optimization. To address this, we study CURL in finite-horizon episodic bilinear MDPs, where the transition model admits a low-dimensional representation in known feature spaces. We use the bilinear structure to efficiently estimate occupancy measures via low-dimensional feature expectations, and propose a model-based optimistic concave utility policy optimization (OCUPO) framework. We first instantiate OCUPO in the on-policy setting, where each iteration uses independent trajectories to estimate the concave utility policy gradient, and prove an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ sample complexity. To improve sample efficiency, we then instantiate OCUPO in the off-policy setting. We propose a novel backward feature regression subroutine that reuses trajectories collected by past policies to estimate the occupancy measure of the current policy. Under suitable technical assumptions on the policy class (satisfied, e.g., by log-linear policies with sufficiently expressive features), we prove that the resulting algorithm achieves an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity, up to a controllable projection error. We further extend our framework to linear MDPs and show that the resulting off-policy algorithm retains the same sample complexity. In these settings, the dependence on $ε$ matches that for standard RL with linear function approximation. Moreover, our algorithms do not rely on the integral oracle required by prior work.

</details>


### [114] [Evacuation Traffic Prediction for Dynamic Transportation Networks over Multiple Hurricanes: A Generalized Graph Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34687)
*Md Nafees Fuad Rafi,Samiul Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于动态图学习的GCN-LSTM框架，利用12次历史飓风的检测器与疏散数据预测飓风疏散交通，并验证其跨飓风泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飓风疏散期间高交通需求导致主干道严重拥堵，实时预测可提升疏散管理。但疏散交通高度动态且不确定：每次飓风特征与疏散情境各异，形成不同时空模式；且基于检测器的路网结构在不同飓风间亦不同，导致单一飓风训练的模型难以泛化。

Method: 构建基于12次历史飓风检测器与疏散数据的通用动态图学习框架；利用路网的动态特性，提出图卷积长短期记忆网络（GCN-LSTM），实现最长6小时提前量的疏散交通预测；并引入模块化训练方法，通过顺序加入多次飓风数据评估性能提升；辅以消融实验比较动态与静态GCN-LSTM。

Result: 在未见过的飓风（Milton, 2024）上表现强泛化：1小时预测RMSE=280.6、R²=0.96；6小时整体预测RMSE=459.8、R²=0.89。模型在6小时预测时域上优于先进基线模型；消融实验显示动态GCN-LSTM较静态版本RMSE改善8.4%。

Conclusion: 动态图学习框架可有效实现跨飓风的疏散交通预测，交通管理部门可据此提前掌握疏散期间未来交通状况并采取主动措施减少延误。

Abstract: High traffic demand during hurricane evacuations causes severe congestion on major highways, leading to delays and safety risks for evacuees. Predicting evacuation traffic in real-time can significantly enhance evacuation management efforts. However, evacuation traffic is highly dynamic and uncertain due to evolving hurricane characteristics and diverse population response. Each hurricane has unique characteristics and evacuation contexts, resulting in different spatial-temporal traffic patterns. Moreover, the structure of detector-based transportation networks varies across multiple hurricanes. Hence, a model trained on the data from one hurricane may not generalize well for others. This study proposes a generalized dynamic graph learning framework using traffic detectors and evacuation data from 12 historical hurricanes. Leveraging the dynamic nature of transportation network, we develop a Graph Convolutional Long Short-Term Memory (GCN-LSTM) model that predicts evacuation traffic up to 6 hours in advance. For an unseen hurricane (Milton, 2024), the model shows strong generalizability, achieving an RMSE of 280.6 and R2 of 0.96 for 1-hour prediction, and an RMSE of 459.8 and R2 of 0.89 for overall 6-hours prediction. We also introduce a modular training approach, where we evaluate performance improvements by sequentially incorporating data from multiple hurricanes. The proposed model outperforms state-of-the-art baseline models for overall 6-hour prediction horizons. Ablation experiments show an 8.4% improvement in RMSE for the proposed dynamic GCN-LSTM model compared to its static counterpart. Traffic management officials can use the developed model to understand future traffic conditions during hurricane evacuation in advance and take proactive measures to reduce delays during evacuation.

</details>


### [115] [Effect of class proportion shifts on classifier curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34702)
*Maxim Bolshim,Alexander Kugaevskikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过冻结特征仅更新输出层、并在各分支内测量固定图像上的损失Hessian迹，论文试图将"类比例变化引起的损失重加权"与"模型自身变化"分离；但受分配训练图像混淆限制，作者转而构建基于初始类别梯度的预测方法，并在前瞻性实验中将其误差降至0.022个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改变类混合比例可在不改动分类器的情况下改变平均损失，这种"重加权效应"与模型参数或表征本身的变化难以区分。作者希望明确界定并量化重加权的影响，而不是把它与训练引起的模型变化混为一谈。

Method: 在CIFAR-10十类分类上设置两个目标类的独立分支，冻结特征、仅对输出层做60次更新；在各类固定图像上计算损失关于输出层参数的Hessian迹，跨十次新初始化比较相对初始迹的变化。另基于初始类别梯度构建预测式，先由第一轮实验结果提出、再不加修改地进行前瞻性验证，并与零变化基线及等权梯度基线对比。

Result: 目标类占比由10%升至19%的分支，迹平均变化-0.152%；降至1%的分支为+0.071%（均相对于各分支初始迹）。作者明确指出该结果无法将类占比效应与分配训练图像的效应分离。对10%→19%的直接提升情形，预测的平均绝对误差为0.022个百分点（初始迹尺度），零变化基线为0.152，等权梯度基线为0.104。

Conclusion: 类别比例本身携带可预测的信息：使用实际分配的类比例可改进对Hessian迹变化量的预测。但实验设计存在混淆，不能把观测到的迹变化单纯归因于类占比；梯度式预测在干净的直接提升场景下表现最佳，其外推性与机制解释仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Changing the class mix can alter mean loss without changing the classifier. To distinguish this reweighting from changes in the model itself, we studied ten-class CIFAR-10 classification in separate branches for two target classes, measuring the trace of the loss Hessian with respect to the output-layer parameters on fixed images of each target class. Across ten new initialisations, after 60 updates to the output layer with frozen features, the trace changed by -0.152% on average in branches where the target class share rose from 10% to 19%, and by +0.071% in branches where it fell to 1%, relative to each branch's initial trace. These changes do not isolate the effect of class share from that of the assigned training images. We developed a forecast based on initial class gradients after examining the first experiment's results and then tested it prospectively without modification. For direct increases from 10% to 19%, rather than increases after a deficit, its mean absolute error was 0.022 percentage points on the initial-trace scale, compared with 0.152 for a zero-change forecast and 0.104 for equally weighted gradients. Using the assigned class proportions improved the forecast of how much the trace would change.

</details>


### [116] [Maco-RL: Scaling Agent Capabilities with Mixed-Task Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34703)
*Jiacheng Zhang,Qixing Zhou,Haiyang Wang,Qipeng Gu,Shuzhe Wu,Feiyang Pan,Lue Fan,Dandan Tu,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Maco-RL通过配额异步调度与Tri-Buffer采样扩展混合任务强化学习，实现稳定训练、高效算力分配与持续领域扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合任务强化学习（MixRL）可直接训练统一策略，但规模化面临三大挑战：稳定高效的混合任务训练、rollout算力的有效分配、以及持续领域扩展的支持。

Method: 提出Maco-RL：(1) 基于配额的调度器，实现多任务域的异步rollout，通过平衡梯度贡献与缓解off-policy效应稳定训练；(2) 正交维度上引入Tri-Buffer Sampling (TBS)，在掌握程度不同的样本间分配算力，促进前沿探索同时保留已获能力。

Result: 在五个基准上，Maco-RL将Qwen3.5-27B总体得分从43.6提升至54.2，经持续扩展进一步达60.1，追平Qwen3.8-27B的前沿并将其推高5.7；同时以更少策略更新算力优于单任务RL。

Conclusion: MixRL可作为实用且算力高效的后训练范式，具备增强通用智能体能力的显著潜力。

Abstract: Mixed-task reinforcement learning (MixRL) directly trains a unified policy across domains to continually expand agent capabilities without training separate specialists or requiring an extra distillation stage. Despite its appeal, scaling MixRL poses significant challenges in achieving stable and efficient mixed-task learning, effectively allocating rollout compute, and supporting continual domain expansion. To address these challenges, we propose Maco-RL, an approach that effectively scales MixRL. Specifically, Maco-RL employs a quota-based scheduler for asynchronous rollouts across multiple task domains, thereby stabilizing training by balancing gradient contributions and mitigating off-policy effects. Along an orthogonal axis, Maco-RL further introduces Tri-Buffer Sampling (TBS) to allocate compute among samples with varying levels of mastery, promoting sustained frontier exploration while preserving acquired capabilities. Experiments on five benchmarks show Maco-RL raises Qwen3.5-27B's overall score from 43.6 to 54.2 and then to 60.1 with continual expansion, reaching the frontier held by Qwen3.8-27B, which is further pushed outward by +5.7. Maco-RL also outperforms single-task RL with less compute for policy updates. These results support MixRL as a practical and compute-efficient post-training approach with substantial potential to strengthen general agent capabilities.

</details>


### [117] [When the Proxy Is Wrong: Mismatch-Corrected Bandits for Adaptive Data Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34707)
*Amine Saadi,Elissa Mhanna,Raphaël Féraud,Gwénolé Lecorvé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对代理模型与目标模型对数据排序不一致导致的数据分配次优问题，将分配决策建模为多保真度老虎机，提出带不匹配校正的老虎机方法，并给出悔界分析及在CivilComments-WILDS上的闭环验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分配常用小代理模型为更大目标模型选数据，但代理与目标的排序可能不同，导致训练次优。既有方法未显式刻画并校正这种代理—目标不匹配。

Method: 将问题建模为多保真度老虎机，每个臂为一次数据干预；每轮观测代理奖励，目标奖励仅在同一更新的稀有审计中揭示。利用配对观测识别与臂相关的不匹配（不假设共享偏移或排序保持），用配对残差校正代理、仅审计不确定决策，并将审计预算在训练中分摊。

Result: 理论上证明悔分解及平稳性下的下界：纯代理分配可致线性悔，且存在最小配对审计次数要求；突变情形下过早用完审计预算也会致线性悔；滑动窗口策略将审计分散到整个训练期可达 O(√(Γ_T T log T)) 悔。实验在CivilComments-WILDS上闭环验证，BERT-Small引导BERT-Large训练，不匹配校正在相同目标预算下提升最差组准确率；审计分散使相同审计次数下最差组准确率提升6.3–10.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模并校正代理—目标不匹配可显著改善数据分配的质量与鲁棒性，且审计预算的时序分配（分散而非集中）对非平稳训练过程至关重要。

Abstract: Data allocation methods often use a small proxy model to select training data for a larger target model. However, this proxy can rank data differently from the target, leading to suboptimal training performance. To address this problem, we model the mismatch between the proxy and target models and incorporate it into the allocation decision. To do so, the problem is cast as a multi-fidelity bandit, where each arm is a data intervention. The proxy reward is observed every round, whereas the target reward is revealed only on rare audits of the same update. These paired observations identify arm-wise mismatches without assuming a shared offset or preservation of rankings. Mismatch-corrected bandits correct the proxy with paired residuals, audit only uncertain decisions, and pace the audit budget over training. From a theoretical viewpoint, we prove a regret decomposition and lower bounds under stationarity. These bounds show that proxy-only allocation can incur linear regret and that a minimum number of paired audits is necessary. Under abrupt changes, using the audit budget early can also incur linear regret. A sliding-window policy that spreads audits over training achieves regret of order $\sqrt{Γ_T T\log T}$ where $Γ_T$ denotes the number of change points. Experimentally, we test the approach in a closed-loop proof of concept on CivilComments-WILDS, where BERT-Small guides the training of BERT-Large. Mismatch correction improves worst-group accuracy over a target-only policy with the same target-model budget. Spreading audits over training improves worst-group accuracy by 6.3 to 10.5 percentage points at the same audit count.

</details>


### [118] [Spec2Weights: Certifying Networks that a Language Model Writes from a Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34711)
*Soumik Bhattacharjee,Musa Tur Farazi,Md Nasir Uddin,Tishna Sabrina,Chowdhury Mohammad Rakin Haider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型直接写出小网络权重后，能否在真实float32算术中证明其精确性，并提出一套可机械化验证的框架，同时给出两条"从示例学习"的理论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可零数据、零梯度地按自然语言规格生成 MLP/CNN/RNN/GNN 权重，但"权重写出来了"不等于"网络真的正确"：浮点舍入、求和顺序、权重非短二进制分数都会破坏精确性。现有工作缺少在目标硬件算术下对生成网络的可证明保证，也缺少对"哪些规格本质上学不会"的边界刻画。

Method: 1) float-safety 引理：使对 float32 的穷举枚举成为对任意求和顺序都成立的证明；2) 分层误差界：覆盖权重非短二进制分数的网络；3) 组合多种证明技术：动态规划、模板检查、SMT 求解、广度优先搜索、以及对权重共享递推的归纳；4) 用新会话（Claude Opus 5.5）独立撰写 30 条新规格作外部测试，并在开源 27B 模型上复现；5) 理论上分析两类不可学习性：训练中从未激活的输入坐标、以及 Luhn 型校验和的梯度方差。

Result: 15 个书面网络中 12 个被证明在其声明域的全部输入上精确（其中一个经一次常数修正），覆盖至多 1e16 个输入或尺寸有界的所有图/网格，单个证明 CPU 时间约一分钟以内。证书还能刻画精确性边界：最短路网络当且仅当距离不超过 4,096 时精确，并在预测的跳数处失败。30 条新规格中，25 条首版即被证明对任意求和顺序精确；5 条税率表在固定 float32 运行中每整数美元误差 ≤ 0.11 美元。开源 27B 模型 30 题通过 23 题，增加思考与代码执行后通过 25 题。理论上证明：训练中从未激活的输入坐标无法通过更多域内数据学到；Luhn 型校验和在其族上的目标相关梯度方差指数级小。调优梯度下降在同架构上与两结论一致，覆盖五类失败中的两类。

Conclusion: "写出权重"可被提升为"可证明精确"：在真实 float32 语义下，借助浮点安全引理与分层误差界、配以 DP/SMT/BFS/归纳的组合验证，能对语言模型生成的小网络给出涵盖全输入域的机器可检验证书，并精确定位失效阈值。方法在独立新会话与开源模型上部分可迁移，说明该范式有一般性。同时，从示例学习存在不可逾越的极限（沉默坐标、校验和的指数小梯度方差），构成失败分类中的两类。整体呈现"神经模型生成 + 形式化证书"的可行路线，但需注意验证成本、失败类别的完备性仍开放。

Abstract: A language model can write the weights of a small MLP, CNN, RNN or GNN from a plain-language specification, with no data and no gradient step. We ask when such a network can be proved exact in the float32 arithmetic it actually runs in. A float-safety lemma makes exhaustive float32 enumeration a proof for any summation order, and a layered error bound covers networks whose weights are not short binary fractions. With dynamic programming, template checks, an SMT proof, breadth-first search and induction for weight-tied recurrences, this certifies 12 of 15 written networks (one after a single constant fix) on every input of a stated domain (up to $10^{16}$ inputs, or every graph or grid up to a size bound), in a minute of CPU or less each. The certificates also say where a network stops being exact: the shortest-path network is exact iff the distance is at most 4,096 and fails at the predicted hops. Thirty new specifications were each written by a fresh session of one model family (Claude Opus 5.5). Twenty-five first submissions are certified exact for any summation order; the five tax schedules stay within \$0.11 of the true tax at every whole dollar in one fixed float32 run. An open 27B model passes 23 of the 30, and 25 with more thinking and code execution, so the method transfers in part. We also prove two limits of learning from examples: input coordinates never active in training cannot be learned from more in-range data, and Luhn-type checksums have exponentially small target-dependent gradient variance across their family. Tuned gradient descent on the same architectures agrees with both, which cover two of our five failure classes.

</details>


### [119] [Privileged On-Policy Distillation for Calibrated Alzheimer's Diagnosis With Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34721)
*Vo Minh Khoi Nguyen,Dat Tien Chung,Quang Minh Khoa Le,Duc Khang Nguyen,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Hoang Thien Nguyen,Minh Huu Nhat Le,Phat K. Huynh,Cuong Tuan Nguyen,Thanh Huy Nguyen,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Graded Rollout Imputation with Privileged teachers（GRIP）：用 on-policy 蒸馏让模型自行逐步填补缺失模态，由仅在训练期可用的“评分教师”（已知诊断的补全教师 + 可见全部真实模态的诊断教师）对每一步补全打分并提供软目标，从而在单次 rollout 中完成诊断并给出反映缺失不确定性的置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病诊断依赖临床、生物标本与影像等多种互补证据，但很少有患者做全所有检查。缺失模态存在大量合理取值，而现有方法要么直接丢弃缺失模态，要么只用单一估计值训练：分类器从未见过该估计的其他可能取值，且该估计本身也从未以诊断结果为标准被检验。

Method: 采用 on-policy distillation 框架：模型自身逐模态采样多个填补（graded rollout），填补过程由训练期才可用的教师评分——一个知晓诊断的 imputation teacher 对每一步填补进行评分，将结局信息引入填补（符合统计学实践）；一个可见全部真实模态的 diagnosis teacher 提供软目标。训练于多样的填补轨迹上，使模型学到的置信度反映缺失检查所留下的不确定性，并在推理时仅需单次 rollout 即完成诊断。

Result: 在 ADNI 队列上优于 SOTA 基线；在两个外部队列上判别力保持有竞争力，且即使所有方法均重新校准后，其校准误差在两个队列上均与最低者持平；将该教师机制加持到三个基线之上，可分别提升其性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为任意检查组合的患者提供单一且校准良好的诊断模型，兼顾缺失模态插补的合理性与不确定性量化的可靠性。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is diagnosed from clinical, biospecimen and imaging evidence, but few patients undergo every test. Because these modalities are complementary, a missing one has many plausible values. Most existing methods leave it out or train on one estimate, so their classifier never sees its other values, and the estimate is never checked against the diagnosis. We address both gaps with on-policy distillation: the model fills in the missing modalities itself, and teachers with information available only in training grade these fills. Our method, Graded Rollout Imputation with Privileged teachers, draws several fills, one modality at a time. An imputation teacher that knows the diagnosis grades each step, bringing the outcome into the imputation as statistical practice recommends. A diagnosis teacher that sees all real modalities supplies a soft target. Trained on fills that vary, the model learns a confidence that reflects what the missing tests leave open, and it diagnoses from a single rollout. On the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On two external cohorts, it remains competitive in discrimination and, even after every method is recalibrated, its calibration error is level with the lowest on both. Added to three of these baselines, the teachers improve each of them. Our method offers a single, well-calibrated model for whatever combination of tests a patient has received.

</details>


### [120] [Robust Recourse under Causal Graph Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34723)
*Muyan Anna Li,Juliette Sinnott,Mohammad Kohandel,Amir-Hossein Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究因果算法追索（causal algorithmic recourse）在图不确定性下的鲁棒性问题，提出最坏情况追索与图加权追索两种方法，在全部或指定比例的候选图中寻找低成本可行动作，并揭示覆盖度与有效性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有追索方法通常假设存在唯一因果图，仅考虑因果机制的不确定性；但实际上多张图可能同样拟合数据却对同一动作给出不同预测效果，因此需要显式建模图结构歧义，以给出跨图仍有效的推荐动作。

Method: 假设一组合理候选图；对每张图拟合高斯过程结构方程，采用后验均值—标准差准则判定动作成功；用基于梯度的搜索求解最小代价动作，并在新后验样本上检验动作有效性。提出 Worst-Case Recourse（要求在全部候选图下成功）与 Graph-Weighted Recourse（要求在给定图权重比例下成功）两种形式。

Result: 在合成与半合成基准上，放松鲁棒性要求可提高有效推荐数量并降低动作代价；在20节点高斯过程基准中，放松使获得追索的事实实例（覆盖度）由341/1000升至436/1000，而真实成功数（生成模型下翻转决策）由251降至184，体现覆盖度—有效性权衡。

Conclusion: 显式考虑图结构歧义有助于决策者在系统因果机制未完全已知时推荐仍具实效的动作；鲁棒性与覆盖度、有效性之间存在内在权衡，需按应用需求选择最坏情况或加权追索设定。

Abstract: Causal algorithmic recourse recommends actions to reverse unfavorable decisions by modeling how interventions change an individual's features. Existing methods typically assume one causal graph that describes the system and account for uncertainty in the causal mechanisms. However, there usually exist several graphs that fit the same data but can predict different effects for the same action. Certain actions may achieve recourse across multiple graphs, but as the set of potential graphs grows, a single best action may become infeasible.
  We propose Worst-Case Recourse and Graph-Weighted Recourse to find a low-cost action that succeeds across all candidate graphs or a specified fraction of graph weight, respectively. Assuming a set of plausible graphs, for each graph we fit Gaussian process structural equations and assess success using the posterior mean--standard-deviation criterion. We use gradient-based search to determine minimal cost actions and check the validity of the actions on fresh posterior samples. In synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks, relaxing robustness, the requirement that an action succeed under every candidate graph, can increase the number of valid recommendations for recourse and lower costs of actions for both methods. In the 20-node GP benchmark, relaxation increases the number of factual instances offered recourse (coverage) from 341 to 436 out of 1,000, while the number of true successes, actions that flip the decision under the generating model, falls from 251 to 184, illustrating the coverage--validity trade-off. Accounting for graph ambiguity can help decision-makers recommend actions that remain effective even when the causal mechanisms of the system are not fully known.

</details>


### [121] [Reading Fine-Tuning Diffs Through Lenses: Model Diffing for Backdoor Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34758)
*Maximilian Petrowitsch,Ben Connors,Chris Kapulkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于token readout的统一评分框架，用于评估fine-tuning场景下后门检测的weight/activation/output三类model diffing方法，并引入J-lens与J++-lens改进既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门检测的model diffing方法（weight、activation、output三类）缺乏统一评测标准，且各方法的鲁棒性边界不清，需要可比的评分体系并探究其在对抗条件下的失效模式。

Method: 构建基于token readout的通用评分系统；将J-lens及其改进版J++-lens引入weight-based与activation-based方法（如ΔW经base model的J++-lens解码得到ΔW J++）；在backdoored model与base model对比的12种设定下进行实验，并对比ADL、Diff Mining等output/activation方法。

Result: J++-lens在所有设定下均提升weight-based方法，J-lens改善其单层内排名；最强单一方法ΔW J++在12个设定中的11个将命名后门的token排进前十。在微调时对后门词在clean输入上的概率施加惩罚，可使ADL、Diff Mining等output/activation方法失效而weight-based方法不受影响；反之，置换隐藏单元或仅训练读取residual stream的层可隐藏后门使weight-based方法失效，但ADL与Diff Mining仍有效。

Conclusion: 没有任何单一方法能同时抵御两类攻击；weight-based与output/activation-based方法在鲁棒性上呈互补关系，J++-lens能显著增强weight-based方法的检测能力。

Abstract: We introduce a common scoring system based on token readouts to evaluate weight, activation and output-based model diffing methods for backdoor detection in fine-tuning processes. We also improve existing weight and activation based methods by incorporating the $J$-lens and its refinement, the $J{+}{+}$-lens. We test our setup in experiments where we compare a backdoored model with the base model from which it was fine-tuned. Our results show that incorporating the $J{+}{+}$-lens improves the weight-based method in all our settings, while the $J$-lens improves its ranks within single layers. The strongest single method is $ΔW J{+}{+}$, the weight difference decoded through the $J{+}{+}$-lens of the base model, which ranks a token naming the backdoor among its first ten tokens in 11 of our 12 settings. Moreover, we show that output and activation based methods such as ADL and Diff Mining can easily break if the backdoored model is penalised during fine-tuning for giving the backdoor's words probability on clean inputs, whereas the weight-based methods are not affected. Conversely, permuting the hidden units or training only the layers that read the residual stream hides the backdoor from the weight-based methods but not from ADL and Diff Mining. No single method resists both attacks.

</details>


### [122] [Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34726)
*Ioannis Kalogeropoulos,Giorgos Bouritsas,Yannis Panagakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层图元网络H-GMNs，利用架构层次结构进行池化/反池化粗化与细化，并扩展到Transformer，成本近常数、样本效率高，可处理至86M参数模型库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间学习模型（如基于消息传递的GMNs）仅在最细粒度（逐神经元、逐权重）处理输入网络，代价随参数量线性增长，难以扩展到大型现代架构；而NN本身具有层次结构，且Lipschitz上界、泛化度量等性质是层/块级量的复合，应引入该归纳偏置。

Method: H-GMNs利用输入架构的层次结构，通过保持置换对称性的pooling与un-pooling层对图进行粗化与细化；并将H-GMNs扩展至Transformer，设计保持置换对称性且支持多级池化的图构造方式。

Result: 在新建的ResNet-18、ViT及不同规模ViT模型库（至多86M参数）上评测，任务覆盖模型评估（Lipschitz界代理、泛化与鲁棒性预测）、测试（后门检测）和编辑（剪枝）。相较非等变方法与平坦GMNs，H-GMNs样本效率显著更高，代价随元网络深度增长近乎恒定，可在平坦GMNs内存耗尽时进行更深、更具表达力的处理，且精度不降、部分情形明显提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模架构层次结构是权重空间学习可扩展且高效的关键归纳偏置，H-GMNs为大型现代网络（ResNet、ViT）的元学习任务提供了实用且高样本效率的方案。

Abstract: The emerging field of weight space learning has demonstrated the potential to enable metanetworks, i.e. models that process neural network (NN) data, to support several steps of the machine learning lifecycle, from model optimisation to evaluation and adaptation. However, this potential has yet to materialise beyond small-scale MLPs or CNNs: most current models, including message-passing based graph metanetworks (GMNs), operate exclusively at the finest level of granularity, processing every neuron and weight of the input model. Their cost therefore scales with the number of parameters. Yet NNs are inherently organised hierarchically, with neurons forming layers and layers forming blocks and modules. Additionally, several of their properties, such as Lipschitz upper bounds and generalisation measures, are compositions of layer- and block-level quantities. To incorporate this inductive bias, we introduce Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks (H-GMNs), which leverage the input architecture's hierarchy to coarsen and refine the graph using permutation-symmetry-preserving pooling and un-pooling layers. Further, we extend H-GMNs to Transformers by introducing a graph construction that preserves permutation symmetries and enables multi-level pooling. Our evaluation includes new ResNet-18, ViT and varying ViT model zoos of up to 86M parameters, on tasks spanning model evaluation (an analytic Lipschitz bound proxy, generalisation and robustness prediction), testing (backdoor detection) and editing (pruning). Experimental results show that, compared to non-equivariant alternatives and flat GMNs, H-GMNs are markedly more sample-efficient, and their cost remains nearly constant as metanetwork depth grows, allowing deeper and more expressive processing where flat GMNs run out of memory, without compromising accuracy, which in certain cases considerably improves.

</details>


### [123] [One Model, Many Modes: Evolving System Prompt Ensembles for Creative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34760)
*Daiwei Chen,Anda He,Binwei Yao,Junjie Hu,Ramya Korlakai Vinayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架SEPO，通过系统提示词集成优化（提示词进化+贪心max-min选择）在质量约束下获取行为各异的系统提示词，推理时每条回复均匀采样一个提示词，从而同时提升LLM创意生成的质量、多样性与新颖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐后的LLM存在模式坍缩，输出分布集中于少数高奖励回复，损害创意生成所需的多样性；而现有测试时方法偏重多样性，忽视单条回复是否同时高质量且新颖，与用户实际"求一条精彩原创回复"的需求不匹配。

Method: 将创意生成形式化为多目标分布优化问题；提出SEPO：先用提示词进化与贪心max-min选择，在质量约束下挑出行为上彼此差异最大的系统提示词集合，构成集成；推理时对每条回复均匀随机采样一个提示词。

Result: 在4个质量要求递增的创意生成任务、3个开源权重模型共12种设置中，SEPO在质量、多样性、新颖性调和平均上8次排名第一（无基线超过2次）；平均将LLM评判多样性从1.92提升至3.43、新颖性从1.00提升至3.34（1-5分），质量由3.72降至3.30；扩大优化集成可增大提示词条件化响应分布间的分离度。

Conclusion: 系统提示词集成优化是提升LLM推理时创意的有效途径；但质量存在一定折损，需在质量-多样性权衡上进一步改进。

Abstract: Preference-aligned LLMs can exhibit mode collapse, concentrating their output distributions around a narrow set of high-reward responses. This limits creative generation, which requires quality, novelty, and diversity. Existing test-time methods often emphasize diversity, giving less attention to whether individual responses are both high-quality and novel. This distinction matters in practical use, where users may seek a single compelling, original response. We formalize creative generation as a multi-objective distributional optimization problem and introduce SEPO, a training-free framework for System Prompt Ensemble Optimization. The framework combines prompt evolution with greedy max-min selection to identify behaviorally distinct system prompts under a quality constraint, then uniformly samples one prompt per response at inference time. Across four creative generation tasks with increasing quality requirements and three open-weight models, SEPO ranks first in 8 of 12 settings on the harmonic mean of quality, diversity, and novelty, while no baseline leads in more than two. On average, it raises LLM-judged diversity from 1.92 to 3.43 and novelty from 1.00 to 3.34 over base sampling (1-5 scale), with quality decreasing from 3.72 to 3.30. We further show that expanding the optimized ensemble increases separation among prompt-conditioned response distributions. These results support system prompt ensemble optimization as a promising approach to improving LLM creativity at inference time.

</details>


### [124] [From NP-Hardness to Exact Tractability Through Sample Abundance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34734)
*Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究带噪奇偶拟合（噪声多项式，最坏情况NP难）问题，指出在不改变目标函数与可行集的前提下，"额外观测"可使精确优化变为可处理的。对于固定交互阶k≥3、每块n个符号、对数平均度与已知信道偏置，额外读取 O(n/(log n)^{1/(k-2)}) 次同一源即可高概率实现精确优化，最坏情况工作量 O(n log² n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带噪奇偶拟合在最坏情况下是NP难的，直接精确优化不可行。作者希望探究：是否可以在不修改目标函数或可行集的情况下，仅通过增加对同一数据源的观测（额外采样），使精确优化从不可处理变为可处理，并刻画其所需代价与复杂度。

Method: 在均匀采样的秩一符号张量条目、独立公平因子符号与独立误差的模型下分析带噪奇偶拟合。设定固定交互阶 k≥3、每块 n 个符号、对数平均度、已知信道偏置。通过引入额外读取（extra readings）并结合深度 O(log log n) 的局部估计器（带独立读取标记）进行分析；利用"带符号场定律"（signed-field law）刻画相对生成源的增益、并列最优数目及后续重加权所需维度。

Result: 额外读取 O(n/(log n)^{1/(k-2)}) 次同一源即可高概率实现精确优化，最坏情况工作量 O(n log² n)；该首项系数对弱恢复（weak recovery）是紧的，由深度 O(log log n) 的局部估计器达成。一个共同的带符号场定律决定了相对生成源的增益、并列最优的数目以及后续重加权所需的维度；剩余坐标的几何性质高概率决定了高分解（high-scoring assignments）的局部验证的精确半径。

Conclusion: 额外观测能在不改变优化问题本身（目标与可行集不变）的情况下，使最坏情况NP难的带噪奇偶拟合实现可处理的精确优化，并给出额外读取量、时间复杂度及弱恢复紧性的精确刻画，同时揭示了带符号场定律与局部验证半径的几何结构。

Abstract: Additional observations can make exact optimization tractable without changing the objective or feasible set. We establish this phenomenon for noisy parity fitting, a worst-case NP-hard problem, with uniformly sampled rank-one sign tensor entries, independent fair factor signs and independent errors. For fixed interaction order $k\ge3$, $n$ signs per block, logarithmic mean degree and known channel biases, $O(n/(\log n)^{1/(k-2)})$ further readings of the same source suffice with high probability for exact optimization in $O(n\log^2 n)$ worst-case work. The leading coefficient is sharp for weak recovery by depth-$O(\log\log n)$ local estimators with independent reading marks. A common signed-field law determines the gain over the generating source, the number of tied optima and the dimension needed for later reweightings. The geometry of the remaining coordinates determines the sharp radius of local verification for high-scoring assignments with high probability.

</details>


### [125] [When Every Rollout Fails: Splicing Learner-Conditioned Options into Long-Horizon Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34777)
*Haowen Hou,Yicheng Liu,Zhen Huang,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPLICE 在 long-horizon agent 任务中对全失败组在线构造局部训练目标，用拼接点附近的选项替代完整轨迹，在 SWE 与 Deep Research 任务上稳定超越 GRPO 与全轨迹引导基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于组的可验证奖励强化学习（如 GRPO）在长时程 agent 任务上常出现全失败组：组内所有采样轨迹奖励为零，组内相对优势消失，无法获得基于奖励的策略梯度。常见补救是用更强模型提供完整解轨迹，但代价高且引入长程 off-policy 历史。

Method: 提出 SPLICE：对每个全失败组，由 proposal 模型在当前策略轨迹上选取拼接点，并从该点生成若干有界选项；用重复的当前策略续写估计各选项价值，将正价值估计转为回报加权的局部目标，再在该选项上拟合当前策略。训练目标在线构造，无需完整专家轨迹。

Result: 在两个策略族、两类长时程任务（SWE-bench Verified、BrowseComp-Plus）四种设置下均优于 GRPO 与全轨迹引导基线，相对 GRPO 提升 2.8–9.8 个百分点；在初始失败的 SWE 任务上毕业率提升 10.8 个百分点；同时降低 proposal 模型计算开销。

Conclusion: 局部化的在线目标构造可有效缓解组相对 RL 的全失败组问题，兼顾性能提升与计算成本下降，为长时程 agent 的强化学习提供可扩展替代方案。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often yields all-fail groups on long-horizon agent tasks: every sampled trajectory receives zero reward, leaving group-relative methods without a reward-based policy gradient. A common remedy supplies complete solutions from a stronger model, but full trajectories are expensive and expose the learner to long off-policy histories. We introduce SPLICE, which constructs local training targets from failed current-policy trajectories online. For each all-fail group, a proposal model selects a splice point in a current-policy trajectory and generates several bounded options from that point. We estimate each option's value using repeated current-policy continuations, convert positive estimates into a return-weighted local target, and fit the current policy on the options. We evaluate SPLICE across two policy families on two long-horizon agent tasks, Software Engineering with SWE-bench Verified and Deep Research with BrowseComp-Plus. SPLICE outperforms GRPO and full-trajectory guidance baselines in all four settings, improving over GRPO by 2.8--9.8 percentage points. On initially failed SWE tasks, it raises GRPO's graduation rate by 10.8 percentage points. Further experiments show that SPLICE improves performance while reducing costly proposal-model computation.

</details>


### [126] [BRIAR: Martingale Belief Smoothing on Bifurcation-Guided Reasoning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34746)
*Tianyi Yang,Haoran Zhang,Yifan Chen,Zixuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRIAR 面向 RLVR 长链推理，基于"前缀通过概率构成有界 Doob 鞅、可用信用方差被严格守恒于 V(1−V)"这一结构事实，设计树状 rollout 与优势分配框架：沿结果分叉前沿扩展推理树、用边界缩放异方差模型 + 两遍 RTS 树信念平滑在 O(|V|) 内求全局后验、并对已吸收节点施加零和、对数长度中心化的后缀优势，从而把算力与梯度信用聚焦到未决决策点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准策略优化器在整条轨迹上"盲目"分配 rollout 算力与梯度信用，导致两类病态：难题上早期一步失误即令后续轨迹脱轨；可解题上答案早已确定后仍持续生成冗余验证（吸收式反刍）。作者将二者归因于同一结构事实——在线验证器下真实前缀通过概率 V^π(u) 为 [0,1] 有界 Doob 鞅，其可学习信用方差守恒且上界为 V^π(u)(1−V^π(u))，在脱轨边界(V^π→0)与吸收边界(V^π→1)均趋于零。因此盲目分配与这一方差几何不匹配，需要围绕之重构 rollout 与信用分配。

Method: (1) 树状 rollout：沿"话语对齐"的推理树、按结果分叉的上置信前沿(UCB)扩展，把采样导向未决决策点、远离已吸收的反刍分支；(2) 信念推断：以边界缩放的异方差边方差建模隐节点值，通过两遍 Rauch–Tung–Striebel (RTS) 树信念平滑在 O(|V|) 线性时间求精确全局后验，避免 O(m^3) 矩阵求逆，同时门控掉边界处的虚假噪声；(3) 优势构造：检测最小吸收前沿节点，仅在吸收之后施加零和、对数长度中心化的后缀优势，使吸收前推导的优势保持不变（可证不变性）。

Result: 摘要未给出实验数据；结果性主张为：(a) 将脱轨与吸收两类病态统一解释为鞅几何下信用方差在边界处消失；(b) 提供线性时间 O(|V|) 的精确全局后验推断，免去 O(m^3) 矩阵求逆并抑制边界噪声；(c) 仅吸收后施加后缀优势，保证吸收前推导的可证不变性，使算力与梯度信用集中于未决分叉点。

Conclusion: RLVR 中的信用分配问题可从"前缀通过概率为有界 Doob 鞅、可学习信用方差上界为 V^π(1−V^π)"这一几何出发加以刻画；BRIAR 以分叉前沿 rollout、RTS 树信念平滑与吸收后零和后缀优势三项组件实现与该几何对齐的算力与信用分配，为长链推理的树形 RLVR 提供了一套原理化框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has rapidly advanced long-chain reasoning, yet standard policy optimizers distribute rollout compute and gradient credit blindly across a trajectory. On hard problems, an early misstep derails the remaining trace, whereas on solvable problems, the policy keeps generating redundant verification long after the answer is already determined. We trace both pathologies to a single structural fact: under an on-policy verifier, the true prefix pass probability $V^π(u)$ along a reasoning trace forms a $[0,1]$-bounded Doob martingale whose learnable credit variance is strictly conserved and bounded above by $V^π(u)(1-V^π(u))$, vanishing at both the derailed ($V^π\to 0$) and absorbed ($V^π\to 1$) boundaries. We introduce BRIAR (Bifurcation-guided Rollout and Inference on Adaptive Reasoning trees), a tree-structured RLVR framework built directly around this martingale geometry. First, BRIAR expands discourse-aligned reasoning trees along an upper-confidence outcome-bifurcation frontier, steering rollouts away from absorbed rumination and toward unresolved decision points. Second, it models latent node values with boundary-scaled heteroscedastic edge variances and computes exact global posteriors in linear $\mathcal{O}(|V|)$ time via two-pass Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) tree belief smoothing, eliminating $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ matrix inversions while gating out spurious boundary noise. Third, BRIAR detects minimal absorbed frontier nodes and applies zero-sum log-length centered suffix advantages strictly post-absorption, leaving pre-absorption derivations provably invariant.

</details>


### [127] [Transferring Architecture as an Optimization Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34816)
*Vighnesh Subramaniam,Boris Katz,Brian Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将网络的初始化几何视为一种优化先验，提出通过将目标网络对齐到另一个随机初始化"引导网络"的表征几何（不传特征、不接收梯度、不用标签），可跨架构转移"优化命运"，从而把深度优势和表征粒度转移到目标网络中并提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初始化不仅决定权重尺度，还在输入数据上建立初始几何，决定哪些样本被视为相近、哪些表征被梯度下降优先细化。作者希望探究一个网络的优化先验能否通过参照完全不同的架构而被改变，即架构的"命运"是否可与其物理结构解耦。

Method: 提出一种初始化对齐程序：在下游训练之前，将目标网络对齐到随机初始化的引导网络的表征几何。该对齐不传递已学特征、引导网络不接收梯度、也不使用标签，属于纯几何层面的跨架构初始化转移。

Result: 在两个场景中验证了"命运"的跨架构转移收益：(1) 将浅层网络对齐到深层网络，为其注入深度的优化优势；(2) 用细分辨率网络塑造粗表征网络的几何，转移表征粒度。两种情况均提升了目标网络的下游性能。

Conclusion: 模型的架构命运可以与其物理结构解耦，并能在初始化阶段被显式修改；初始化几何作为可迁移的优化先验，为跨架构的初始化设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Initialization determines the optimization "fate" of a model, going far beyond just setting the scale of its weights. It establishes a fundamental initial geometry over the input data, dictating certain aspects such as which examples are considered close and which representations gradient descent will naturally refine first. In this paper, we view the initial geometry of a network as an optimization prior and ask whether a network can change its optimization prior by referencing a completely different architecture. To investigate this question, we use an initialization procedure where a target network is aligned to the representational geometry of a randomly initialized guide network before any downstream training occurs. Crucially, alignment to the guide network cannot transfer learned features, never receives gradients, and does not use labels. We demonstrate that this cross-architecture transfer of "fate" benefits the target network in two key domains: (1) instilling the optimization benefits of depth into shallow networks by aligning them to deeper networks, and (2) transferring representational granularity by using fine-resolution networks to shape the geometry of networks with coarser representations. In both cases, the alignment results in improved downstream performance of the target network. Ultimately, this demonstrates that a model's architectural destiny can be decoupled from its physical structure and explicitly modified at initialization.

</details>


### [128] [Not all world models can doubt: how training objective and architecture shape epistemic uncertainty in JEPA and RSSM world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34830)
*Simon Baur,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对两类世界模型（JEPA型LeWM与RSSM型DreamerV3）的认知不确定性（EU）进行受控实证比较，发现RSSM在估计前通过循环更新的上下文平均与重建目标的像素加权两机制衰减状态扰动，而JEPA将扰动无损传递至不确定性，导致五项下游任务上存在系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的可信度取决于其预测能力，而预测仅在模型经验范围内可靠，认知不确定性（EU）正是标记经验边界的信号；然而训练目标与架构如何塑造世界模型的EU尚缺乏系统认识。

Method: 在两个不同范式的代表性模型上做受控比较：LeWorldModel（JEPA，无解码器、预测下一嵌入）与DreamerV3（RSSM，由循环隐状态重建观测）。采用事后EU估计器（末层Laplace与集成）。在十个模拟视觉控制环境中，对每一阶段执行受控的状态扰动追踪，直至最终EU估计。

Result: RSSM在扰动到达预测器特征上的估计器之前有两条衰减机制：(1) 循环更新对上下文做平均，单帧变化以部分幅度进入状态，仅当变化持续时才恢复；(2) 重建目标按像素覆盖面积给实体加权，小目标可能因训练目标而丢失。JEPA不具备这两种机制，扰动贯穿所有阶段进入不确定性。该差异转化为五项不确定性相关下游任务（如陌生状态检测、不确定性惩罚规划）上的差距：RSSM的不确定性遗漏了其后验自身已记录到的偏差，而JEPA的不确定性如实报告其模型所见。

Conclusion: 世界模型的不确定性质量由训练目标与架构共同决定：重建型RSSM的EU可能低估真实偏差，而JEPA的EU更忠实反映模型感知；评估世界模型不确定性需考虑其范式固有偏差。

Abstract: World models predict how an environment responds to actions, so that candidate actions can be evaluated before execution. Executed actions, or decisions, taken in this manner are only as reliable as the model's predictive capabilities, and predictions in turn are only reliable within the model's experience. Epistemic uncertainty (EU) is the signal that marks where that experience ends. In this work we offer an extensive empirical study on how training objectives and architectures shape epistemic uncertainty of world models. More precisely, we present a controlled comparison of post-hoc EU (last-layer Laplace and ensembles) between two dominant models of different paradigms: 1) LeWorldModel (LeWM): A joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), which predicts the next embedding without a decoder, and 2) DreamerV3: A recurrent state-space model (RSSM), which reconstructs the observation from a recurrent latent state. In ten simulated visual-control environments, we follow a controlled state perturbation of the environment state through every stage of the models up to the final EU estimate. We find that the RSSM attenuates the state perturbation through two mechanisms before it reaches an estimator on the predictor feature. Its recurrent update averages over context, so a change in a single frame reaches the state at a fraction of its size and recovers only as the change persists. Its reconstruction objective weights an entity by the pixels it covers, so a small object can be lost due to the training objective. The JEPA has neither mechanism and passes the change through all stages to the uncertainty. We further show that this translates to gaps in five uncertainty related downstream tasks, from unfamiliar-state detection to uncertainty-penalised planning: the RSSM uncertainty misses deviations that its own posterior registers, whereas the JEPA uncertainty reports what its model sees.

</details>


### [129] [From Prediction to Decision: Decision-Focused Fine-Tuning of Autoregressive Tabular Transformers for Cost-Sensitive Financial Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34858)
*Ashkan Golgoon,Giacomo Domeniconi,Samuel Atkins,Sergio Servantez,Zain Khandwala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将预训练自回归Transformer用于金融事件（如欺诈）预测，并主张直接按决策目标微调模型，而非仅做校准后的后验贝叶斯阈值后处理。其在标签token上的受限softmax上最大化机构指定的、按金额缩放的代价矩阵期望效用，属样本依赖代价敏感学习，梯度可闭式求得，并以KL锚定与辅助语言建模维持校准与生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构不直接使用概率，而是做后果不对称且异方差的决策：漏掉高额欺诈与误拒小额消费的代价并不相同。常规做法是对校准概率做后验贝叶斯最优阈值化，作者追问：直接面向决策目标微调模型能否带来更优决策。

Method: 对同一预训练模型微调，使其在标签token上的受限softmax（本身即业务动作分布）在机构指定、按金额缩放的代价矩阵下最大化期望效用；因奖励是(动作,结果,金额)的已知函数且结果可在数据中观测，期望效用目标及梯度可闭式表达，无需采样、critic或优势估计。加入对预训练模型的KL锚与辅助语言建模项以保持校准与生成式事件模型；另给出采样的组归一化（GRPO风格）变体作为轨迹级奖励的扩展点。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数值；方法层面的结果为：期望效用目标与梯度存在闭式解，属逐样本损失对预测概率线性的样本依赖代价敏感学习，且KL锚与辅助LM项可在微调中维持校准与生成建模能力。

Conclusion: 面向决策目标的微调可作为校准后阈值化的替代与改进路径，关键在于利用奖励的已知结构获得免采样的闭式优化，同时以KL正则与辅助损失保住校准与生成能力；GRPO风格变体提供了向轨迹级奖励扩展的接口。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformers trained on tokenized tabular event streams are a simple and strong baseline for predicting financial events such as fraud. Yet institutions do not act on probabilities; they act on decisions whose consequences are asymmetric and heteroscedastic: the cost of a missed high-value fraud and that of a wrongly declined small purchase are not the same. The standard bridge is post-hoc Bayes-optimal thresholding of calibrated probabilities under a cost matrix. We ask whether fine-tuning the model to the decision objective can do better. We fine-tune the same pretrained model so that its restricted softmax over label tokens (already a distribution over business actions) maximizes expected utility under an institution-specified, amount-scaled cost matrix. Because the reward is a known function of (action, outcome, amount) and the outcome is observed in the data, the expected-utility objective and its gradient are available in closed form. This is example-dependent cost-sensitive learning whose per-example loss is linear in the predicted probability, with no sampling, critics, or advantage estimation. A KL anchor to the pretrained model and an auxiliary language-modeling term preserve calibration and the generative event model. A sampled, group-normalized (GRPO-style) variant provides the extension point to trajectory-level rewards.

</details>


### [130] [When to Trust a PFN: Detecting Harmful Shift in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34767)
*Viet Nguyen,Herman Bergström,Stephan Rabanser,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CALD，一种无需测试标签的轻量级无监督监控器：通过微调解码器在保持ID锚集精度前提下最大化与测试预测的分歧，以微调后锚损失作为检验统计量检测有害OOD批次，并理论证明其单侧检验性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格型先验拟合网络（PFN，如TabPFN、TabICL）在表格分类上表现优异，但部署时分布漂移会导致静默失败，缺乏可靠的失效预警机制。

Method: CALD（Calibrated Anchor-Loss Disagreement）：微调解码器，使其在ID锚集上保持精度的同时最大化与测试集预测的分歧；用微调后的锚集损失作为统计量，在无测试标签条件下识别有害OOD批次。理论证明该锚损失在足够严重漂移下的期望严格低于ID评估时，从而构成单侧检验。

Result: 在覆盖表格型与结构化分布漂移的14个数据集上，于TabPFN和TabICL两个骨干模型上，CALD在有害漂移上匹配或超越各基线，且对良性漂移的误报显著更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CALD以极低开销为表格PFN提供可理论保证的部署期失效监控，兼顾有害漂移检出率与良性漂移低误报。

Abstract: Tabular prior-fitted networks (PFNs) work well across tabular classification problems but can fail silently at deployment when distributions shift. We propose CALD (Calibrated Anchor-Loss Disagreement), a lightweight unsupervised monitor that triggers an alarm to warn when a PFN might fail. By finetuning the decoder to maximize disagreement with the test-set prediction while maintaining accuracy on an ID anchor set, CALD identifies harmful OOD batches through the resulting anchor loss, requiring no labels on test batches. We prove that the post-finetune anchor loss has a strictly lower expectation under sufficiently severe shift than under ID eval, yielding a one-sided test. Across 14 datasets covering tabular and structured distribution shift, on both TabPFN and TabICL, CALD matches or outperforms baselines on harmful shifts and is markedly more resilient to benign-shift false alarms.

</details>


### [131] [Interpretable and Accurate Hybrid Decision Trees with Selective Case-Based Splits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34887)
*Cristiano Landi,Alessio Cascione,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出融合特征型与案例型分裂的混合贪婪决策树架构，并引入选择性案例型分裂（自动搜索最具信息量的特征子集定义分裂），在表格数据集上超越标准决策树、媲美集成方法且保持浅层可解释树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树是可解释模型的代表，既有分裂策略（轴平行、斜向、基于邻近/样例的案例型、混合型）种类繁多，但没有任何单一策略普遍最优；作者希望在贪婪学习框架内结合特征型与案例型分裂的优点，兼顾案例型的高特异表达力与特征型分裂的简洁性。

Method: 两项贡献：(1) 混合树架构，将特征型分裂与案例型分裂统一整合，允许树的不同节点采用异构分裂；(2) 新型"选择性案例型分裂"——当需要分裂时，算法搜索最具信息量的特征子集来定义分裂条件，从而在案例型的表达特异性和特征型的简洁性之间取得平衡。整体仍沿用贪婪学习框架。

Result: 在多样化的表格数据集上，该方法优于标准决策树，性能与集成方法相当，同时生成的树更浅，且保持甚至提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 特征型与案例型分裂的混合及选择性案例型分裂可在贪婪决策树框架内有效提升性能，在保持浅层、可解释结构的同时达到接近集成模型的精度，说明异构分裂设计是提升决策树表达力与性能的可行方向。

Abstract: Decision trees are among the most well-known interpretable models in Machine Learning. Over the years, a wide variety of strategies have been proposed to define split conditions within the greedy learning framework, aiming to guide instance routing and enhance predictive performance. These strategies range from axis-parallel and oblique splits based on feature values to proximity-based splits using exemplar instances and more complex hybrid approaches. However, no single method has emerged as universally superior. In this work, we present two key contributions to the construction of greedy decision trees. First, we propose a hybrid tree architecture that integrates both feature-based and case-based splits, allowing for heterogeneous splits throughout the tree. Second, we introduce a novel type of split that leverages a selective case-based approach: when a split is needed, our method searches for the most informative subset of features to define the split, effectively balancing the specificity of case-based methods with the simplicity of feature-based ones. Experiments on a diverse set of tabular datasets show that our method outperforms standard decision trees and achieves performance on par with ensemble methods, while producing shallow trees that retain or even improve predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [132] [Attack Policy Selection can Mislead Monitor Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34934)
*Charlie Griffin,Oliver Makins,Asa Cooper Stickland,Alessandro Abate,Yarin Gal,Abby D'Cruz,Xander Davies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 以某已投产OpenAI监控器为案例，发现其漏报率高度依赖攻击策略的生成方法及优化程度（单一RL方法内0.5%–20.8%，不同最优方法间6.4%–23.0%），故仅用基础攻击方法的监控评估可能造成虚假安全感；把代理攻击轨迹结果解读为部署证据需要单独的合法性论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI监控器（对AI智能体危险行为打标的分类器）通常用攻击策略生成的对话轨迹作代理来评估，但此类代理的可靠性缺乏检验；作者希望量化评估结论对攻击策略选择与优化程度的敏感性。

Method: 以一款已投产的OpenAI监控器为案例，系统性改变攻击策略的生成方法与优化水平（强化学习各级优化、优化提示词、轨迹编辑），所有攻击策略仅针对代理监控器开发，并将结果迁移到留出的GPT-5.4目标监控器上评估漏报率。

Result: 漏报率对方法内优化程度高度敏感：单一RL方法内从0.5%升至20.8%；各方法在最佳优化水平下跨方法为6.4%–23.0%（优化提示词 vs. 轨迹编辑）。所有攻击均成功迁移至留出的GPT-5.4目标监控器。

Conclusion: 监控器规避评估易产生误导：只用基础攻击方法会错误高估监控器强度；代理轨迹可能引入可见伪影（如推理未被指令要求的"side task"）而影响判断。将结果解读为部署证据，须为代理的信息性（含保守性）提供独立合法性论证，而如何做出有说服力的论证仍是开放问题。

Abstract: AI monitors, classifiers that flag dangerous behaviour by AI agents, are often evaluated against transcripts generated by attack policies acting as proxies for dangerous behaviour in deployment. We evaluate an ex-production OpenAI monitor as a case study and find that measured false negative rate is highly sensitive to both the method used to generate attack policies and the level of optimisation applied within that method. This rate ranged from 0.5\% to 20.8\% across optimisation levels of a single method (reinforcement learning), and from 6.4\% to 23.0\% across methods at their best observed optimisation level (optimised prompting vs.\ transcript editing). Every attack policy was developed against proxy monitors only: all reported results show attacks transferring to the held-out GPT-5.4 target monitor. Our results suggest that monitor evasion evaluations can easily mislead: an evaluation using only basic attack methods may give false confidence that a monitor is strong.
  The sensitivity to attack-policy choice raises a problem: interpreting any particular result as evidence about deployment performance requires justification. The transcripts produced by attack policies are proxies for the dangerous behaviour a monitor must flag in deployment. These proxies can introduce visible artefacts, such as reasoning about a ``side task'' absent from the instructions shown to the monitor, that affect monitor judgements. Given the sensitivity above, the choice of proxy can change an evaluation's conclusion. Reading these results as evidence about deployment therefore requires a separate legitimacy argument for why the proxies are informative, including any justification of conservativeness. Making convincing arguments remains an open problem.

</details>


### [133] [Adaptive Reliability Reserves for Residential Battery Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34779)
*Maruthi Vemula,Neeraj Praneeth Gajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HomeFleet-ERCOT基准，用县级风险阈值替代固定20%电池备电策略，在休斯顿2024回放中把五小时备电覆盖率从75.0%提升到86.5%并增加约USD 10.2/户年化模型收益，但配对区间含零；同时以21次PPO训练考察收益—备电权衡，并发布可复现的控制器与检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅电池在市场放电与家庭停电备电之间存在能量争夺，固定20%备电比例缺乏风险意识；需在同一框架下联合评估市场收益与家庭备电服务，填补可复现基准的空白。

Method: 构建HomeFleet-ERCOT（观测电价+县级停电数据+可识别仿真家庭+因果控制器输入）；对比独立家庭优化与21次PPO训练（10个收益—成本候选，备电覆盖31.1%–96.3%）；采用5小时备电覆盖与年化边际收益配对评估；设告警充电覆盖机制并给出条件性能量与功率充足性保证；进行时间顺序、地理、车队、风暴迁移测试及合成测试（短/漏/误告警、负荷抬升、容量衰减、丢包），并以静态AC审计与老化对比做敏感性分析。

Result: 风险阈值策略：备电覆盖75.0%→86.5%，边际收益+USD 10.2/户（95%配对区间+1.7至+21.7），但覆盖增益区间含零；21次PPO中10个候选收益低于固定20%，其中3个覆盖亦更低；41次县级大面积停电事件中仅19.5%能为所有电池预设提供足够连续预警；静态AC审计保留原有馈线违规，老化对比显示模型敏感性。

Conclusion: 结果为条件性仿真，而非实测公司可靠性；风险阈值权衡在统计上不稳健，学习型控制器多未优于简单基线；所发布的基准、控制器与检查点支持收益与备电的联合评估与可复现比较。

Abstract: In a Houston 2024 replay, replacing a fixed 20 percent battery reserve with a county-risk threshold raises observed five-hour backup coverage from 75.0 percent to 86.5 percent and adds USD 10.2 in modeled annualized margin per home (paired 95 percent interval: USD +1.7 to USD +21.7). The paired coverage-gain interval includes zero. This is a conditional simulation result, not measured company reliability. Residential batteries face this tradeoff because market discharge consumes energy also needed for household outages. We release HomeFleet-ERCOT, combining observed prices and county outages with identified simulated homes and causal controller inputs. We compare independent-home optimization with 21 proximal policy optimization (PPO) training runs; ten promise-cost candidates span 31.1 percent-96.3 percent backup coverage. Of these, 10 have lower modeled margin than fixed 20 percent, and 3 also have lower coverage. Chronological, geographic, fleet, and storm tests examine transfer. An alert charging override has a conditional energy-and-power sufficiency guarantee, but only 19.5 percent of 41 county mass-outage episodes provide enough continuous warning for every battery preset. Synthetic tests include short, missed, and false warnings, load uplift, capacity loss, and dropout. A static AC audit retains existing feeder violations; aging comparisons expose model sensitivity. The released benchmark, controllers, and checkpoints provide a reproducible basis for comparing these tradeoffs. This work supports the design of residential battery programs that assess market value and household backup service together.

</details>


### [134] [Split-RL: Local Conflict Resolution in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34799)
*Benjamin Fuhrer,Chen Tessler,Gal Dalal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Split-RL 是一种基于梯度提升树（GBT）的强化学习算法，利用引导标签在状态空间中局部隔离相互冲突的目标信号，避免梯度聚合造成的目标信息丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略常需融合在空间和时间上局部存在的矛盾反馈（如开阔区域追求速度、狭窄缝隙追求精度）。标准神经网络参数共享全部来源的反馈，缺乏结构性机制来隔离冲突信号；而标准树拟合过程也会在到达叶节点前聚合相互矛盾的梯度信号。因此需要一个能在结构上显式分离冲突更新的方法。

Method: 提出 Split-RL：以 GBT 提供的归纳偏置显式地按目标划分状态空间，并将引导标签（可来自启发式规则、传感器事件或现有规则系统）整合进树的构建过程，把这些更新路由到互不相交的状态空间区域，从而防止信号干扰。理论上分析了早期梯度聚合如何丢失目标特定信息，并刻画了 Split-RL 打分何时能够隔离冲突更新。

Result: 在约束 RL 和含混合数据集的离线 RL 场景（各需平衡两个局部冲突的目标）中，涵盖空间冲突、稀有事件冲突和时间冲突领域，Split-RL 在满足回合成本预算的同时保持有竞争力的奖励，并持续优于现有方法。离线设定下，它局部化了模仿与策略改进之间的权衡，在不同数据集规模下均优于其他方法并接近专家性能。

Conclusion: 通过在树构建阶段引入引导标签进行局部路由，Split-RL 有效缓解了梯度聚合导致的冲突信号干扰，为约束 RL 与离线 RL 中多目标权衡问题提供了兼具规则结构与可学习策略的方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies are often trained by mixing contradictory feedback that is localized in space and time. For example, a robot may prioritize speed in open areas versus precision in tight gaps. We introduce Split-RL, a Gradient Boosting Trees (GBT) algorithm that uses guidance labels to locally isolate these conflicting signals. Standard neural networks (NNs) lack the structural mechanism to do this, as their parameters share feedback from all sources. While GBT provides the inductive bias to explicitly partition the state-space per objective, standard tree-fitting procedures aggregate contradictory gradient signals before they reach the leaves. Split-RL leverages this bias to integrate guidance labels into tree construction, routing these updates into disjoint state-space regions and preventing signal interference. Such labels can arise from heuristics, sensor-derived events, or existing rule-based systems, allowing Split-RL to incorporate rule-derived structure while retaining a learned policy. We theoretically show how early gradient aggregation loses objective-specific information and characterize when the Split-RL score isolates conflicting updates. We focus on constrained RL and offline RL with mixed datasets, each balancing two objectives that conflict locally. Across domains with spatial, rare-event, and temporal conflicts, Split-RL consistently outperforms existing methods by meeting the episodic cost budget while maintaining competitive rewards. In offline settings, Split-RL localizes the tradeoff between imitation and policy improvement, outperforming other methods and achieving near-expert performance across varying dataset sizes.

</details>


### [135] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Multi-UAV Data Harvesting Under Variable Team Sizes and Agent Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34818)
*Thiago de Souza Lamenza,Bruno José Olivieri de Souza,Markus Endler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出F-MADDPG，一种支持无线传感器数据采集任务中可变数量无人机与传感器的灵活MADDPG变体，并能应对智能体永久性灾难失效。实验表明F-MADDPG在各规模群体下均可学习、平均任务完成速度优于MLP版MADDPG基线、逆基数团队规模采样优于均匀采样、在永久性智能体损失后仍保持有效性，而训练中暴露故障无一致收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限通信基础设施下无线传感器无法卸载测量数据，需借助无人机机动性进行本地数据采集，多无人机协同可提供高效冗余覆盖；但为不同团队与传感器规模分别训练策略代价高，且固定规模策略对智能体失效脆弱。

Method: 提出F-MADDPG——MADDPG的灵活变体，支持不同数量无线传感器与无人机的数据采集，并容纳任务中永久移除智能体的灾难性故障；与采用多层感知机（MLP）的MADDPG基线对比，评估基于团队规模采样与智能体终止暴露的训练策略。

Result: F-MADDPG在所评估的各群体规模下均能学习；平均任务完成速度快于基线；逆基数团队规模采样相较均匀采样更优；在智能体永久损失后仍有效；训练中暴露故障未带来一致收益。

Conclusion: F-MADDPG为变规模、可容错的无人机协同数据采集提供了有效且灵活的去中心化协调策略学习框架，团队规模采样策略对性能有显著影响，而训练期故障暴露的必要性存疑。

Abstract: Collecting data from wireless sensors can be challenging when limited communication infrastructure prevents them from offloading their measurements. In such settings, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can exploit their mobility to harvest data locally, while coordinated UAV teams can provide efficient and redundant coverage of remote areas. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables such teams to learn decentralized coordination policies. However, training separate policies for different team and sensor populations is costly, and fixed-population policies may be vulnerable to agent failures. This work proposes F-MADDPG, a flexible variant of Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) that supports data harvesting with varying numbers of wireless sensors and UAVs. The framework also accommodates unexpected catastrophic failures that permanently remove agents during a mission. We compare F-MADDPG with a MADDPG baseline that uses multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and evaluate training strategies based on team-size sampling and exposure to agent terminations. The results show that F-MADDPG learns across the evaluated population sizes, completes missions faster than the baseline on average, benefits from inverse-cardinality team-size sampling compared with uniform sampling, remains effective after permanent agent losses, and gains no consistent benefit from exposure to failures during training.

</details>


### [136] [LoopTitan: What Does Parameter Revisit Buy a Mixture-of-Experts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34821)
*Zichen Tian,Yudong Lu,Xuansheng Wu,Na Zou,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Qianru Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用匹配的非重访对照实验拆解循环MoE的性能来源，发现参数重访本身几乎不带来收益；增益主要来自网络形状、残差缩放与额外计算，标准MoE同样可获得。据此提出更强基线LoopTitan-Base并开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期循环MoE（looped MoE）研究报告在相同预算（参数、FLOPs、KV cache）下显著优于标准MoE，但这些比较将"参数重访"与网络形状、残差尺度等其他设计选择混在一起，无法判断重访本身的真实贡献。作者由此提出核心问题：参数重访本身究竟买到了什么？

Method: 采用匹配的非重访对照（matched non-revisit control）进行消融分解。第一步，在匹配预算下对SOTA循环配方逐步分解，将重访置于最后；证明残差缩放在重访层上等效于更低学习率，并在AdamW与Muon两种优化器下对比层间步长不均衡程度。第二步，在固定参数下通过增加计算评估重访：与成倍增加专家数比较，并与等参数等计算量的更深标准MoE比较。

Result: 匹配预算下，非重访对照保留了全部增益：大部分来自网络形状，其余来自残差缩放；该缩放在AdamW不均匀层步长下有益，但在层步长近乎相等的Muon下效应消失。超出匹配预算时，单次额外重访的收益超过专家数翻倍；但在Muon下，无重访的更深标准MoE（等参数等计算）可获得该增益的90.2%。
