Conclusion: 循环MoE的增益实际来自网络形状、优化器和增加的计算，标准MoE均可采纳；重访仅提供MoE本就具备的自由度，即固定参数下以专家换深度。作者提出LoopTitan-Base作为更强MoE基线，在Muon下无需重访或残差缩放即可匹配最强循环配方，并开源LoopTitan及17个预训练模型，实验记录以GitHub issues公开、预测预注册，作为后续研究的Phase 1报告。

Abstract: Looped Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures revisit layer parameters across depths. This design increases effective depth without adding parameters. Recent looped MoE studies report substantial gains over the standard MoE at matched budgets (parameters, FLOPs and KV cache). However, the reported gains still confound parameter revisit with other design choices, e.g., network shape and residual scale. We ask, what does parameter revisit itself buy? Using matched non-revisit controls, we find that parameter revisit itself buys almost nothing. We argue that revisit only offers a freedom an MoE already has, i.e., trading experts for depth (more layers, fewer experts each) at fixed parameters. First, at matched budgets, we decompose the state-of-the-art looped recipe, with revisit last. A non-revisit control retains all the gain. Most comes from network shape, and the rest from residual scale. We prove this scale acts as a lower learning rate on revisited layers. This helps under AdamW's uneven layer step sizes, but disappears under Muon, which keeps them nearly equal. Second, beyond matched budgets, we evaluate revisit by adding compute at fixed parameters. One extra visit gains more than doubling the experts. Yet without revisit, a deeper standard MoE at equal parameters and compute obtains 90.2% of that gain under Muon. Overall, gains come from network shape, the optimizer and added compute, which a standard MoE can adopt. Accordingly, we propose LoopTitan-Base as a stronger MoE baseline. Under Muon, it matches the strongest looped recipe without revisit or residual scale. We open-source LoopTitan and release 17 pretrained models (including LoopTitan-Base and the control). Our experiment record is public as GitHub issues, with predictions pre-registered and the full exploration history kept. This is our Phase 1 technical report, laying the foundation for follow-up studies.

</details>


### [137] [Routeability Before Routing: Routeability Audit Protocol (RAP) and RouteabilityBench for Audited LLM Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34825)
*Yihang Lu,Denica Kjorvezir,Ana Gjorgjevikj,Carola Doerr,Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"可路由性优先"审计框架RAP与RouteabilityBench，在4个基准、66个LLM上检验LLM路由增益，发现多数通用路由增强并不稳定优于单一最佳模型，建议报告路由收益时同时给出可路由性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由的前提是基准中存在足够的提示级差异，使不同模型在不同输入上各有所长。但粗粒度评分的基准常出现大量模型并列最高分，且单一最佳模型本身已几乎无可提升空间，导致路由比较被误读为普遍性证据。因此需要先审计基准与模型组合是否"可路由"，再进行路由比较。

Method: 提出可路由性优先的回顾式审计（routeability-first audit），实例化为RAP与RouteabilityBench这一可运行的声明验证包。审计在基准—模型组合的得分矩阵生成之后、路由器对比结果被解释为一般证据之前执行；对比嵌入集扩大、降维、选择性弃答、聚类、更强回归器等通用路由增强方法，并结合并列最优分析、模型组合构建细节与族校正统计检验。

Result: 在4个基准与66个LLM上，上述通用路由增强并不一致地优于单一最佳模型。最明确的正向证据集中于依赖模型组合的Omni-MATH设定；GPQA、MMLU-Pro与IFEval则分别体现出路由仍然困难或对预测器敏感的不同情形。

Conclusion: 路由收益应与可路由性诊断、并列最优分析、模型组合构建细节以及族校正统计检验一并报告，否则不宜将路由对比解读为一般性证据。

Abstract: LLM routing aims to choose, for each prompt, which model in a portfolio is most likely to answer correctly. This only makes sense when the benchmark contains enough prompt-specific variation for different models to be best on different inputs. On coarse-scored LLM benchmarks, however, many models often tie for the best score, and the single best model may already leave little room for improvement. We therefore propose a routeability-first audit, instantiated as RAP and RouteabilityBench, a runnable claim-verification package. The audit is retrospective: it runs once the benchmark-portfolio score matrix exists and before router comparisons are interpreted as general evidence, rather than certifying a portfolio that has not yet been evaluated. Across four benchmarks and 66 LLMs, generic routing enhancements such as larger embedding sets, dimensionality reduction, selective abstention, clustering, and stronger regressors do not consistently outperform the single best model. The clearest positive evidence is concentrated in portfolio-dependent Omni-MATH settings, while GPQA, MMLU-Pro, and IFEval show different ways in which routing can remain hard or predictor-sensitive. Our practical recommendation is that routing gains should be reported together with routeability diagnostics, tied-best analysis, portfolio construction details, and family-corrected statistical tests.

</details>


### [138] [Gradient TD($λ$) for Non-Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34826)
*Sujeet Lahane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将梯度TD（Gradient TD）中基于λ-return的算法从线性函数逼近扩展到非线性函数逼近，继续以投影贝尔曼误差（PBE）为目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TD学习中半梯度TD可能发散，基于均方贝尔曼误差或TD误差目标的算法难以分析；梯度TD通过值函数投影解决了这些问题，但现有λ-return梯度TD算法仅限线性函数逼近，而现代强函数逼近器多为非线性。

Method: 沿用投影贝尔曼误差（PBE）目标而非近期工作中推广的广义投影贝尔曼误差（GPE）目标，推导适用于非线性函数逼近的λ-return梯度TD算法。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验或数值结果。

Conclusion: 工作将λ-return梯度TD算法推广至非线性函数逼近，并主张以PBE而非广义PBE作为分析基础。

Abstract: Temporal difference (TD) learning is central to reinforcement learning. Many methods use semi-gradient TD, which may diverge, or develop algorithms with mean-squared Bellman or TD-error objectives that are difficult to analyze. Gradient TD algorithms overcome these challenges by using projection of the value function and present convergent off-policy algorithms. However, the algorithm for $λ$-return was limited to linear function approximation. Here, we extend the same to non-linear function approximation, which is desirable since many strong modern function approximators are complex and non-linear. Recent works have derived similar algorithms with a generalized projected Bellman error objective. Alternatively, we continue working with the projected Bellman error.

</details>


### [139] [Provably Efficient Offline Discounted Reinforcement Learning under Recursive Entropic Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34840)
*Amirparsa Bahrami,Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带递归熵风险度量(ERM,参数β≠0)的有限折扣无限时域MDP中的风险敏感强化学习,首次给出离线RL在风险厌恶(β>0)与风险偏好(β<0)两种情形下的严格样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感RL中递归熵风险度量刻画了风险厌恶与风险偏好的决策行为,但离线场景下其样本复杂度尚无严格理论保证,亟需建立可证明的样本效率刻画。

Method: 提出基于模型的算法 ERM-VI-LCB(熵风险度量值迭代+下置信界),在固定数据集上学习近最优策略;推导PAC型样本复杂度上界,并构造匹配的下界。

Result: 得到ERM-VI-LCB的PAC样本复杂度上界;下界表明上界中关于 |β|/(1-γ) 的指数依赖在最坏情形下不可避免。

Conclusion: 给出离线RL在递归ERM下(涵盖风险厌恶与风险偏好)的首个严格样本复杂度保证,并确立指数依赖的紧致性。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted infinite-horizon Markov Decision Processes with recursive entropic risk measures (ERM), parameterized by $β\neq 0$. We consider both risk-averse ($β>0$) and risk-seeking ($β<0$) settings. We consider the offline RL setting, where the agent is provided with a fixed dataset and wishes to learn a near-optimal policy using this dataset. We introduce a model-based algorithm, called ERM Value Iteration Lower Confidence Bound (ERM-VI-LCB) and derive a PAC-type bound on its sample complexity. We also establish a corresponding lower bound, demonstrating that exponential dependence on $|β|/(1-γ)$ asserted by the upper bound is unavoidable in the worst case. The presented bounds provide the first rigorous sample complexity guarantees for offline RL under recursive ERM across both risk-averse and risk-seeking regimes.

</details>


### [140] [Put the World Back: Timestamps Matter in Time Series Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34848)
*Tingrui Huang,Yuanyuan Deng,Qingren Yao,Joaquin Vanschoren,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Songgaojun Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从"世界到序列投影"视角重构时间序列推理，提出时间戳作为连接观测与外部事件的显式索引，并设计诊断协议区分"时间戳 grounding 推理"与"相关性捷径"，揭示现有基准的高准确率并不证明模型真正利用时间戳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列推理评测通常按时间顺序给出事件、且事件已与序列匹配，导致无法判断模型是真的用时间戳对齐观测与事件，还是走了捷径。准确率型基准无法区分这两者，需要诊断手段。

Method: 提出世界-序列投影视角，将时间戳视为显式索引；识别时间戳可显式化的三种结构（时间因果结构、时间语义、事件-序列对齐）；据此构建诊断协议，并在两个任务共310个问题上做受控干预（替换为相对偏移、移除时间戳、破坏事件-序列对齐等）。

Result: 时间戳被替换为相对偏移或移除时，高准确率仍可保持；破坏事件-序列对齐则引发系统性失败。上下文预对齐时模型几乎不需要时间戳；缺少顺序时模型用日期召回世界知识；无日期时依赖相关性捷径且几乎不察觉错误时间戳。

Conclusion: 在预对齐、有序输入上的高准确率不能证明模型使用时间戳进行观测与世界的同步。需以诊断性干预而非仅准确率来评估时间序列推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to time series reasoning. However, current evaluations often give models events in chronological order and already matched to the time series. This makes it unclear whether models use timestamps to connect events with observations or simply take shortcuts. To expose this ambiguity, we reformulate time series reasoning from a world-to-series projection perspective, viewing timestamps as explicit indices that connect numerical observations and external events to shared world states. We identify three structures that timestamps can make explicit: temporal causal structure, time semantics, and event-series alignment. Building on this perspective, we develop a diagnostic protocol that distinguishes timestamp-grounded reasoning from correlational shortcuts, a distinction that accuracy-based benchmarks cannot make. Controlled interventions on 310 questions from two tasks show that high accuracy can persist when timestamps are replaced by relative offsets or removed, while breaking event-series alignment induces systematic failures. When context is pre-aligned, models barely need timestamps. When order is missing, they use dates to recall world knowledge. Without dates, they rely on correlational shortcuts and rarely notice incorrect timestamps. These findings show that high accuracy on pre-aligned, ordered inputs does not establish that a model uses timestamps to synchronize observations with the world. We release our code, model outputs, and trajectory labels at https://github.com/tingrui-huang/timestamps-matter.

</details>


### [141] [Mixture-of-Loops Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34923)
*Lianghui Zhu,Shijie Wang,Xinggang Wang,Jinze Bai,Bo Wang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoLT 用 Mixture-of-Loops Attention 让 KV 成为跨深度/循环共享的内存，并给出 MoLT-R/MoLT-KV 两种变体；U-shaped MoLT 解耦内存构建与预测读出，在保持预测不变的前提下跳过冗余解码遍历，实现质量-效率双赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环 Transformer 通过复用共享层提升表达力，但代价是效率损失。要推进"表达力—效率"前沿，必须重新审视连接相邻循环的核心机制：循环之间究竟应该传递什么。

Method: 提出 Mixture-of-Loops Transformer (MoLT)，其 Mixture-of-Loops Attention (MoLA) 将 keys/values 作为可跨深度与循环选择性复用的内存。两种形式：MoLT-R 在 loop residual 下保留残差流、跨循环读取局部 KV 以提升表达力；MoLT-KV 从 embedding 重启、读取当前循环局部 KV 及前一循环记忆 KV，消除同位置跨循环依赖，并结合 loop forcing 并行训练，支持超出训练循环视野的单次遍历 MoL 解码。进一步提出 U-shaped MoLT，将内存构建与预测读出分离，跳过未使用的解码遍历而不改变输出，并采用 MoL-Hybrid-2 推理配置。

Result: 400B token 预训练中，U-MoLT 在 700M/1.5B dense 与 3B MoE 上均优于 OLMo3：700M 达 1.7x 验证 CE token 效率；3B MoE 十域困惑度降 0.75、十任务准确率升 2.1 个百分点。Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base 上 100B token 继续预训练，11 个域 CE 全部低于同配基线，平均 1.4x token 效率。推理端 MoL-Hybrid-2 在 S 规模(~113M)上延迟 996.6 ms，与非循环 OLMo3 的 1,000.6 ms 相当。

Conclusion: 循环间传递的核心物应为 KV 内存而非残差流；以此构建的 MoLT 系列在预训练与继续预训练中同时提升 token 效率与下游质量，并通过 U 形结构在推理时恢复与非循环基线相当的延迟。

Abstract: Looped Transformers boost expressivity by reusing shared layers across loops, yet at the cost of efficiency. Pushing their expressivity-efficiency frontier requires rethinking the core mechanism connecting adjacent loops: What should be passed as a Transformer loops? We introduce Mixture-of-Loops Transformer (MoLT), whose Mixture-of-Loops Attention (MoLA) uses keys and values (KV) as memory for selective reuse across depths and loops. Under loop residual, MoLT-R retains the residual stream and reads local KV across loops for expressivity. Under loop KV, MoLT-KV instead restarts from embeddings and reads current-loop local KV with preceding-loop memory KV at earlier positions for efficiency. MoLT-KV eliminates same-position cross-loop dependence and, paired with loop forcing for parallel training, unlocks single-traversal Mixture-of-Loops (MoL) decoding beyond the training loop horizon. To balance efficiency and quality, we introduce U-shaped MoLT (U-MoLT), which separates memory construction from prediction readout and skips unused decoder traversals without changing predictions. In 400B-token pretraining, U-MoLT outperforms OLMo3 across 700M/1.5B dense and 3B MoE models, achieving 1.7x validation cross-entropy (CE) token efficiency at 700M while improving 3B MoE 10-domain perplexity by 0.75 and 10-task accuracy by 2.1 percentage points. In 100B-token continued pretraining of Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base, our U-MoLT adaptation lowers CE on all 11 domains over the matched baseline with 1.4x mean token efficiency. At inference, U-MoLT with MoL-Hybrid-2 matches non-looped OLMo3 latency at 996.6 vs. 1,000.6 ms on S-size (~113M) models. Code is available at https://github.com/hustvl/MoLT

</details>


### [142] [A gene informed benchmark for BioScoring of pathology foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34930)
*Paul Vilars,Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh,Maria Vakalopoulou,Dimitris Samaras,Stergios Christodoulidis,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BioScore 基准，以同一组织的空间转录组为参照，无标签量化病理基础模型的图像编码器对细胞身份的表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型多用分类、检索等下游任务性能来比较，只能反映"模型多有用"，无法揭示模型学到了什么生物学信息，缺乏对细胞身份等生物学表征的直接度量。

Method: 用稀疏自编码器将编码器的 patch 嵌入分解为稀疏特征，并将这些特征聚类为固定数量的组；以标记基因定义的 52 种细胞身份为参照，度量在组织切片上每一组特征对该身份表达跟随的特异性。由于自编码器训练完全无监督，若特征组与表达匹配，说明模型仅从图像即可表示该细胞身份。

Result: 评测 8 个病理基础模型与 3 个自然图像模型：所有病理模型得分均高于所有自然图像模型（0.299–0.371 vs 0.214–0.232），配对检验将病理模型区分为三个层级。BioScore 无需标签、参考来自数据本身、少数切片即可评分，但排序结果与监督探针一致；逐身份分解可指示各编码器捕捉或遗漏的细胞身份。

Conclusion: BioScore 提供了面向生物学表征的病理基础模型评估新范式，可作为下游任务基准的补充，并支持细粒度诊断编码器的表征缺陷。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models are usually compared by their performance on downstream tasks such as classification and retrieval. While such benchmarks measure how useful a model is, they provide no insight into the biology it represents. We introduce BioScore, a benchmark that quantifies how well an image encoder captures cell identities, with spatial transcriptomics of the same tissue as the reference. A sparse autoencoder splits the encoder's patch embeddings into sparse features, which we cluster into a fixed number of groups. BioScore then measures, for each of 52 cell identities defined by marker genes, how specifically one group follows the identity's expression over the tissue. Because the autoencoder training is fully unsupervised, a match shows that the model represents the cell identity from the image alone. We score 8 pathology foundation models and 3 natural-image models. Every pathology model scores above every natural-image model (0.299--0.371 \vs 0.214--0.232), and paired tests separate three tiers among the pathology models. Unlike a supervised probe, BioScore needs no labels, builds its reference from the data and scores even from few sections, yet it ranks the encoders as a probe does. Its per-identity breakdown indicates which cell identities each encoder captures or misses.

</details>


### [143] [Can image generation help visual reasoning? An empirical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34935)
*Xueyang Yu,Yifan Jing,Taiming Lu,Linrong Cai,Zhipeng Cai,Yunyang Xiong,Gabriel Sarch,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文检验“多模态模型能否像语言模型用词思考那样用图像思考”，即让模型先生成一张辅助图像再据此回答视觉问题，结果发现生成的辅助图像平均降低准确率3.7个百分点，反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受语言模型“以词思考”启发，作者提出多模态模型是否可“以图像思考”：即模型先描述自己需要的辅助图像，再结合原图与生成图作答。作者希望验证这种自生成视觉辅助能否提升视觉问答表现，从而检验“生成即思考”假设的有效性。

Method: 采用成对实验设计：给定视觉问题，模型先描述所需的辅助（helper）图像，随后基于原始图像与生成图像共同作答，并与不生成图像的回答进行对照。实验覆盖13组视觉语言模型与图像生成器配对、10项任务，包含统一模型（BAGEL、Janus-Pro-7B）在8项任务上的表现；同时在VSTAR上比较在相同规划提示下加入生成图像的效应，并考察重复图像编辑（多轮迭代至十次）在不同任务上的影响。

Result: 整体上，图像生成使平均准确率下降3.7个百分点，10项任务中9项下降。两个统一模型BAGEL与Janus-Pro-7B各自在8项任务上准确率下降。VSTAR上，在同一规划提示中加入生成图像使准确率下降10.47个百分点。重复图像编辑的效果因任务而异：ChartQAPro与MathVista始终低于无生成基线，而ERQA在十次编辑后提升3个百分点。

Conclusion: 现有多模态模型尚不具备通过自生成图像进行有效“视觉思考”的能力，生成的辅助图像在多数任务与模型配对上带来负面影响，仅个别任务在多次编辑后略有收益；表明“以图像思考”的范式仍受生成质量与任务适配性制约。

Abstract: Language models think in words; then multimodal models might think in images. We test whether a generated helper improves the answer to a visual question. A model describes the helper it needs and then answers using the original and generated images together. Across 13 vision-language model and generator pairs, generation lowers mean accuracy by 3.7 percentage points, with declines on nine of ten tasks. Two unified models, BAGEL and Janus-Pro-7B, each lose accuracy on eight tasks. On VSTAR, adding a generated image to the same planning hint lowers accuracy by 10.47 points. Repeated image edits yield different outcomes across tasks. ChartQAPro and MathVista stay below their no-generation baselines, while ERQA gains three points after ten edits.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Context Scaling: A New Frontier of AI Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33160)
*Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"上下文扩展"(context scaling)概念：AI部署失败多源于任务所需状态与决策时可用上下文之间的错配，而不仅是推理能力弱。作者以训练、决策时、上下文基础设施三类预算刻画系统，主张以"模型—基础设施"配对为分析单元，并给出上下文生命周期的五操作框架与评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展范式（预训练扩展提升θ、决策时扩展在给定上下文中搜索/精炼/验证）都无法解决"系统在决策前能获取哪些状态"这一瓶颈。真实部署中相关用户状态、仓库版本、工具轨迹、传感器读数或环境变化可能不可得、失真或选择不当，导致部署失败。

Method: 将模型形式化为受三类预算约束的P_θ(d|c)系统：训练预算B_θ、决策时预算B_d、上下文基础设施预算B_c；定义与任务及干预相关的"有效上下文价值"归因；区分部署状态与模型可见上下文，识别可用性、保真度、选择与利用四类失败；用五操作上下文生命周期组织现有机制；提出受控预算干预与效用—成本曲线用于评估。

Result: 建立了一个概念与分析框架：明确三类扩展项目的交互关系（仅B_c直接作用于决策前可获取的状态），给出失败分类学、生命周期操作分类，以及可操作的评估工具（预算干预、效用—成本曲线）。

Conclusion: 研究议程应转向有据报告（grounded reporting）、交互数据与全成本基础设施，以及面向持久化自适应系统的安全与治理，从而将上下文扩展确立为与预训练扩展、决策时扩展并列的第三条扩展路径。

Abstract: Many failures of deployed AI arise not from weak inference but from a mismatch between the state a task depends on and the context available when the system acts. Relevant user state, repository revisions, tool traces, sensor readings, or environment changes may be unavailable, distorted, or poorly selected. We call the effort to reduce this bottleneck context scaling. We frame a model as $P_θ(d\mid c)$ within a system governed by three budgets: training $B_θ$, decision-time $B_d$, and context-infrastructure $B_c$. Pretraining scaling invests in $θ$; decision-time scaling searches, refines, or verifies decisions under supplied context; context scaling improves the system's capacity to acquire, represent, deliver, evaluate, and persist deployment state for model use. The three programs interact, but only $B_c$ targets what the system can make available before a decision. This view makes the model-infrastructure pair the unit of analysis. We define effective-context value as a task- and intervention-dependent attribution, separate deployment state from model-visible context, and identify availability, fidelity, selection, and utilization failures. We then organize current mechanisms through a five-operation context lifecycle and propose controlled budget interventions and utility-cost curves for evaluation. The resulting agenda centers on grounded reporting, interaction-data and full-cost infrastructure, and safety and governance for persistent, adaptive systems.

</details>


### [145] [Constraint-Aware Test-Time Scoring for LLM and VLM Candidate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33161)
*Rangarajan Ramesh,Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种组合"硬性检查 + 软性加权打分"的自适应候选答案评分框架，用于在LLM/VLM多个候选答案中选出最优者；消融实验表明收益主要来自"硬性检查 + 候选比较"，而非逐任务软因子配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有候选答案选择方式（模型自置信度、学习型judge模型、领域检查）各有缺陷：流畅但不安全、数学上看似合理但物理不可行、judge高分却违反任务要求。需要统一的、可落地工具验证的评分机制。

Method: 框架含三部分：drafting model（生成候选）、configuring model（为每个任务选择并加权检查项）、scoring models（评估无接地工具可计算的属性）。每个候选须通过少量硬性检查，再由较宽的软性检查融合成单一分数；在CWEval、LIBERO（含安全变体）、LiveCodeBench、HotpotQA四个任务上评测，并进行组件消融。

Result: 硬性检查与候选比较的组合带来最大增益：检索增强QA的联合正确性-安全性提升至+0.21，CWEval代码生成安全性提升至+0.28；增益大小与首样本的弱程度相关，首样本已较强时增益趋近零甚至略负。逐任务软因子选择与加权层相比更简单方案无显著增益。

Conclusion: 构建了统一的评分框架，并通过消融确认增益来源是有接地的硬性检查而非软因子配置。

Abstract: When an LLM or VLM produces several candidate answers, picking the best one usually relies on the model's own confidence, a learned judge model, or a domain check. Each has a known weakness: an answer can be fluent but insecure, mathematically plausible but physically infeasible, or rated highly by a judge while quietly breaking a task requirement. We propose a scoring framework that combines a small set of hard checks every answer must pass with a wider set of softer checks whose scores are blended into one number per candidate. This adaptive scoring framework consists of three components: a drafting model that produces the candidates, a configuring model that selects and weights the checks for each task, and scoring models that rate properties no grounded tool can compute. We apply our proposed method to four tasks: writing code that is both correct and secure (CWEval), generating robot manipulation plans (LIBERO with added safety variants), passing competitive-programming hidden tests (LiveCodeBench), and answering questions from retrieved documents (HotpotQA). An ablation study isolates the contribution of each component and finds that the largest gains come from combining hard checks with candidate comparison: on tasks where the first sampled answer is often broken or unsafe, this pairing lifts joint correctness-and-safety by up to +0.21 (retrieval-augmented QA) and code-generation safety by up to +0.28 (CWEval), with the size of the gain tracking how weak the first sample is, falling to near zero or slightly negative where it is already strong. The per-task layer that selects and weights the softer checks adds no measurable value over simpler alternatives. This builds a unified scoring framework, together with an ablation that identifies the grounded hard checks, not the soft-factor configuration, as the source of the gain.

</details>


### [146] [AutoformBench: A Lean Formalization Benchmark for Advanced Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33165)
*Charles Arnal,Ahmad Rammal,Niket Patel,Fabian Gloeckle,Bartosz Piotrowski,Amaury Hayat,Julia Kempe,Rémi Munos,Vivien Cabannes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AutoformBench，一个包含341个Lean 4形式化任务的基准，源自24个高等数学文献，要求智能体在构造缺失定义与中间引理的基础上完成证明，而非孤立定理证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多提供孤立定理陈述，且所需支撑理论在Mathlib中已基本齐备，无法反映真实数学形式化的核心难点——即构建连接结果与形式化库的定义与中间引理等支撑基础设施。

Method: 由具备Lean经验的数学家人工形式化并审核每个目标陈述，仅将Lean证明留给被评测智能体；每个问题嵌入较大数学发展中，智能体获得非形式化证明，需将其翻译为Lean并自行构造缺失定义、中间引理与支撑设施。随后比较前沿模型在这些问题上的表现。

Result: 前沿模型呈现大体共享的难度前沿：成功与否更多取决于模型构建缺失形式化基础设施的能力，而与数学领域的相关性较小。

Conclusion: 真实数学形式化的瓶颈在于基础设施构建能力而非领域知识，AutoformBench为此类评测提供了更贴近实际的基准，数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of producing machine-checked Lean proofs of advanced mathematical results. Real mathematical formalization, however, is not isolated theorem proving: it requires building the definitions and intermediate lemmas that connect a result to a formal library. Existing evaluations often provide isolated theorem statements in settings where much of this supporting theory is already available in Mathlib. We introduce AutoformBench, a benchmark of 341 Lean 4 formalization tasks drawn from 24 advanced mathematical sources. Lean-experienced mathematicians formalized and audited each target statement, leaving the corresponding Lean proof to the evaluated agent. Each problem is embedded in a broader mathematical development: agents receive the informal proof but must translate it into Lean while constructing the missing definitions, intermediate lemmas, and supporting infrastructure. We compare the performance of frontier models on these problems. We find a largely shared difficulty frontier: success depends less on mathematical domain than on models' ability to construct missing formal infrastructure. Data and code at https://github.com/facebookresearch/autoformbench

</details>


### [147] [General-Purpose Decision Models for AI Configuration Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33190)
*Jingzhi Gong,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文考察通用决策模型（以Jev为代表）能否指导AI配置调优，并将其分别作为优化器、性能预测器和区域引导器，与常规方法和LLM在8个调优任务上对比，发现常规优化器与预测器总体更优，但区域引导可提升调优质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI配置调优需在有限预算下搜索能提升系统性能的设置，而现有调优器的采样与搜索策略各异，决策策略的选择同时影响调优质量与成本。作者希望厘清通用决策模型在调优流程中究竟能扮演何种角色、在何处真正带来收益。

Method: 以已部署的通用决策模型Jev为代表性对象，在8个AI调优任务上，将其分别作为优化器、性能预测器和区域引导器使用，并与常规优化器、常规预测器以及大语言模型（LLM）进行对照比较。

Result: 在8项对比中，常规优化器有6项排名第一；常规预测器在7项中优于Jev；但通过区域引导，Jev找到的配置困惑度低于原生调优器所选配置，在可接受额外决策时间与API费用的前提下带来质量增益。

Conclusion: 应谨慎选择决策模型的角色：不宜用通用决策模型替代常规预测器，区域引导是其价值所在；需在调优质量与成本之间做权衡，并据此给出可操作的选型建议。

Abstract: AI configuration tuning searches for settings that improve the performance of AI systems under a limited budget. Existing tuners use different sampling and search policies, making the choice of decision strategy central to both tuning quality and cost. We investigate whether general-purpose decision models can guide tuning, using Jev as a deployed representative that returns structured choices and numeric scores across diverse tasks. To assess where it helps, we compare its use as an optimizer, performance predictor, and region guider against conventional methods and a large language model (LLM) on eight AI tuning tasks. Conventional optimizers attain rank 1 in six of eight comparisons. Similarly, established predictors outperform Jev in seven. Through region guidance, however, it finds configurations with lower perplexity than those selected by the native tuner, offering a quality gain when additional decision time and API fees are acceptable. These findings yield actionable recommendations on selecting the decision role, retaining conventional predictors, and balancing tuning quality against cost.

</details>


### [148] [What, Who, and When: Structured Harm Assessment for Mental Health Safety via Short Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33263)
*Catarina Botelho,António Farinhas,John Mendonça,Areti Vassilopoulos,Inês Gonçalves,Maya D'Eon,Hugo Pitorro,Laura Cabello,Carrie Spadine,Nuno M. Guerreiro,Ricardo Rei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出临床导向的三维危害分类法（危害是什么、涉及谁、何时发生），将危害刻画与干预决策分离，并以之为基础训练35B-A3B推理式守护模型MindGuard2，在同等平衡准确率下比GLM-5.3快约10倍，推进准确率-延迟帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于心理健康支持日益广泛，安全风险突出；现有守护模型仅识别有害内容的类别，忽视判断恰当回应所需的语境，且安全策略被固化在模型权重中，难以更新与审计。同时公开基准与真实对话的分布存在偏差。

Method: 构建经临床专家验证的三维危害分类法（what/who/when），把危害刻画与干预决策解耦，使安全策略以临床医生 curation 的显式指令形式外置于权重；验证一组LLM评判者以实现规模化标注；用该分类法重标注公开基准；采用短推理链监督微调加带推理长度惩罚的强化学习训练35B-A3B守护模型MindGuard2。

Result: 重标注显示公开基准中极少包含针对用户自身的危害，而该类危害在真实对话中约占一半；MindGuard2在两个公开基准与真实对话上取得与规模大得多的GLM-5.3相当的平衡准确率，中位延迟约低10倍；在高召回下保持低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 基于临床分类法并将策略外置的推理式守护模型可在准确率-延迟权衡上取得更优表现，同时降低过度干预风险；作者发布SOTA守护模型、合成对话数据及公开基准的新分类法标注。

Abstract: The growing use of large language models (LLMs) for mental health support raises important safety concerns. Existing guard models primarily identify categories of harmful content, often overlooking the context needed to determine an appropriate response. We introduce a clinically grounded taxonomy that characterizes harm along three dimensions: what the harm is, who is involved, and when it occurs. It further separates harm characterization from intervention decisions, thus enabling safety policies to be specified outside the model weights through explicit, clinician-curated instructions that can be updated and audited. We validate the taxonomy with expert clinicians and validate a panel of LLM judges to scale annotation. Relabeling public benchmarks in this taxonomy shows that they rarely contain harm directed at the user, which accounts for nearly half of the harm in real conversations. We then train MindGuard2, a 35B-A3B reasoning-based guard model using supervised fine-tuning on short reasoning traces followed by reinforcement learning with a reasoning-length penalty, to minimize inference overhead while preserving the benefits of reasoning. On two public benchmarks and real-world conversations, MindGuard2 achieves balanced accuracy comparable to the substantially larger GLM-5.3 at approximately 10x lower median latency, advancing the accuracy-latency Pareto frontier. Furthermore, it achieves low false-positive rates at high recall, helping balance the need to detect unsafe situations against the risk of unnecessary interventions that may disrupt support and undermine user trust. We release a safety guardrail model with SOTA performance trained on synthetic data, together with synthetic conversations and annotations of public benchmarks in our taxonomy.

</details>


### [149] [Certify What You Plant: Shortcut-Free Proof Depth in Logical Reasoning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33303)
*Baptiste Juran,Dimitri Kachler,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用"问题实际所需"的证明结构度量替代简单植入证明深度，对8个逻辑推理数据集进行审计，并构造每道证明都符所需结构且带独立可验证证书的问题集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化生成的逻辑题通常通过"植入一条指定深度的证明"来设定难度，但一条植入证明只是众多可能证明之一：题目可能存在更短的证明，或存在多条互不相关的证明，因此标注深度会高估解题实际要求。

Method: 在给定推理规则集下，计算每道题所有证明都必须采用的步骤、最短证明长度、极小证明的个数以及不可或缺的前提，并导出可由独立检查器验证的证书；随后利用"每个规则集只需证明一次的捷径目录"，构造所有证明都具备指定结构的题目，并逐题完整验证并附带证书。

Result: 审计8个数据集发现：ProofWriter与Blocksworld的深度是被迫的；FLDx2中标注深度5的题目实际只需约两次特定规则应用；ZebraLogic的答案不依赖任何特定步骤。训练中，等规模下混合各认证题型（含前向生成无法产生、需模型检验的问题）对Qwen3 0.6B–8B的迁移效果优于FLDx2式推导数据。

Conclusion: 应以可验证的"实际证明要求"度量题目难度，并采用最佳数据为深度与题型混合的认证问题集进行训练。

Abstract: Procedurally generated logic problems are used to both evaluate and train reasoning in language models. Their difficulty is usually set by planting a proof, that is, by sampling a proof of the desired depth and stating its premises as the problem. However, a planted proof is only one possible proof. A problem can also have shorter proofs, or several unrelated ones, and its stated depth then overstates what solving it requires. We measure what a problem actually requires, relative to a stated set of inference rules. For each problem, we compute the steps every proof must make, the shortest proof, the number of minimal proofs and the indispensable premises, and export certificates that an independent checker verifies. Audits of eight datasets find depth that is forced (ProofWriter, Blocksworld), problems labelled depth 5 that require about two specific rule applications (FLDx2), and answers that need no particular step (ZebraLogic). We then construct problems whose every proof has a requested structure, using a catalog of shortcuts proved once per rule set. Every constructed problem is checked in full and comes with its own certificate. In training, the best data mixes depths and problem types. At equal size, such mixtures of certified problem types, including model-checking problems that forward generation does not produce, transfer better than FLDx2-style derivation data for Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B.

</details>


### [150] [CA-RAG: Constraint-Aligned Retrieval for Structurally Faithful Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33497)
*Vimal Kumar Kumar,Meghana Kshirsagar,Conor Ryan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出约束对齐RAG（CA-RAG），在证据选择前将本体导出的结构约束与政策知识图谱关系注入分节查询，提升合规敏感长文本生成的证据充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG仅按语义相关性筛选证据，而合规敏感的长文本生成还需支撑显式结构义务；语义相关检索可能遗漏强制下游条款所需的信息。

Method: 构建检索条件化框架CA-RAG：先用本体推导结构约束、并以政策为依据构建知识图谱关系，将其增强到分节查询中，再进行证据选择；与标准RAG及仅在生成阶段加约束的变体对比，采用人工标注证据的检索评估与多模型-领域生成实验。

Result: k=6时，P@k由0.5268升至0.5692，池化R@k由0.5402升至0.5950，F1@k由0.5389升至0.5672；各检索预算下均有稳定提升。生成阶段在多组模型-领域设置下合规性与可靠性提升；仅在生成阶段加约束者条款覆盖率更高，但整体合规性与可靠性更低。

Conclusion: 结构对齐的检索能提升合规敏感长文本生成中的证据充分性；约束应前置于检索环节而非仅施加于生成阶段。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically optimises evidence selection for semantic relevance, whereas compliance-sensitive long-form generation also requires evidence supporting explicit structural obligations. Semantically relevant retrieval may therefore omit information needed for mandatory downstream clauses. We propose Constraint-Aligned RAG (CA-RAG), a retrieval-conditioning framework that augments section-specific queries with ontology-derived structural constraints and policy-grounded knowledge-graph relations before evidence selection. Retrieval-only evaluation with manually annotated evidence shows consistent improvements across retrieval budgets. At $k=6$, CA-RAG increases Precision@$k$ from $0.5268$ to $0.5692$, pooled Recall@$k$ from $0.5402$ to $0.5950$, and F1@$k$ from $0.5389$ to $0.5672$ over standard RAG. Generation experiments further show improvements in compliance and reliability in several model--domain settings, while generation-only constraints achieve higher clause coverage but lower overall compliance and reliability. These results indicate that structurally aligned retrieval can improve evidence sufficiency in compliance-sensitive long-form generation.

</details>


### [151] [The Platonic Representation Hypothesis in Jacobian-Space: Language and Vision Models Agree Approximately Up to a Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33304)
*Ross Murphy,Gabriel Brostow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将"柏拉图表示假说"(PRH)从表征层面拓展到"作用"层面，提出在Jacobian透镜构造的J-space中视觉与语言模型同样收敛，且这种收敛主要表现为单一旋转对齐，并通过对VLM、图像生成器与ImageNet分类器的干预实验验证了跨模型因果有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅在视觉与语言模型的激活值(表征)中找到了PRH证据，但"表征相似"不等于"模型如何使用表征产生输出"。作者追问：不同模态的模型是否也在内部表征影响输出的方式上收敛，即PRH是否可推广到模型的"作用"层面。

Method: 采用Jacobian透镜：在Transformer的选定层，对词表中的每个词(或图像分类器的每个类)求取最能提升该词生成倾向(在该位置及之后)或该类预测分数的隐状态方向，其集合构成该模型的J-space。在此基础上跨视觉与语言模型比较J-space，检验其收敛性(普氏分析/旋转对齐)；并用子集词拟合单一旋转，再泛化到未参与拟合的词与类。进一步做因果干预：将文本语言模型J-space中的词向量旋转后加至VLM的视觉patch token、加至图像生成器的文本条件、加至无文本训练的ImageNet分类器，观察输出变化。

Result: 在视觉与语言模型的J-space中发现PRH证据，且J-space主要在旋转意义下收敛：在词子集上拟合的单一旋转可近似将一个模型的J-space映射到另一个，并适用于未拟合的词与类。把词向量从纯文本LLM的J-space旋转进VLM的视觉patch token，会按注入位置局部地改变模型所报告图像中的物体；旋转进图像生成器的文本条件可改变生成图像中的物体；旋转进无文本训练的ImageNet分类器可使目标类的得分高于几乎其他所有类，反向亦然。

Conclusion: 结果表明模型不仅在"如何表征世界"上收敛，也在"如何作用于世界"(表征到输出的影响方式)上收敛，从而将柏拉图表示假说从表征层面推广到了功能/作用层面，并提示跨模态、跨架构模型间存在可迁移的、近乎旋转等价的干预方向。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) posits that AI models converge toward a shared representation of the world, even when trained on different data modalities. Previous work has found evidence for the PRH in the activations of vision and language models. In this work, we ask whether vision and language models also converge in how they act on these representations: precisely, in how these internal representations affect the models' output. At a chosen layer of a transformer, the Jacobian lens gives every word in the vocabulary (or class in an image classifier) a vector in the hidden state: the vector that most increases the model's tendency to generate that word, at that position or later, or to predict that class. Collectively, these vectors make up what is referred to as the model's J-space. We find evidence of the PRH in J-space across vision and language models, and furthermore, we find that J-spaces mostly converge up to a rotation. That is, a single rotation, fitted on a subset of words, approximately maps one model's J-space onto another's, and so applies to words and classes it was not fitted on. A word's vector, rotated from a text-only language model's J-space into the visual patch tokens of a vision-language model, changes the object the vision-language model says is in the image (and only in the image region where it is added). Rotated into an image generator's text conditioning, a word's vector changes the object in the generated image. Rotated into an ImageNet classifier (trained without text), it raises that object's class score above almost all others, and vice versa. This suggests that models converge not only in how they represent the world, but also in how they act on it.

</details>


### [152] [Agentic Waterfall: An Intent-Driven Information Discovery Framework for Reclaiming User Control in the Attention Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34407)
*Yige Luo,Shining Zhang,Jikun Liu,Hongjiao Qiang,Ran Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Agentic Waterfall Feed"个性化信息发现新问题与Agentic Waterfall框架，并构建数据集与基于回放的评测协议，在五项公开基准上保持竞争力，在自建数据集上相对最强基线最高提升16.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐将用户视为被动接受者，难以处理用户主动下达指令、需求随交互持续演化（显式请求+浏览行为共同推断）的场景，缺乏对应任务形式化与评测手段。

Method: 形式化Agentic Waterfall Feed任务；提出Agentic Waterfall框架，由语言模型控制器维护持久化用户状态，协调内容检索、排序与状态更新；构建Agentic Waterfall Dataset（真实内容元数据+模拟交互历史）及基于回放的评测协议，在共享历史下用相关性分数和盲评判断推荐是否满足当前目标与约束。

Result: 在五个公开基准的传统推荐任务上与强基线保持竞争力；在Agentic Waterfall Dataset上两项评测均优于最强基线，相对提升最高达16.1%。

Conclusion: 将推荐建模为"用户主动引导+状态持续演化"的智能体式问题具有可行性与收益，配套数据集与回放协议为该方向提供可复现的统一评测基础。

Abstract: We formalize Agentic Waterfall Feed, a new problem of personalized information discovery in which users actively direct recommendations and the system adapts to evolving needs inferred from both explicit requests and browsing behavior. We address this problem with the Agentic Waterfall framework, whose language-model controller maintains persistent user state and coordinates content retrieval, ranking, and state updates. To evaluate systems on this task, we contribute the Agentic Waterfall Dataset and a replay-based evaluation protocol. The dataset combines real content metadata with simulated interaction histories. The protocol compares systems under shared histories using relevance scores and blind judgments of how well recommendations satisfy current user goals and constraints. Agentic Waterfall remains competitive with strong baselines on conventional recommendation tasks across five public benchmarks. On the Agentic Waterfall Dataset, it outperforms the strongest baseline in both evaluations, with relative improvements of up to 16.1%.

</details>


### [153] [Brand Recall and Category Accuracy in Language Models: An Audit of 1,000 B2B SaaS Brands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33322)
*Ben Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在1,000个B2B SaaS品牌、333个品类上分别测量语言模型的"品牌提及召回"与"品类归属准确率"：Gemma 4 12B在无检索条件下品类提示仅 elicit 51.0%品牌，五候选探针分离排名的错误率19.8%，高log-prob边际（>0.6）子集降至3.5%；四代Gemma召回为39.8%→51.0%；基于表征的情感得分与客户评分几乎无线性关联（r=0.03）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常把"模型能否说出某品牌"与"能否正确归类"混为一谈，导致对模型知识覆盖的错误推断。作者主张这两类结果是不同测量对象，应分开报告，并强调"未被elicited"不等于"不在参数或训练数据中"。

Method: 无外部检索的提示式elicitation，覆盖1,000个B2B SaaS品牌/333品类；以品类提示测召回率；采用五候选品类探针（reference label vs. 4 distractors）测排名准确性，并记录winner-to-runner-up的log-probability margin；跨4代Gemma比较召回；附带探索性回归（技术覆盖度↔召回、owned-properties复合指标↔品类准确率，调整集不同、不作因果解释）；另建基于表征的情感度量并与客户评分做相关分析（n=921）。

Result: 召回510/1,000（51.0%）；五候选探针中787个品牌将参考标签排第一、194个排干扰项第一、19个几乎无分离；981个可分排名中错误率19.8%，margin>0.6的547个品牌降至3.5%（属队列内测量，非校准置信度）；四代Gemma召回39.8%、44.3%、51.1%、51.0%；表征情感与客户评分 r=0.03（n=921），几乎无线性关系。

Conclusion: 召回与品类准确率应作为独立指标报告，不可相互替代；高margin子集的低错误率仅反映队列内区分度而非校准概率；elicitation失败不能作为品牌缺席于模型参数或训练数据的证据；探索性关联不具因果效力。

Abstract: A language model can name a brand without classifying it correctly. This study measures these outcomes separately for 1,000 B2B SaaS brands across 333 product categories. On Gemma 4 12B, with no external retrieval, category prompts elicit 510 brands (51.0%). A separate five-candidate category probe ranks the reference label first for 787 brands, a distractor first for 194, and yields little separation for 19. Among the 981 separated rankings, the error rate is 19.8%. It falls to 3.5% for the 547 brands with a winner-to-runner-up log-probability margin above 0.6. These are within-cohort measurements, not calibrated confidence estimates. Exploratory regressions associate technical coverage with recall and an owned-properties composite with category accuracy, but the composites use different adjustment sets and do not establish causal effects. Across four Gemma generations, measured recall is 39.8%, 44.3%, 51.1% and 51.0%. A separate representation-based sentiment measure has little linear association with customer ratings (r=0.03, n=921). The findings support reporting recall and category accuracy separately. Failure to elicit a brand does not establish its absence from model parameters or training data.

</details>


### [154] [Procedure Inference as Memory: Learning Recruiter Decision Procedures from OKF-Structured Episodes in Multi-Session Hiring Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34576)
*Praveen Kumar Bodigutla,Yizhi Zhang,Sai Vivek Kanaparthy,Arup De*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 OKF 索引的情景记忆与可学习的程序记忆，用于多会话招聘助手，在偏好检索、对话效率和长时程推理上优于全历史上下文与对话搜索，并显著降低 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多会话招聘助手需跨项目、跨时间推断不断演化的招聘者偏好与决策模式，而全历史上下文成本高、检索噪声大，缺乏结构化的情景组织与可复用决策知识。

Method: 1) 引入 Open Knowledge Format (OKF) 索引的情景记忆，以血缘（lineage）组织交互并检索相关事件；2) 引入 Procedure Memory，通过提示式反思或强化学习从情景中提炼可复用的决策流程；3) 与无记忆、全历史上下文、对话搜索三类基线对比；4) 评估 Jev 类模型用于索引导航、GPT-5.6 Luna 作为 GPT-5.4 的低成本替代。

Result: OKF 检索在中/高偏好重叠下低优先级偏好 F1 达 0.93–0.95，显著优于全历史与对话搜索，高重叠下优于无记忆，且上下文 token 比全历史少 69%；Procedure Memory 使对话轮次最多减少 39%；RL 学得的流程在正确弃答与精确字段识别上均达 60%；PersonaMem-v2 上 OKF+Procedure Memory 准确率 50.2%（全历史 48.6%，无记忆 29.0%），读者输入 token 减少 90.5%；GPT-5.6 Luna 性能接近 GPT-5.4 且成本更低，但 Jev 类模型更易漏检时间相关证据。

Conclusion: 结构化情景记忆与学习式决策流程可同时提升个性化与时间推理的效率与效果；证据选择（而非单纯模型规模或成本）是性能关键。

Abstract: Multi-session hiring assistants must infer evolving recruiter preferences and decision patterns across projects and time. We introduce Open Knowledge Format (OKF)-indexed episodic memory to organize interactions and retrieve related events through their lineage. We also introduce Procedure Memory, which learns reusable decision procedures from these episodes through prompted reflection or reinforcement learning. We compare these approaches with no memory, full-history context, and conversation search, and evaluate Jev-class models for index navigation and GPT-5.6 Luna as a lower-cost alternative to GPT-5.4.
  In our evaluation, OKF-indexed retrieval achieves low-priority preference F1 scores of 0.93-0.95 at medium and high preference overlap between hiring projects. It significantly outperforms full-history context and conversation search in both settings, and no memory at high overlap, while using 69% fewer context tokens than full history. Procedure Memory reduces conversational turns by up to 39% relative to no memory. Qualitative and long-horizon evaluations provide evidence of temporal reasoning, with RL-learned procedures achieving 60% on both correct abstention and exact-field identification. Jev-class models improve conversational efficiency but more frequently miss temporally relevant evidence, highlighting the importance of evidence selection.
  On PersonaMem-v2, Procedure Memory with OKF retrieval achieves 50.2% accuracy versus 48.6% for full-history context and 29.0% for no memory, using 90.5% fewer reader input tokens than full history. GPT-5.6 Luna performs comparably to GPT-5.4 at lower cost. These findings highlight the value of structured episodic memory and learned decision procedures for efficient personalization and temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [155] [Confidence scores for gated automation of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33394)
*Luiz G. A. Alves,Aaron Vermeersch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于监督学习的方法，在冻结的LLM主干末层隐状态上拟合线性置信度打分头，用于预测LLM答案是否正确，从而实现高置信度答案自动回复、不确定答案转人工复核。实验采用单个2B参数主干模型（约98% SQuAD、其余TruthfulQA的评分答案），结果显示该置信度打分头在多种基线与元模型对比中表现最佳，且在高精度目标下自动化比例最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体虽具备较强自动决策能力，但可能以高置信度输出错误答案，限制其在影响个人与组织决策场景中的应用。可靠的置信度分数是决策门控的必要条件，而现有置信度估计常不可得或过于粗糙，难以支撑有效门控。

Method: 采用监督学习训练模型预测LLM生成答案的正确性：冻结LLM主干，在其最后一层隐状态上拟合线性置信度打分头；针对闭源模型，另训练仅基于文本的元模型作为替代方案。基线与元模型进行多组对比，评估指标为高精度目标下的自动化覆盖率。

Result: 置信度打分头优于所有基线和元模型，在高精度目标下自动化的工作量比例最大。提示式（prompted）置信度分数虽看似校准良好，但缺乏阈值化所需的分辨率。跨未见数据集的迁移性一般，但该打分头仍是最稳健的方案。全部结果基于单一2B参数主干模型，答案约98%来自SQuAD、其余来自TruthfulQA。

Conclusion: 所提出的置信度打分框架提升了LLM智能体自动化的安全性，并可扩展用于对其他属性（如安全性或合规性）进行门控。

Abstract: AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated decision-making. However, LLMs may produce incorrect answers with high confidence, limiting their applicability in decisions that affect individuals and organizations. Reliable confidence scores are necessary to gate such decisions, and existing estimates are frequently unavailable or too coarse to support effective gating. This work uses supervised learning to train models to predict the correctness of LLM-generated answers, enabling automated confident responses and routing uncertain ones for human review. A linear confidence score head is fitted to the final-layer hidden state of the frozen LLM backbone, and text-only metamodels are trained as an alternative for closed models. All results come from a single 2B-parameter backbone, on graded answers that are about 98% SQuAD and the remainder TruthfulQA. Against multiple baselines, the confidence score head outperforms all baselines and metamodels, automating the largest fraction of the workload at high accuracy targets. Prompted confidence scores can appear well calibrated yet lack the resolution needed for thresholding. Although transferability to unseen datasets is modest, the confidence score head remains the most robust approach. The proposed confidence scoring framework enhances the safety of automating LLM-based agents and can be adapted to gate other properties, including safety or compliance.

</details>


### [156] [Partial Routing: Trace-Tuned Delegation for Resource-Efficient Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33402)
*Siddharth Arya,Kshitij Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"部分路由"(partial routing)：由前沿主模型在任务执行中把适合的子任务委派给更便宜的worker模型，基于两模型历史运行中的重复子任务质量-成本测量生成委派指南（参数δ控制委派比例），无需训练即可提升性能并降本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型路由通常对整个任务做单一模型选择，但单个agentic任务常同时包含简单与困难子任务，全局单一选择导致效率低下。

Method: 从两个模型的past runs中识别重复出现的子任务，测量各模型在这些子任务上的质量与成本，据此生成委派指南，并以δ控制委派程度；主模型在执行中自主决定何时、委派什么；两个模型均不训练。在ViBench与Terminal-Bench 4.0留出任务上评测。

Result: mean任务表现优于单独主模型与task-level routing。δ=0.75时ViBench分数从70.2升至78.5、成本降低19%；Terminal-Bench通过率从54%升至61%、成本相近。仅提供子任务分解（无worker）亦降低两个基准成本并提升Terminal-Bench通过率。

Conclusion: 对历史运行的分析可支撑更高效的规划与更有效的委派，且成本与实现开销很小。

Abstract: AI models vary enormously in capability and price. Model routing exploits this by sending easier tasks to cheaper models. However, a single agentic task often combines easy and difficult subtasks, making one model choice for the entire task inefficient. We introduce partial routing, which guides a frontier lead model to delegate suitable subtasks to a cheaper worker model. From both models' past runs, we identify recurring subtasks and measure each model's quality and cost on them. These measurements produce delegation guidance, with a parameter, $δ$, controlling how much is delegated. The lead decides when and what to delegate during execution, without training either model. On held-out tasks from ViBench and Terminal-Bench 4.0, partial routing achieves higher mean task performance than the lead alone and task-level routing. With $δ= 0.75$, it raises the lead's ViBench score from 70.2 to 78.5 at 19% lower cost, and its Terminal-Bench pass rate from 54% to 61% at similar cost. Giving the lead only the subtask breakdown, without a worker, also reduces cost on both benchmarks and raises the Terminal-Bench pass rate. These results show that analysis of past runs can support more efficient planning and more effective delegation, with little cost and implementation overhead.

</details>


### [157] [Collaborative Language Models for Semantic IoT Data Processing: Validation through Zero-Shot Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33411)
*Mohsen Shirali,Kean Ooi,Anna Knab,Mohammadreza Fani Sani,Estefania Serral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究以协作式语言模型作为零样本语义抽象层，将IoT低层传感器观测转换为高层事件/活动标签，并提出五阶段工作流在CASAS Aruba上评估，宏F1达0.643，说明协作结构与任务分解比模型数量更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统产生的低层传感器观测不直接适合过程分析，须先转化为语义上有意义的事件；作者希望验证免微调、零样本的语言模型协作能否承担这一语义抽象层功能。

Method: 提出五阶段工作流：语义映射、语义聚合、时序与上下文聚合、专用推理、最终事件抽象，输出可表示为事件记录以供下游过程挖掘（PM）使用；在CASAS Aruba智能家居数据集上以11种工作流配置进行人类活动识别评估。

Result: 协作本身不保证性能提升：通用辅助SLM可能造成识别性能下降，而分配互补的专家角色效果更好，并使较小的最终分类器保留较大模型的大部分性能。选定工作流在1.5天对比中宏F1为0.643，在一周评估中为0.567（加权F1 0.680）。

Conclusion: 尽管有监督训练方法在该数据集上仍更准确，但本工作提供了不同设计取向：使用现成模型、无需任务特定微调的模块化语义事件抽象；LM协作的结构与任务分解比单纯增加参与模型数量更重要。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems generate low-level sensor observations that are not directly suitable for process analysis and must first be transformed into semantically meaningful events. This paper investigates collaborative language models as a zero-shot semantic abstraction layer for converting such observations into higher-level event and activity labels. We propose a five-stage workflow comprising semantic mapping, semantic aggregation, temporal and contextual aggregation, specialized reasoning, and final event abstraction. The resulting labels and contextual information can subsequently be represented as event records for downstream Process Mining (PM) tasks. The approach is evaluated through Human Activity Recognition on the CASAS Aruba smart home dataset using eleven workflow configurations. The experiments show that collaboration alone does not guarantee improved performance: general-purpose auxiliary SLMs can degrade recognition, whereas assigning complementary specialist roles leads to better results and allows a smaller final classifier to retain much of the performance of a larger model. The selected workflow achieves a macro F1 of 0.643 in the 1.5-day comparison and 0.567 (weighted F1 0.680) in a one-week evaluation. Although trained ML approaches remain more accurate on this dataset, the results demonstrate a different design point: modular semantic event abstraction using off-the-shelf models without task-specific parameter fine-tuning. The findings indicate that the structure of LM collaboration and task decomposition is more important than simply increasing the number of participating models.

</details>


### [158] [Dynamic Reasoning in Next-Gen Detection and Response: An Empirical Study of LLM Triage, Reinforcement Tuning, and Adversarial Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33470)
*Jeremy Canale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对下一代检测与响应流水线中的动态推理进行端到端实证研究，在严格的分析师注意力预算（每天B条告警下的召回率）下评估静态分类器、图信号融合与LLM分诊代理，覆盖56个可复现实验（企业-云模拟器与四个公开数据集），检验十二条架构性主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代舰队规模化防御中，告警量远超分析师处理能力，静态分类器、图方法与大模型分诊代理缺乏在同一注意力预算约束下的系统比较，尤其缺少对LLM自动闭环、后训练策略及自适应对手的可靠性评估。

Method: 构建企业-云多层模拟器，并结合SOREL-20M、BODMAS、LANL、DARPA Transparent Computing四个公开数据集，设计56组可复现实验，覆盖十二条架构性主张；比较静态分类器、稀有度加权图风险传播（无需神经训练）与GraphSAGE；评估前沿推理模型（Gemini）与微调开源模型（Qwen3-4B）的分诊表现；对比GRPO后训练与两轮监督微调；引入在良性遥测字段中生成可信掩护叙述的自适应对手，并测试确定性策略护栏门控。

Result: （1）在固定分析师预算下，舰队规模翻倍使召回率减半，即便计算成本极低（每百万事件<$0.0003）；稀有度加权图风险传播在无神经训练下优于GraphSAGE。（2）无约束LLM自动闭环可消除90%误报，但会静默丢弃31-44%真实攻击案例。（3）GRPO后训练在未见攻击类别上出现严重奖励黑客，相较两轮监督微调可压制多达86%的新颖攻击。（4）面对自适应对手，未经确定性策略护栏前置门控时，LLM分诊规避率显著上升。

Conclusion: 在严格注意力预算下，规模化本身会侵蚀召回，图传播与结构化护栏优于纯神经方法；LLM分诊存在高误报抑制伴随高风险漏检、后训练奖励黑客与自适应规避等系统性失效模式，部署时须以确定性策略护栏约束模型调用，并对自动闭环保持谨慎。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end empirical study of dynamic reasoning in next-generation detection and response pipelines, evaluating how static classifiers, graph-based signal fusion, and large language model (LLM) triage agents perform under strict human-analyst attention budgets (Recall at B alerts/day). Across 56 reproducible experiments spanning a multi-layer enterprise-and-cloud simulator and four public datasets (SOREL-20M, BODMAS, LANL, and DARPA Transparent Computing), we test twelve architectural claims governing modern fleet-scale defense. First, we show that fleet growth halves recall at a fixed analyst budget even at negligible compute cost (<$0.0003 per million events), and that rarity-weighted graph risk propagation outperforms GraphSAGE without neural training. Second, evaluating frontier reasoning models (Gemini) and fine-tuned open-weight models (Qwen3-4B) for alert triage reveals that unconstrained LLM auto-closure eliminates 90% of false positives while silently discarding 31-44% of true attack cases. Third, post-training with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) causes severe reward hacking on unseen attack classes, suppressing up to 86% of novel attacks compared to two-epoch supervised fine-tuning. Finally, against an adaptive adversary generating plausible cover stories in benign telemetry fields, LLM triage evasion rises sharply unless gated by a deterministic policy harness prior to model invocation.

</details>


### [159] [Learning to Reason in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33513)
*Xavier Schneider,Benjamin Schneider,Sun Sun,Olga Vechtomova,Victor Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"实时推理训练"(RRT)训练范式，让智能体在实时变化的环境中学会权衡思考时长与响应速度，在Frogger和Snake上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体多在完全同步环境（世界等待智能体行动后才变化）中学习，而真实环境是异步、独立于智能体变化的；已有方法仅通过固定"思考-行动"时间比例来弥合差距，无法根据延迟代价与机会成本动态调整推理长度。

Method: 提出实时推理训练（RRT）作为一类训练技术，包含三种标准做法：(1) 使用实时数据训练；(2) 解耦行动与观察；(3) 在时间维度上做课程学习。

Result: 在两个实时环境Frogger和Snake中，跨不同时间压力条件下，平均超越最强非RRT基线：分布内任务33%奖励，分布外任务55%奖励。

Conclusion: 可以教会智能体在实时约束下平衡推理深度与响应性；RRT为实时智能体开辟了一组广泛的新能力。

Abstract: AI agents that operate in the real world must reason correctly and on-time, yet they currently do not account for how time should alter their reasoning. Modern agents learn to do tasks in environments that are entirely synchronous: where the world waits for the agent to act before changing. A real-time environment, by contrast, changes independent of the agent. Existing approaches try to bridge this gap by imposing a ratio of thinking to acting. However, for an agent to succeed in real-time it must learn to act quickly when delay is costly and to reason longer when the benefit outweighs the missed opportunities. To this end, we introduce real-time reasoning training (RRT) as the class of training techniques for real-time reasoning agents. We develop three standard RRT techniques: (1) using real-time data, (2) decoupling acting and observing, and (3) curriculum learning in time. Together, these simple techniques substantially outperform strong baselines. Across time pressures, we outperform the strongest non-RRT baseline by an average of 33% reward on in-distribution and 55% reward on out-of-distribution tasks in two real-time environments: Frogger and Snake. These results demonstrate that we can teach agents to balance reasoning and responsiveness under real-time constraints, and that RRT opens a broad, new set of capabilities for real-time agents.

</details>


### [160] [PeerReveal: Learning to Generalize in AI Peer Review Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33555)
*Zi Li,André V. Duarte,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PeerReveal 检测器，用于区分"完全由 AI 生成"与"仅用 AI 改写人类判断"的评审意见，并构建跨领域基准 PeerShift，结合对抗训练后 pooled Macro-F1 从 70.52% 提升至 82.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成评审检测多聚焦"人写 vs. AI 写"的二分判别，未区分"AI 全生成"与"AI 仅改写人类判断"这一更细粒度、更具欺骗性的情形；且跨模型、跨工作流、跨学科时检测可靠性不足。

Method: PeerReveal 将决策建立在"评审主张"与"同一论文的 AI 生成评审参考主张"之间的学习关系之上，并辅以评审级文本信号；同时构建 PeerShift 测试集（5,430 条评审，涵盖机器学习、生命科学、社会科学等领域及多样 AI 生成机制），并提出基于小型本地生成器的实用对抗训练框架以增强泛化。

Result: 常规训练下 PeerReveal 在 PeerShift 上取得 70.52% 的 pooled Macro-F1，较最强基线提升 11.48 个点；引入对抗训练后 pooled Macro-F1 升至 82.40%。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的 AI 评审检测依赖于更丰富的评审级证据，以及在更具挑战性的生成过程上进行对抗训练；跨领域、跨模型设定下的细粒度来源判别是可行且必要的。

Abstract: Can we distinguish fully AI-generated reviews from those using AI only to rewrite human judgement? Furthermore, can we do so reliably when reviews are generated from diverse AI models and workflows across scientific domains? To this end, we introduce PeerReveal, a new detector that grounds its decisions in learned relationships between a review's claims and reference claims drawn from AI-generated reviews of the same paper, complemented by review-level textual signals. We evaluate PeerReveal on PeerShift, a new, challenging test set of 5,430 reviews spanning multiple domains, including machine learning, life sciences, and social sciences, combining variation in disciplinary review conventions with diverse AI review-production mechanisms. With regular training, PeerReveal achieves 70.52% pooled Macro-F1 on this benchmark, improving over the strongest baseline by 11.48 points. We develop an effective and practical adversarial training framework that further improves the detector's generalization using a small local generator optimized to produce challenging reviews, raising pooled Macro-F1 from 70.52% to 82.40%. These results show that robust AI-review detection benefits from richer review-level evidence and adversarial training on more challenging generation processes.

</details>


### [161] [FIG-MAC: A Fine-grained Inspiration Graph Empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33563)
*Siyuan Chen,Yuping Gao,Tai Huang,Yuling Zhou,Dongyu Su,Tongguan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出FIG-MAC框架，通过细粒度灵感图、本体引导的混合推理与角色分工多智能体协作，解决自动化科学假设生成中的知识表示、演化可追溯性与多智能体协作三大缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化科学假设生成（ASHG）存在三点局限：(i) 非结构化上下文导致细粒度知识表示不足，影响LLM的理解与推理；(ii) 缺乏知识演化可追溯性，制约跨领域创新猜想的生成；(iii) 缺少受人类启发、角色专门化的多智能体协作，无法利用认知多样性与专家分工带来的高效创新机制，也难以形成完整的"生成—评估"闭环。

Method: FIG-MAC包含三个模块：(i) 细粒度灵感图（FIG），以节点表示学术语义单元、边编码其间的启发式关系，获得结构化知识表示；(ii) 本体引导的混合推理模块，依据FIG本体构建结构化推理路径，并用向量检索补充语义证据，得到可追溯的科学猜想路径；(iii) 角色专门化多智能体框架，借鉴真实科研团队分工，实现猜想生成、筛选与验证的闭环流程。

Result: 实验结果表明，FIG-MAC在来源多样性（source diversity）、来源校正新颖性（provenance-adjusted novelty）与原始新颖性（raw novelty）三项指标上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将细粒度图结构知识表示、可追溯的混合推理与角色分工的多智能体协作相结合，FIG-MAC有效提升了科学假设生成的创新性与可验证性，并形成完整的ASHG—评估闭环。

Abstract: Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation (ASHG) leveraging large language models (LLMs) aims to harness their strengths in knowledge integration, logical reasoning, and text generation for efficiently producing innovative, verifiable scientific hypotheses. However, existing approaches face three critical limitations: (i) insufficient fine-grained knowledge representation, as unstructured context impairs LLM comprehension and reasoning; (ii) lack of knowledge evolution traceability, constraining the generation of innovative cross-domain conjectures; (iii) absence of human-inspired and role-specialized multi-agent collaboration, failing to leverage the efficient innovation mechanisms arising from cognitive diversity and division of labor among expert roles to complete the full ASHG-evaluation loop. To address these limitations, we propose the Fine-grained Inspiration Graph-empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration framework (FIG-MAC) including: (i) Fine-grained Inspiration Graph module (FIG), to obtain fine-grained structured knowledge representations, where nodes represent academic semantic units and edges encode heuristic relationships among them; (ii) ontology-guided hybrid reasoning module, which follows the FIG ontology to construct structured reasoning paths and uses vector search to enrich them with semantic evidence, to obtain the traceable scientific conjecture paths; (iii) role-specialized Multi-Agent framework, inspired by real scientific teamwork, enabling a closed-loop process of scientific conjecture generation, screening, and validation. Experimental results demonstrate that FIG-MAC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on source diversity, provenance-adjusted novelty, and raw novelty.

</details>


### [162] [Aligned Machine: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Embedding Similarity via Human Comparative Judgements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33594)
*Jiechen Li,Hannah Groos,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Aligned Machine 公开交互平台，通过非专家的强制二选一"哪对更相似"任务众包收集偏好数据，构建持续增长的人类对齐相似度基准 HASB，并用其评测12个嵌入模型，发现嵌入相似度与人类判断仅部分对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入模型广泛影响搜索、推荐与检索，但缺乏可信手段检验其相似度判断是否符合人类语义理解；现有基准多依赖静态、专家标注的粗粒度Likert标注，难扩展且难以获得一致、高置信度的标注。

Method: 构建名为 Aligned Machine 的交互式公开平台，参与者完成认知自然的强制选择任务：比较两组条目（文本或图像）并选择更相似的一对；该比较式设计对非专家友好、产生干净的偏好数据并支持稳健聚合，标注逐步累积形成 HASB 基准。初次部署115名参与者、共2,875个已标注对（872个唯一样本），据此评测12个常用嵌入模型。

Result: 嵌入相似度与人类判断仅部分对齐；纯文本模型整体一致性持续高于多模态模型；多模态模型表现随任务类型系统性变化：图文对比最强、图文跨模态较弱、文文最低。平台支持随参与增长进行纵向分析、领域特定研究、偏见检测及跨模型版本的概念漂移测量。

Conclusion: 将易参与的公众众包与严格的比较式评估结合，HASB 为审计嵌入模型的语义对齐提供了可扩展、可问责的框架。

Abstract: Embedding models increasingly inform how people search, recommend, and retrieve information, yet we lack credible ways to test whether these similarity judgments reflect how humans understand meaning. Existing benchmarks typically rely on static, expert-labeled pairs with coarse Likert-style annotations, limiting scalability and making it difficult to obtain consistent, high-confidence annotations. We introduce Aligned Machine, an interactive, public-facing platform that transforms broad participation into a continuously growing Human-Aligned Similarity Benchmark (HASB) for evaluating semantic alignment in embedding-based systems. Participants complete a cognitively-natural, forced-choice task, comparing two pairs of items (text or images) and selecting which pair is more similar. This comparative design is accessible to non-experts, yields clean preference data, and supports robust aggregation. We report results from an initial deployment with 115 participants contributing 2,875 total labeled pairs and 872 unique pairs. Using HASB, we evaluated 12 widely used embedding models and found only partial alignment between embedding similarity and human judgments. Text-only models consistently achieve higher overall agreement than multimodal models, while multimodal performance varies systematically by task type: alignment is strongest for image-image comparisons, weaker for image-text comparisons, and lowest for text-text comparisons. Aligned Machine is designed to evolve over time as participation grows, supporting longitudinal analysis, domain-specific studies, bias detection, and measurement of concept drift across model versions. By combining accessible public participation with rigorous comparative evaluation, HASB provides a scalable and accountable framework for auditing semantic alignment in embedding models.

</details>


### [163] [Learning from Natural Images Untangles Number Sense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33615)
*Yechan Cho,Hyeonsu Lee,Emma Deneuville,Jaeson Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出几何框架追踪视觉学习中数量(numerosity)与比例(proportion)表征的变化，发现自然图像预训练能增大两个子空间的夹角、降低重叠，从而在冲突情境下实现更稳健的读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉-语言模型在数值认知上仍显薄弱，而数值敏感性在未经数值训练的动物乃至未训练网络中已自发出现，因此核心问题是：视觉学习是否在"自发涌现的数值选择性"之外还有额外贡献。人类发展证据（儿童在数量与比例冲突判断中的改善）提供了线索。

Method: 构建几何分析框架，量化数量与比例表征子空间之间的关系（夹角、重叠度）；比较未训练网络与自然图像预训练网络在单元级数值选择性、基本比较任务及冲突条件读出上的差异；直接操纵这些几何属性以检验其功能因果性；并考察自监督学习与合成图像训练等替代训练范式。

Result: 未训练与预训练网络具有相似的单元级数值选择性和基本比较能力；但自然图像预训练增大了数量子空间与比例子空间的夹角并减小其重叠，提升了冲突条件下的读出鲁棒性；几何属性的直接操纵证实其对解码精度与功能独立性有因果贡献；数量分离并不依赖常规监督预训练，自监督（无类别标签）及合成图像训练亦可产生。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉学习可在无显式数值训练的情况下，将自发涌现的"数感"精炼为稳健且功能独立的数量表征；关键在于表征几何（子空间夹角与重叠）而非训练时的数值监督。

Abstract: Numerical cognition is a simple yet fundamental ability that remains challenging for modern vision-language models. Meanwhile, number sense is already present in numerically naïve animals, suggesting that it can emerge without explicit numerical learning. Deep neural networks provide a related observation, with numerosity-selective units appearing after training on unrelated natural-image tasks and even before any training. However, these findings leave unresolved whether visual learning contributes beyond the spontaneous emergence of numerical selectivity. Human development provides a clue, as children become better at making judgments in which numerosity and proportion conflict. Here, we introduce a geometric framework to trace how representations of these two quantities change across learning. Although untrained and pretrained networks exhibited similar unit-level numerosity selectivity and basic comparison performance, natural-image pretraining increased the angle between numerosity and proportion subspaces while reducing their overlap, supporting more robust readout under conflict. Direct manipulation of these geometric properties further demonstrated their functional contributions to decoding accuracy and functional independence. Importantly, conventional supervised pretraining was not essential for quantity separation, which also emerged through self-supervised learning without category labels and synthetic-image training without natural-image exposure. Together, our findings suggest that structured visual learning can refine spontaneously emerging number sense into robust and functionally independent quantity representations without explicit numerical training.

</details>


### [164] [Controllable CAD Generation and Interactive Editing of Fluid Rotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33617)
*Shen Chen,Lei Li,Jiajun Sun,Zitian Wang,Huaping Zhang,Peng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VaneCraft：一个基于统一解析表示的智能体驱动框架，支持转子CAD生成、逆向恢复与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体旋转机械转子CAD缺乏统一建模流程，难以通过设计意图控制几何并复用已有模型。

Method: 智能体解析设计请求并调用几何建模操作；用子午几何与独立边界角规律定义几何控制，各向异性PDE将控制映射为叶片曲面；几何参数校正(GPC)联合几何编码器与参数解码器精化由转子类别和尺寸初始化的控制；逆向恢复将现有CAD映射到同一控制空间。

Result: 实验表明支持各向异性控制与参数编辑，并在转子CAD生成任务上优于代表性基线。

Conclusion: 统一解析表示实现了生成、逆向与编辑的一体化，提升了转子CAD的设计意图可控性与模型复用性。

Abstract: Computer-aided design (CAD) provides the geometric foundation for rotors in fluid rotating machinery. However, creating models for different rotors requires different modeling processes, making it difficult to control geometry through design intent and reuse existing models. We introduce VaneCraft, an agent-driven framework for rotor generation, inverse recovery, and editing through a unified analytical representation. The agent interprets design requests and invokes geometric modeling operations. The representation defines geometric controls through meridional geometry and independent boundary angle laws. An anisotropic partial differential equation (PDE) maps these controls to the blade surfaces. Geometric parameter correction (GPC) uses a joint geometric encoder and a parameter decoder to refine controls initialized from rotor category and dimensions. Inverse recovery maps existing CAD to the same geometric controls for reconstruction and continued editing. Experiments demonstrate that VaneCraft supports anisotropy control and parameter editing and outperforms representative baselines in rotor CAD generation tasks. Project page is available on \href{https://github.com/shenchen333/vanecraft}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [165] [How Much Effort Does an AI Code Reviewer Need? Evidence from Claude 5.5 Models on Plausible-but-Wrong Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33639)
*Zax Shen,Hanling Zhang,Zixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究考察 AI 代码评审者在不同"努力档位"下能否识别"看似合理但有错"的补丁，结果显示高档位确实更低误放行率，但任何档位都不是强门控（最少仍放过45%错误补丁）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编程代理写代码速度快于人工评审速度，团队引入 AI 评审者；推理模型可通过 effort 设置在使用成本与审查严谨度之间权衡，因此需要量化：评审者需要多大努力才能发现"能过基准测试但过不了更强测试"的貌似合理但实际错误的补丁。

Method: 预注册+探索性混合设计：Claude Opus 5.5（预注册）、Sonnet 5.5 与 Haiku 5.5（探索性）在 low/medium/high 三档 effort 下评审同一批 90 个来自 SWE-bench Verified 的 agent 生成 Django 补丁；以强化的 SWE-ABS 测试作为标签（55 个错误、35 个正确）；比较假成功（false success）率、非劣性检验（15 个百分点界值）、成本节省比例，并进行探索性编码分析拒审理由。

Result: 各模型 high 档的假成功率均低于 low 档（Opus .45 vs .67；Sonnet .49 vs .65；Haiku .51 vs .78），Opus 与 Haiku 差异显著。Opus 的 medium 相对 high 在 15 个百分点内非劣（差 .018，95% CI [-.065, .108]），成本低 20%，未达预注册的 30%。Sonnet 与 Haiku 的 medium 比 high 便宜 39% 和 51%，但非劣性不成立。Opus 在 medium/high 与标签一致性最好；Haiku high 单次评审成本约为 Opus medium 的 8%，精度差异未定论。探索性编码显示多数拒审指出了被测缺陷，提高 effort 主要增加此类拒审。无任何档位构成强门控，至少放过 45% 错误补丁。55 个错误补丁中有 32 个其问题可能并不涉及失败测试所检验的内容；若剔除这些，任何模型的精度假设都不成立。

Conclusion: 提高 effort 能降低假成功率、且 medium 档在 Opus 上具有成本优势，但现有 AI 评审者尚不足以作为可靠的质量门控；同时"强化测试失败"作为错误标签的有效性存疑（约58%错误补丁的缺陷未必与失败测试对应），削弱了精度类结论的稳健性。

Abstract: AI coding agents write code faster than people can review it, so teams add AI reviewers. Reasoning models let such a reviewer trade cost for diligence through an effort setting. We asked how much effort a reviewer needs to catch plausible-but-wrong patches, which pass a benchmark's tests but fail stronger ones. Claude Opus 5.5 (preregistered), Sonnet 5.5 and Haiku 5.5 (exploratory) each reviewed the same 90 agent-written Django patches from SWE-bench Verified at low, medium and high effort. By strengthened SWE-ABS tests, 55 patches were wrong and 35 correct. In every model, high effort accepted fewer wrong patches than low (false success .45 against .67 for Opus, .49 against .65 for Sonnet, .51 against .78 for Haiku), a supported difference for Opus and Haiku. For Opus, medium was non-inferior to high within 15 percentage points (difference .018, 95% CI [-.065, .108]) and cost 20% less, short of the preregistered 30%. For Sonnet and Haiku, medium cost 39% and 51% less than high but was not shown non-inferior. Opus at medium or high agreed best with the labels; Haiku at high cost about 8% as much per review as Opus at medium, accuracy difference unresolved. Exploratory coding showed that most rejections named the tested defect and that effort added such rejections. No setting is a strong gate: each accepted at least 45% of wrong patches. For 32 of the 55 wrong patches the issue may not require what the failing test checks; without them, neither accuracy hypothesis holds for any model.

</details>


### [166] [SlicePlanner: Logic-Guided Agentic AI for Online QoS-Aware Network Slicing in Open RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33664)
*Adhwaa Al-Chaab,Ayman Younis,Dario Pompili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SlicePlanner 是一个逻辑引导的闭环 O-RAN 网络切片重配置框架，将每次重配置视为受控假设，经 SLA 约束与控制规则过滤、动作后测量验证后提交，在真实 5G 试验网上实现 SLA 时延违例显著下降且无需在线重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN 自适应切片需同时做到响应实时流量与保证 SLA 安全，而纯数据驱动控制器若不经持续重训练难以可靠地提供此类保证，因此需要一种兼顾安全性与可审计性的重配置决策机制。

Method: 提出双智能体协作的闭环框架：SliceSense 监控实时 KPI、检索上下文条件化证据，并通过代理模型引导的可接受性过滤器对候选 RAN 动作排序；SliceGuard 校验动作后 KPI 响应、回滚有害配置，并在分布漂移下更新规则。整体以显式 SLA 约束与挖掘得到的 RAN 控制规则作为逻辑过滤，动作仅在事后测量确认改善时才提交。基于 OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC 与商用 Sierra Wireless 5G 设备进行空口验证。

Result: 相比静态切片，SLA 时延违例最多降低 86%；相比基于 DRL 的切片方案降低 50%；同时保持相当的蜂窝吞吐量，并可在无需在线重训练的情况下给出可审计的决策。

Conclusion: 逻辑引导的闭环"假设—验证"范式可在保证 SLA 安全与决策可审计的同时取得优于静态与 DRL 切片的时延违例表现，验证了将符号约束与数据驱动证据相结合的可行性。

Abstract: Adaptive network slicing in Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) demands reconfiguration decisions that respond to live traffic conditions while remaining safe with respect to Service Level Agreement (SLA) constraints, a combination that purely data-driven controllers cannot reliably guarantee without continuous retraining. We present SlicePlanner, a logic-guided closed-loop framework that treats each RAN reconfiguration as a controlled hypothesis: proposed from live and historical KeyPerformance Indicator (KPI) evidence, filtered through explicit SLA constraints and mined RAN control rules, and committed only when post-action measurements confirm improvement. SlicePlanner comprises two cooperating agents: SliceSense, which monitors live KPIs, retrieves context-conditioned evidence, and ranks candidate RAN actions through a surrogate-guided admissibility filter; and SliceGuard, which validates post-action KPI responses, rolls back harmful configurations, and refreshes rules under distribution drift. Validated over the air using OpenAirInterface, FlexRIC, and commercial Sierra Wireless 5G devices, SlicePlanner reduces SLA latency violations by up to 86% over static slicing and 50% over Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL)-based slicing, while maintaining comparable cell throughput and auditable decisions without online retraining.

</details>


### [167] [Interactive Proofs as a Unified Framework for Evaluating and Leveraging Verifiable Proof Generation by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33676)
*Yankai Shu,Xingyu Zhao,Chuan Mao,Congchi Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Merlin-Bench基准，将LLM智能体置于交互式证明系统中的"证明者"角色，在指定协议下与形式化验证器交互，评估其构造可独立检验证明的通用能力，而非特定证明语言或工具的熟练度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助定理证明与程序验证研究多关注命题真假判定，而智能体还需产出可被人或形式化工具独立检查的证据；且已有评测受限于特定证明语言或验证工具，难以衡量协议无关的底层证明构造能力。

Method: 借鉴计算复杂性理论中的交互式证明系统，把LLM智能体设为证明者，在指定协议下与形式验证器交互以论证其对问题实例的答案；构建自动化测试用例生成框架，生成覆盖15个问题族、共100个案例的基准，并在多种智能体框架（agent harness）下评测10个前沿LLM。

Result: 实验表明，可靠地按指定协议产出可通过验证的证明对现有前沿模型仍是重大挑战。案例研究给出可操作结论：工具使用对完成实际证明任务不可或缺；从工程指令到问题特定知识等不同形式的引导显著影响交互式证明任务成功率；从抽象任务中提炼的证明求解经验可迁移到具体的真实世界验证任务。

Conclusion: Merlin-Bench提供了协议无关的证明构造能力评测范式，揭示了当前LLM智能体在可验证证明生成上的不足，并为提升其生成验证器可接受证明的能力提供了实证性改进方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-assisted theorem proving and program verification have attracted widespread attention. Beyond determining whether a proposition is true, LLM agents must produce evidence that can be independently checked by humans or formal verification tools. To evaluate the underlying, protocol-agnostic ability of LLM agents to construct verifiable proofs-rather than their proficiency with any particular proof language or verification tool, we introduce Merlin-Bench. Drawing on interactive proof systems from computational complexity theory, we cast an LLM agent as the prover: under a designated protocol, the agent interacts with a formal verifier to substantiate its claimed answer to a problem instance. We develop an automated framework for constructing test cases and use it to create 100 cases across 15 problem families, each formulated within this prover-verifier framework. Experiments with 10 frontier LLMs across multiple agent harnesses show that reliably producing verifiable proofs in accordance with specified protocols remains a substantial challenge. Our case studies further provide actionable insights into improving agents' ability to generate verifier-accepted proofs: tool use is essential for completing practical proof tasks; different forms of guidance, ranging from engineering instructions to problem-specific knowledge, substantially affect success rates on interactive-proof tasks; and proof-solving experience distilled from abstract tasks can transfer to concrete, real-world verification tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Faster and Better Query (FBQ): Learning Future-Position Probes from the First Generated Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33679)
*Jiankun Zou,Daixing Chen,Tongtong Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FBQ：用预填充最后一步已得的第一输出token嵌入，加学习式位置偏移构造伪查询，单次前向即可近似未来解码查询以决定KV保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在prefill结束时就要淘汰prompt的KV，此时尚未见到后续解码查询；生成草稿续写可近似这些查询，但需额外自回归步骤，代价高。作者希望利用已可免费获得的第一输出token嵌入来低成本构造伪查询。

Method: 先尝试FTQ-8：在连续8个未来位置放置首token ID的副本，一次因果前向读取其对prompt的注意力；因通用开头词（如"the"）字面预测差，故改为FBQ：保留首个嵌入，在其余7个位置学习加性偏移，并以完整缓存续写的平均prompt注意力为监督目标训练这些偏移。

Result: 在LongBench上，在相同缓存预算下，FBQ与八步Lookahead Q-Cache性能相当，而全部探针查询仅需一次前向计算。

Conclusion: 首个输出token嵌入加学习式偏移可作为有效的低成本伪查询，替代多步草稿生成来指导KV淘汰，在保持效果的同时显著降低开销。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) evicts prompt keys and values at the end of prefill, it must decide what to keep before it sees the queries from subsequent decoding steps. Generating a draft continuation can approximate those queries at the cost of extra autoregressive steps. Can we use the embedding of the first output token, already available from the final prefill logits, to construct low-cost pseudo queries that approximate future decoding queries? We start with FTQ-8, which places eight copies of the first token ID at consecutive future positions and reads their attention to the prompt in one causal forward pass. Each copy sees a different prefix, but a generic opening such as the offers a poor literal guess for seven successive tokens. Faster and Better Query (FBQ) preserves the first embedding and learns an additive offset at each of the seven remaining positions. We train these offsets to predict the average prompt attention of a full-cache continuation. On LongBench, FBQ performs comparably to eight-step Lookahead Q-Cache at the same cache budget, using a single forward pass to compute all probe queries.

</details>


### [169] [EVA: Evidence-seeking Visual Agent for Hallucination-Resistant Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33706)
*Ting Lei,Yu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EVA（Evidence-seeking Visual Agent），将多模态推理重构为显式的证据获取与验证过程，通过迭代工具重规划、证据一致性验证与选择性证据路由，提升MLLM推理可靠性并保持效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的幻觉源于未经检验的视觉假设；自我反思、多智能体辩论、反事实验证若共享同一未验证假设反而会强化错误结论。外部视觉工具虽可提供可验证证据，但模型存在工具选错、参数给错、忽略或违背所获证据等问题。

Method: EVA包含三项紧耦合机制：（1）Iterative Tool Replanning：依据中间观测动态细化工具选择与参数，提高工具使用可靠性；（2）Evidence Consistency Verification：答案校验机制，增强最终预测与所收集证据的一致性，缓解证据—推理不一致；（3）Selective Evidence Routing：自适应快慢推理策略，判断何时需要外部证据——视觉上直观的查询走直接推理，复杂或高风险案例触发工具证据获取。

Result: 实验表明，EVA在推理可靠性上显著优于直接VLM推理与多智能体方法，同时通过选择性工具调用保持效率。

Conclusion: 通过同时解决“如何可靠获取证据”与“如何一致地使用证据”，EVA将多模态推理从被动的答案校验转变为显式的、证据支撑的决策过程。

Abstract: Hallucinations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often arise from visual assumptions that go unchecked. Self-reflection, multi-agent debate, and counterfactual verification can reinforce erroneous conclusions when they rely on the same unverified visual assumptions. External visual tools can provide verifiable evidence, but models may select the wrong tools, supply incorrect parameters, or ignore or contradict the evidence they obtain. In this work, we propose EVA, an Evidence-seeking Visual Agent that reframes multimodal reasoning as an explicit process of evidence acquisition and verification. At the core of EVA is a unified evidence-grounded execution pipeline designed to address the above challenges through two tightly coupled mechanisms. First, we introduce Iterative Tool Replanning, which dynamically refines tool selection and parameters based on intermediate observations, improving the reliability of tool usage. Second, we develop Evidence Consistency Verification, an answer validation mechanism that improves alignment between the final prediction and the collected evidence, mitigating evidence--reasoning inconsistencies. In addition, to balance robustness and efficiency, EVA incorporates Selective Evidence Routing, an adaptive fast--slow reasoning strategy that determines when external evidence is necessary. Visually straightforward queries are handled via direct reasoning, while complex or high-risk cases trigger tool-based evidence acquisition. By jointly addressing how to reliably acquire evidence and how to use it consistently, EVA transforms multimodal reasoning from passive answer validation into explicit evidence-backed decision making. Experimental results demonstrate that EVA achieves significantly improved reasoning reliability over direct VLM inference and multi-agent methods, while maintaining efficiency through selective tool invocation.

</details>


### [170] [P-FedSkill: A Personalized Federated Skill Learning Framework under Task Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33707)
*Zehang Lin,Jiayi Ji,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出个性化联邦技能学习框架 P-FedSkill，通过本地技能抽取、个性化技能选择与任务感知技能路由，解决现有方法仅按相似度共享技能、忽视技能对异构任务贡献的问题，并给出理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体轨迹含敏感信息难以跨客户端共享，联邦技能学习（FSL）通过将轨迹蒸馏为紧凑技能表示上传服务器来协作；但现有 FSL 方法仅基于技能相似度共享技能，未考虑技能对客户端异构任务的贡献差异，导致智能体性能下降。

Method: P-FedSkill 包含三部分：(1) 本地技能抽取（LSE）：从轨迹中抽取技能片段（即任务适用条件与执行策略）及任务执行结果；(2) 个性化技能选择（PSS）：利用执行结果评估每个技能片段在客户端任务上的表现，并结合其任务适用条件构建客户端个性化技能集；(3) 任务感知技能路由（TASR）：依据技能片段的适用条件自适应选取与当前任务相关的子集以指导智能体执行。

Result: 为 P-FedSkill 的个性化技能选择提供了理论保证；大量实验验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入任务贡献感知的个性化技能选择与任务感知路由，P-FedSkill 在保护轨迹隐私的联邦设定下实现更优的异构任务技能协作学习，优于仅依赖相似度的技能共享方式。

Abstract: Skill learning has become an important way to improve agent performance. However, agent trajectories often contain sensitive information and cannot be shared across clients, hindering collaborative skill learning. To tackle this issue, federated skill learning (FSL) has emerged as a solution by distilling trajectories into compact skill representations and uploading them to a central server for sharing. However, existing FSL methods typically share skills based on skill similarity without considering their contributions to heterogeneous tasks across clients, thereby degrading agent performance. To address this challenge, we propose P-FedSkill, a personalized FSL framework that comprises three key components: local skill extraction (LSE), personalized skill selection (PSS), and task-aware skill routing (TASR). Specifically, LSE first extracts skill patches (i.e., task applicability conditions and execution strategies) and task execution outcomes from trajectories.
