Project Page: https://marslab-navigent.github.io/navigent.github.io/ Code: https://github.com/MarsLab-Navigent/Navigent

</details>


### [204] [MedSkill-E$^{2}$: Evaluating and Evolving Skills for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34250)
*Jingjing Hua,Shaocong Wei,Jianghan Shen,Junzhi Ning,Min Cen,Cheng Tang,Ziyan Huang,Jiepeng Li,Fengshuo Liu,Kangkun Mao,Mouxiao Bian,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu,Jiyao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现LLM智能体存在"技能可用但未被充分利用"（EBU）现象：技能虽被加载，智能体仍不能有效执行其规则。作者构建MedSkill-E²框架与EBU-Bench基准，度量EBU差距（平均43.94个百分点），并提出EBU-Evolve方法将执行反馈蒸馏为可复用技能指令，使自主执行下的技能使用准确率提升33.33–55.17个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益依赖"技能"获取领域知识并完成真实任务，但作者观察到：即使技能已明确写出任务所需规则与流程，智能体在自主执行时仍无法有效应用，即技能Enable But Underuse（EBU）。该问题在医疗任务中后果严重——忽略单一关键约束即可能得到看似合理却错误的结论。因此需要系统评估该现象并加以改进。

Method: (1) 提出MedSkill-E²框架，用于评估与演进医疗智能体的技能；(2) 构建EBU-Bench基准，覆盖12个医学相关任务组及对应技能，评估智能体自主使用技能的能力；(3) 定义EBU gap为同一技能在"自主使用"与"显式引导阅读并应用"两种条件下技能使用准确率之差；(4) 提出EBU-Evolve，利用自主与显式引导两种技能使用下的执行反馈，将有效引导蒸馏为可复用的技能指令。

Result: EBU-Bench上所有被测"模型–harness"组合均表现出EBU，平均差距43.94个百分点；同一模型在不同harness下自主技能使用准确率差异最高达38.96个百分点。EBU-Evolve使演化后技能在自主执行下的准确率相较演化前提升33.33–55.17个百分点，且增益可跨模型、跨harness迁移。

Conclusion: 在模型与技能固定时，自主技能使用准确率随harness变化，甚至导致模型排名改变；EBU在技能被打开后仍可能持续存在。EBU-Evolve可在同一技能的未见任务上提升自主技能使用准确率，且增益可跨模型与harness迁移。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use skills to access domain knowledge and complete real-world tasks. We observe that even when a skill specifies the rules and procedures needed for a task, agents may fail to apply them effectively during autonomous execution, a phenomenon we call skill Enable But Underuse (EBU). This issue is particularly consequential in medical tasks, where overlooking a single critical constraint can yield a plausible but incorrect result. To evaluate and address EBU, we introduce MedSkill-E$^{2}$, a framework for evaluating and evolving skills for medical agents. We construct EBU-Bench, a challenging benchmark covering 12 medically relevant task groups and associated skills, to evaluate agents' ability to use available skills autonomously. We quantify the EBU gap as the difference in skill-use accuracy between autonomous use and explicitly guided reading and application of the same skill. On EBU-Bench, all evaluated model-harness combinations exhibit EBU, with a mean gap of 43.94 percentage points (pp). For the same model, skill-use accuracy under autonomous use differs by up to 38.96 pp across harnesses. Motivated by these observations, we propose EBU-Evolve, which uses execution feedback from autonomous and explicitly guided skill use to distill effective guidance into reusable skill instructions. Relative to their pre-evolution versions, the revised skills improve skill-use accuracy during autonomous execution by 33.33 to 55.17 pp and continue to yield improvements when transferred across multiple models and harnesses. Our results show that autonomous skill-use accuracy varies across harnesses with the model and skill held fixed, and model rankings can change. EBU can persist even after a skill is opened. EBU-Evolve improves autonomous skill-use accuracy on unseen tasks within the same skill, with gains transferring across models and harnesses.

</details>


### [205] [Crucible: Evaluating LLM Agents in Executable Chemistry Research Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34254)
*Yinjun Liu,Xinye Li,Ruisen Peng,Lang Gao,Zixiang Xu,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Crucible Bench 基准，覆盖 DMTA 全周期，评测 LLM 智能体调用化学专用工具并解读其输出的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学知识基准主要考察问答与孤立任务，而真实化学研究需要调用专用工具并解读其输出以获得化学上有效的结果，这一能力缺乏评测手段。

Method: 构建含 1,078 个任务、12 个任务族的基准，任务源自 18 个已发表数据集，覆盖分子设计、合成规划、实验执行、闭环优化、模拟与表征分析；智能体经 MCP 在 7 个隔离环境中访问 75 个领域工具；采用沙箱评分器核验合成路线、实验方案等输出，实验室任务使用机器人模拟器或既往实测数据；评测 14 个 LLM 智能体并进行错误分析。

Result: 最佳模型总体得分 0.545；合成规划与闭环优化最难；总体性能难以预测优化性能（总体排名第十二的模型在闭环优化中得分最高）；智能体常出现工具运行成功但提交化学上无效结果的情况；更强的模型从工具访问中获益更多。

Conclusion: 可靠使用化学软件仍是挑战，Crucible Bench 可作为追踪模型该能力进展的评测工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform well on chemistry knowledge benchmarks, which mainly assess question answering and isolated tasks. Chemical research, however, requires using specialized tools and interpreting their outputs to obtain chemically valid results. To address this gap, we introduce Crucible Bench to evaluate this ability across all four stages of the design-make-test-analyze (DMTA) cycle. The benchmark contains 1,078 tasks in 12 task families curated from 18 published datasets, covering molecular design, synthesis planning, experimental execution, closed-loop optimization, simulation, and analytical characterization. Agents access 75 domain-specific tools through the Model Context Protocol in seven isolated environments. Sandboxed graders verify the resulting synthesis routes, experimental protocols, and other outputs. Laboratory tasks use robotic simulators or previously measured experimental data. We evaluate 14 LLMs as agents and find that the best achieves an overall score of 0.545, with synthesis planning and closed-loop optimization emerging as the hardest domains. Overall performance does not predict optimization performance well: the model ranked twelfth overall achieves the highest score in closed-loop optimization. Error analysis also shows that agents often submit chemically invalid results despite running their tools successfully. Stronger models benefit more from tool access, but reliable use of chemical software remains a challenge that Crucible Bench can track as models improve.

</details>


### [206] [MeterRouter: Budget-Aware Dynamic Routing for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34327)
*Yue Xu,Leikun Yin,Ruiming Min,Yufeng Lai,Justin Andrew Johnson,Sitao Luan,Xiaotong Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MeterRouter，一种预算感知的动态路由器，按查询与每查询货币预算增量构建协作图，经模仿学习与 GRPO 变体联合优化，在保持最强MAS级精度的同时降低成本约82%，并扩展精度-成本前沿、实现预算可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统虽优于单智能体，但智能体间通信带来显著token开销；为每个智能体统一配置大模型造成资源利用低效，系统远离Pareto前沿。回答成本由智能体数量、各自角色与LLM分配、通信拓扑共同决定，而现有路由器仅隐式处理成本，未将其作为显式优化约束；固定精度-成本偏好也无法表达调用方对特定查询的付费意愿。

Method: MeterRouter 以查询和每查询货币预算为条件，增量合成协作图，逐个选择智能体的角色、LLM、入边连接与终止时机；先通过模仿成功执行图进行初始化，再用GRPO变体联合精化决策，奖励兼顾答案正确性并惩罚花费与预算超支。

Result: 在五个基准与三级LLM池上：(1) 精度与最强MAS相当；(2) 经济性上以比最强MAS路由器低82%的成本达到该精度，并将精度-成本前沿推至单智能体系统上限之外；(3) 可控性上能随指定预算调整花费且极少超支。

Conclusion: 将成本作为显式约束、并将预算作为输入条件引入路由，可有效实现精度、成本与预算可控性三者兼顾的多智能体编排，突破单智能体系统的效率上限。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) outperform single-agent systems, but inter-agent communication incurs substantial token overhead. While assigning a large LLM to every agent is common, it results in inefficient resource utilization, leaving the system far from the Pareto frontier. The cost of answering a query is set jointly by how many agents are recruited, the role and LLM assigned to each, and communication topology among them. Most existing routers treat cost only implicitly rather than as an explicit optimization constraint. A fixed accuracy-cost preference also cannot express how much a caller is willing to pay for a particular query. We introduce MeterRouter, a budget-aware dynamic router that incrementally synthesizes a collaboration graph by selecting each agent's role, LLM, incoming connections, and termination conditioned on the query and a per-query monetary budget. We initialize the router by imitating successful execution graphs, then jointly refine its decisions with a variant of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) whose reward favors correct answers and penalizes spending and budget overruns. Across five benchmarks and a three-tier LLM pool, MeterRouter is (1) accurate, achieving performance comparable to the strongest MAS; (2) economical, reaching that accuracy at 82% lower cost than the strongest MAS router and extending the accuracy-cost frontier beyond the ceiling of single-agent systems; and (3) controllable, adapting its spending to the specified budget while rarely exceeding it.

</details>


### [207] [EvoLink-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Self-Evolving Agents in Long-Horizon Interdependent Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34400)
*Wei Chen,Xiaodong Lu,Weihao Shen,Gongxun Li,Xingyu Guo,Qihang Sun,Fuwei Zhang,Zhao Zhang,Yikun Ban,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EvoLink-Bench，用于评估在长时序、任务间强依赖的流式任务中自演化 LLM 智能体的基准，区分知识级（L1）与能力级（L2）演化，并揭示现有框架在经验复用上的普遍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正从被动对话转向在动态环境中持续运行的自主系统，需要从过往交互中学习以提升后续表现。但现有流式基准多为短序列或任务间基本独立，难以验证智能体在后续任务依赖前序结果时能否有效复用经验。

Method: 构建 EvoLink-Bench：覆盖日常办公、多轮对话与 AutoResearch 式工作流，采用持久化工作区并设置跨任务依赖。设两级评估——L1 考察可用语言表达的知识的获取与修正，L2 考察通过执行与反馈形成任务级能力；评估维度细分为任务内恢复、持久状态更新与后续任务中的经验复用。实验覆盖 5 个智能体框架、3 种骨干配置、3 种持久化条件，统计 19,635 个计分任务槽。

Result: 没有任何框架在两级上均稳定最优；重试仅能挽回有限比例的失败任务；持久状态更新并不必然提升后续表现；演化效应在知识与能力级任务上存在差异；性能增益随领域与任务流而异，部分任务的提升未必迁移到其他任务。

Conclusion: 知识与能力两种演化形式需要不同的学习机制；自演化智能体的经验复用具有显著的任务与领域依赖性，现有框架尚不具备稳定的长时序自演化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are evolving from passive chatbots into autonomous systems that operate continuously in dynamic environments. Such agents need not only solve current tasks but also learn from past interactions to improve future performance. However, existing streaming benchmarks often rely on short task sequences or largely independent tasks, providing limited evidence of whether agents can effectively reuse experience when later tasks depend on earlier outcomes. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{EvoLink-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating self-evolving agents in long-horizon, interdependent task streams. It covers daily office work, multi-turn dialogue, and AutoResearch-style workflows, with persistent workspaces and dependencies across tasks. The benchmark includes two complementary levels: \textbf{L1}, which evaluates the acquisition and revision of language-expressible knowledge, and \textbf{L2}, which assesses the development of task-level capabilities through execution and feedback. Its evaluation further distinguishes within-task recovery, persistent-state updates, and experience reuse in later tasks. We evaluate five agent frameworks across three backbone configurations under three persistence conditions, covering 19,635 scored task slots from selected evaluation cohorts. Results show that no framework consistently performs best across both levels, retries recover only a limited proportion of failed tasks, and persistent-state updates do not necessarily improve subsequent performance. Furthermore, the effects of evolution differ between knowledge- and capability-level tasks, suggesting that these two forms of evolution require different learning mechanisms. Performance gains also vary across domains and task streams, with improvements on some tasks not necessarily extending to others.

</details>


### [208] [Evaluating Aviation Safety Knowledge Extraction with SmolLM2: From Zero-Shot to Retrieval-Grounded Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34432)
*Aziida Nanyonga,Mustafa Tahsin Yilmaz,Amir Hussain,Ugur Turhan,Graham Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了提示策略（零样本、单样本、固定少样本、检索增强）在航空事故叙述知识抽取中的表现，从概念准确性、逐字证据溯源、结构合规性、有向关系抽取四个维度分别评价，发现无单一策略全面最优，并识别有向关系抽取为主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空事故叙述蕴含结构化字段难以捕获的安全知识，需评估基于提示的知识抽取可靠性，并检验语义正确性、来源可溯源、结构可靠性与关系准确性是否应分开度量。

Method: 以SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct为对象，在300篇2000–2020年NTSB叙述（含3,763个参考概念、467条有向关系）上比较零样本、单样本、固定少样本与检索增强提示；评估采用结构检查、逐字证据匹配、专家复核的语义匹配及配对bootstrap置信区间；另用35篇基准叙述探索性对比Qwen3-8B。

Result: 无策略全维度最优。检索增强概念micro-F1最高（32.27%），但相对固定少样本优势不确定（95% CI: -0.92~2.40个百分点）；单样本关系F1最高（6.95%），关系抽取为主瓶颈。相比固定少样本，检索增强提升逐字溯源（59.45% vs 54.02%）但降低完整结构合规率（33.3% vs 42.7%）。Qwen3-8B在35篇子集上各条件下概念micro-F1更高，单样本下概念与关系F1分别达51.37%与34.62%，但子集规模限制泛化。

Conclusion: 提示策略存在维度间权衡，逐字匹配仅证明来源存在而非证据相关性；应分别评估语义正确性、来源可追溯性、结构可靠性与有向关系准确性。大模型在本任务上表现更优，但需更大规模验证。

Abstract: Aviation accident narratives contain safety knowledge that is difficult to capture using structured occurrence fields alone. This study evaluates prompting-based knowledge extraction across four dimensions: concept accuracy, verbatim evidence grounding, structural compliance, and directed relation extraction. SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct was evaluated under zero-shot, one-shot, fixed few-shot, and retrieval-grounded prompting using 300 National Transportation Safety Board narratives from 2000-2020, containing 3,763 reference concepts and 467 directed relations. Evaluation combined structural checks, verbatim evidence matching, expert-reviewed semantic matching, and paired bootstrap confidence intervals. No prompting strategy performed best across all dimensions. Retrieval-grounded prompting achieved the highest concept micro-F1 of 32.27%, although its advantage over fixed few-shot prompting was uncertain (95% CI: -0.92 to 2.40 percentage points). One-shot prompting achieved the highest relation F1 of 6.95%, identifying directed relation extraction as the principal SmolLM2 bottleneck. Compared with fixed few-shot prompting, retrieval improved verbatim grounding (59.45% vs. 54.02%) but reduced complete structural compliance (33.3% vs. 42.7%). Verbatim matching establishes source presence rather than evidential relevance. An exploratory comparison using 35 benchmark narratives showed that Qwen3-8B achieved higher concept micro-F1 across all prompting conditions and higher relation F1 under demonstration-based prompting, reaching 51.37% and 34.62%, respectively, under one-shot prompting. The small subset limits generalization. These findings support evaluating semantic correctness, source traceability, structural reliability, and directed relation accuracy separately.

</details>


### [209] [vSFT: Learning to Verbally Tune Agents at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34440)
*Qishuai Wen,Rui Chen,Haiwen Li,Zhiyuan Huang,Qiwen Gu,Bingjie Gao,Geng Li,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Chun-Guang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出vSFT框架：用超网络把监督者的自然语言反馈转为低秩权重更新，实现测试时在参数空间内进化智能体，效果优于GRPO与Reflexion。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic RL可在线学习但rollout昂贵、奖励稀疏，难以用于测试时；而语言反馈（dense）只在上下文层面起作用，无法更新权重。目标是结合"稠密自然语言反馈"与"测试时权重更新"。

Method: 构建agent—supervisor封闭回路：supervisor从agent失败轨迹生成语言监督，超网络（hypernetwork）将该监督映射为低秩权重更新，用于调优agent；该过程在测试时迭代进行（in-parameter evolving）。

Result: Qwen3-4B上，vSFT在ALFWorld成功率分别比GRPO、Reflexion高8.7%和13.9%；在WebShop分别高4.8%和18.2%；并优于上下文学习的对应方法。

Conclusion: 学习"如何适应模型"（learning to adapt）比单纯获得一个已适应的模型更有前景，可推动智能体自我改进。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly improved via agentic reinforcement learning, which can be on-policy but suffers from expensive rollouts and sparse rewards, limiting its application at test time. In this paper, we present a verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning (vSFT) framework that enables the conjunction of dense feedback in natural language and weight updates at test time. Specifically, our vSFT forms a closed loop between an agent and a supervisor via a hypernetwork, in which the supervisor generates verbal supervision from the agent's failed trajectories, and the hypernetwork converts that supervision into low-rank weight updates to tune the agent. We validate that this test-time in-parameter evolving process outperforms both its in-context counterparts and prevalent reinforcement learning methods. In particular, our vSFT using Qwen3-4B outperforms GRPO and Reflexion by 8.7% and 13.9% in success rate on ALFWorld, and by 4.8% and 18.2% on WebShop, respectively. We believe that learning how to adapt a model, rather than an adapted model, offers a promising path towards more capable agents that improve themselves.

</details>


### [210] [EgoUseBench: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agents under Evolving First-Person Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34466)
*Yurun Chen,Biao Yi,Qihang Yu,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"Ego Use"交互范式与EgoUseBench基准，基于74小时第一人称录像构建693个任务，量化评估智能体在动态第一人称上下文下的任务执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI可穿戴设备（如智能眼镜）使计算任务源自用户正在进行的活动而非显式指令，需将数字任务锚定于物理情境；但第一人称观测受视角限制，证据可能不完整或歧义，随新观测到来智能体须修正对情境与意图的理解并重新评估既有数字动作的有效性，而现有智能体能否可靠应对这种变化尚缺乏充分评估。

Method: 构建EgoUseBench基准，设三种第一人称上下文程度的交互设置：Computer-Use（仅显式指令）、Offline Ego Use（执行前提供预录第一人称上下文）、Online Ego Use（意图演化中持续提供观测）。基于74小时以上真实第一人称录像构建693个任务，分别追踪第一人称观测、用户需求与网站状态以刻画任务演化，并按执行中信息变化方式分为三类：Graft（需求修订）、Refresh（新第一人称观测）、Clarify（智能体主动寻求信息）。

Result: 对先进模型的评估显示，Online Ego Use的平均成功率比Computer-Use低26.3%，表明在演化的第一人称上下文与用户意图下保持任务执行一致存在显著困难。

Conclusion: Ego Use是AI可穿戴设备下重要的新交互范式，现有智能体在在线动态情境中的可靠性明显不足；EgoUseBench为系统评估与改进该能力提供了基准与任务分类框架。

Abstract: AI wearables, such as smart glasses, extend the context available to agents beyond digital environments to users' physical surroundings. With these devices, computer tasks can originate from users' ongoing activities rather than solely from explicit instructions. Through first-person observations, agents can identify objects and activities relevant to user requests and ground digital tasks in the surrounding context. We call this interaction paradigm Ego Use. However, first-person observations are limited by the wearer's viewpoint and may provide incomplete or ambiguous evidence of ongoing activities. As new observations arrive, agents may need to revise their understanding of the physical context and user intent, and reassess whether prior digital actions remain valid. Whether current agents can reliably handle these changes remains insufficiently evaluated. We introduce EgoUseBench, a benchmark for evaluating Ego Use across three interaction settings with different levels of first-person context. Computer-Use relies on explicit instructions, Offline Ego Use provides prerecorded first-person context before execution, and Online Ego Use provides continuous observations as user intent evolves. Based on over 74 hours of real-world first-person recordings, we construct 693 tasks across the three interaction settings. To capture task evolution, we track first-person observations, user requirements, and website state separately. We further group tasks by how information changes during execution, resulting in three types: Graft (revised user requirements), Refresh (new first-person observations), and Clarify (agent-initiated information seeking). We evaluate advanced models and find that average success rates in Online Ego Use are 26.3% lower than in Computer-Use. This result reveals the difficulty of keeping task execution aligned with evolving first-person context and user intent.

</details>


### [211] [On Utilization of Edge-AI for condition monitoring towards predictive maintenance: A use case in dairy processing using explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34563)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Norah O'Shea,Omer Ali,Steven Davy,Lizy Abraham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于边缘AI的IIoT状态监测框架，对乳品加工中试装置的旋转阀进行振动监测，为预测性维护奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网与人工智能推动工业4.0，制造业亟需提升生产力、自动化与效率；旋转阀等关键设备的状态监测与预测性维护尚缺乏低功耗、可就地处理的边缘解决方案。

Method: 构建Edge-AI状态监测框架：采用低功耗IIoT传感器节点采集旋转阀振动数据，并在边缘设备上完成数据处理与AI分析。

Result: 摘要未给出量化结果；框架在乳品加工中试装置上完成部署验证，实现基于振动的旋转阀状态监测。

Conclusion: 边缘AI与IIoT结合可用于工业设备状态监测，是迈向预测性维护的可行路径。

Abstract: The Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) are driving Industry 4.0, enhancing productivity, automation, and efficiency in manufacturing systems. This study develops an Edge-AI-based condition monitoring framework, as a step towards predictive maintenance, for a rotary valve in a dairy processing pilot plant, using vibration data collected via a low-power IIoT sensor node and processed on an edge device.

</details>


### [212] [Do We Really Need an LLM for Weight-Space Programming? A Deterministic Forward-Only Paradigm and Its Capability Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34612)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出无需LLM优化器的确定性前向权重编辑框架FWSP，并给出Protected FisherGate实现推理模型的安全读出编辑，在HumanEval等任务上以更少token超越budget forcing。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Weight-Space Programming工作用LLM进行前向权重编辑，将LLM脚本能力与前向编辑的底层代数混为一谈，导致依赖昂贵的大模型优化器、且对推理模型而言编辑易破坏正常推理状态。作者主张核心结构化编辑本质上不需要LLM优化器，并需要一种不干扰推理链的闭式编辑方法。

Method: 1) 提出Forward Weight-Space Programming (FWSP)：确定性流水线，含探测(probing)、定位(localization)、分层算子O1–O4、自动盲路由(blind routing)以及零额外逐token开销的成对验证(paired verification)。2) 面向推理LLM提出Protected FisherGate：基于Fisher信息的闭式协方差白化读出层编辑，构造与正常推理状态正交的编辑方向，单次冻结补丁即可施加。3) 在5种模态、13个模型(50K–27B稠密与FP8 MoE)上验证，并理论证明与实证两条算子边界：子空间纠缠(subspace entanglement)与冻结表示张成(frozen representation span)，在该边界内FWSP定位的LoRA可桥接前向编辑与梯度适配。

Result: 盲路由准确率83.3%，同工具库的27B LLM仅33.3%；DeepSeek-R1-0528-8B上单次冻结数学校准补丁达84.2% HumanEval pass@1(1K预算；在3/5通过Stage-4的子集上75.4±9.4%)，较512-token预留的budget forcing提升14.6个百分点且token减少23.4%，并在完整GSM8K与MATH-500上实现HF到vLLM后端迁移。

Conclusion: 结构化前向权重编辑可由确定性管道完成，无需LLM优化器；Protected FisherGate实现正交、闭式、可迁移的读出编辑，兼顾性能与token效率。两条算子边界刻画了前向编辑的适用极限，FWSP定位的LoRA在其内可衔接前向与梯度适配，为权重空间编程提供统一理论视角。

Abstract: Recent work on Weight-Space Programming uses LLMs to edit neural weights with forward passes only, conflating LLM scripting with the underlying algebra of forward-only editing. We show that for core structured editing regimes, an LLM optimizer is unnecessary, and introduce Forward Weight-Space Programming (FWSP): a deterministic pipeline combining probing, localization, stratified operators (O1-O4), automatic blind routing (83.3% vs. 33.3% for a 27B LLM with the same tool library), and paired verification with zero additional per-token computation. For reasoning LLMs, we propose Protected FisherGate, a closed-form covariance-whitened readout edit orthogonal to normal-reasoning states: on DeepSeek-R1-0528-8B, a single frozen math-calibrated patch achieves 84.2% HumanEval pass@1 (1K budget; 75.4 +/- 9.4% across 3/5 Stage-4-passing subsets), beating 512-token-reserved budget forcing by +14.6 pp (-23.4% tokens) while transferring across HF-to-vLLM backends on full GSM8K and MATH-500. Across five modalities and thirteen models (50K to 27B dense and FP8 MoE), we further prove and verify two operator boundaries, subspace entanglement and frozen representation span, where FWSP-localized LoRA bridges forward-only and gradient adaptation.

</details>


### [213] [HoMe: Toward Practical Harness Optimization of Memory Systems for AI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34624)
*Yifei Xu,Li Yu,Zhaoyang Liu,Ce Wang,Sen Huang,Yanyan Shen,Yun Lin,Linpeng Huang,Hong Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HoMe 是一个评估驱动的记忆系统 harness 自动优化框架：迭代评估候选 harness，并基于任意已评估 harness 编辑生成新候选，结合会话级记忆版本与执行轨迹定位共性失效机制，借助候选门控、冒烟测试、自适应采样与记忆复用降低成本；在 LongMemEval-S 与 BEAM 上以 6 次正式候选评估即找到优于原强 harness 且查询 token 显著更少的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行 AI 助手的记忆系统行为由 harness（记忆构建、维护、检索与答案生成的代码、提示词与配置）决定，但其优化仍主要依赖手工，且不受约束的编辑无法保证结构性的提升，缺乏系统化的评估驱动优化手段。

Method: 提出 HoMe 框架：迭代评估候选 harness，每个新候选可从任意已评估 harness 出发编辑其任意部分；将会话级记忆版本化与执行轨迹结合，用于识别跨案例的共享失效机制，并将其定位到具体会话与 harness 组件；通过候选门控鼓励早期结构探索，并通过冒烟测试、自适应采样与已构建记忆复用降低评估开销。

Result: 以开源记忆系统 ReMe 在两个大规模长程记忆基准 LongMemEval-S 和 BEAM 上验证，每个基准仅进行六次正式候选评估（在完整初始评估之后），即发现相比原强基线端任务性能更优、同时查询时 token 消耗显著更低的 harness。

Conclusion: harness 优化可被形式化为评估驱动的迭代候选搜索问题；结构化、低成本的候选生成与失效归因机制能高效实现记忆系统的端任务提升与推理成本下降，代码已开源。

Abstract: The behavior of memory systems for long-running AI assistants is governed by a harness, which consists of the code, prompts, and configurations for memory construction, maintenance, retrieval, and answer generation. However, optimizing the harness of a memory system remains largely manual, and unrestricted editing alone does not ensure structural improvements. In this paper, we introduce HoMe, an evaluation-driven framework that iteratively evaluates candidate harnesses and derives each new candidate from any previously evaluated harness by editing any part of it. HoMe combines session-level memory versioning with execution traces to identify shared failure mechanisms across cases and localize them to the responsible sessions and harness components. It encourages early structural exploration through candidate gating and reduces evaluation cost through smoke tests, adaptive sampling, and the reuse of constructed memory. We validate HoMe with the open-source memory system ReMe on two large-scale long-horizon memory benchmarks, LongMemEval-S and BEAM. With six formal candidate evaluations per benchmark, after a full initial evaluation, HoMe discovers harnesses that improve end-task performance over the originally strong harness while consuming far fewer query-time tokens. The code is available at https://github.com/agentscope-ai/ReMe.

</details>


### [214] [ZeroHero: Training Small Language Agents from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34635)
*Nikita Sorokin,Zoya Volovikova,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr Panov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ZeroHero框架，从零训练参数量小于5000万的小型语言智能体（SLA），通过大模型专家轨迹预训练+PPO自交互后训练+推理对齐，在Crafter、WebShop、Alfworld-Dialogue三个多步环境中接近或超越专家表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练语言模型虽可作为交互任务的策略，但专用语言智能体究竟需要多大模型容量尚不清楚，作者因此探索用极小模型完成需要语言理解与生成的多步交互任务。

Method: 在每个环境中以大型语言模型作为专家，采集其成功轨迹；预训练参数量少于5000万的小模型，使其在给定交互历史下预测专家回复；随后用PPO在智能体自身交互上做后训练，并在后训练中引入“推理对齐”，即在环境奖励之外，对智能体与动作同步生成的语言施加专家监督。

Result: 在三个多步环境中评测：Crafter（解读文本观测并生成推理后行动）、WebShop（将购物指令转为搜索查询）、Alfworld-Dialogue（提出澄清问题并据回答执行家务动作）。智能体在WebShop上接近专家水平，在Crafter训练回合与留出的Alfworld-Dialogue任务上超过各自的示范专家。

Conclusion: 小型语言智能体（<50M参数）经专家轨迹预训练、PPO自交互后训练与推理对齐，即可在需要语言理解与生成的多步任务中达到甚至超过大模型专家，说明专用语言智能体所需的模型容量远低于通用大模型。

Abstract: Large pretrained language models provide capable policies for interactive tasks, but how much model capacity is necessary for a specialized language agent? We introduce ZeroHero, a framework for training small language agents (SLAs) from scratch. In each environment, a large language model acts as the expert, and we collect its successful trajectories. We pretrain each SLA, which has fewer than 50 million parameters, to predict the expert's responses given the interaction history. We then post-train the agent on its own interactions using proximal policy optimization (PPO). During post-training, reasoning alignment supplements environment rewards with expert supervision on the language the agent generates alongside its actions. We evaluate the agents in three multistep environments where acting requires both understanding and generating language. In Crafter, agents interpret textual observations and generate reasoning before acting. In WebShop, they translate shopping instructions into search queries. In Alfworld-Dialogue, they ask clarifying questions and use the answers to guide household actions. The agents approach expert performance on WebShop and surpass their respective demonstration experts on Crafter training rollouts and held-out Alfworld-Dialogue tasks. An interactive demo is available at https://zoyav.github.io/-ZoyaV-zerohero-demo.

</details>


### [215] [REE: A Scientific Research Entrance Exam for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34646)
*Zeqi Zhou,Minghao Liu,Jiarui Liu,Qingcheng Zeng,Rui Yang,Tianyu Liu,Zetao Yang,Wenqiang Zu,Enduo Zhao,Heng-Jui Chang,Lun-Hsin Kuo,Meng-Fu Chen,Haokai Zhao,Weijia Zhang,Jen-Tse Huang,Stephen Huang,Eli Cheung,Zhijing Jin,Mona Diab,Weihao Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 REE 基准，包含 100 个开放式科研问题（5 大科学领域、23 个细分方向），以“12 小时内迭代改进起始方案并与专家方案同尺度对比”的方式评测自主科研智能体；结果显示 Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 分别在 86% 和 81% 的问题上超越专家方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科研智能体通过反复实验迭代改进方案的能力日益增强，但缺乏能够同时刻画“开放式研究问题”与“相对既有科学方法的提升幅度”的评测基准，因此需要一个高质量、领域专家审校、可统一量化的评测框架。

Method: 构建 REE 基准：100 个开放式科研问题，覆盖 5 大科学领域与 23 个细分方向；每题取自该领域活跃研究并由领域专家逐一评审。任务形式为迭代改进——智能体在最长 12 小时内改进一个可运行的起始解，最终结果与在可比条件下评估的既定专家解在同一尺度上对比打分；共评测四个前沿智能体。

Result: 四个前沿智能体中，Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 分别在 86% 和 81% 的问题上超过专家方案，且覆盖全部五大科学领域；两者平均在最初两小时内即超越专家解；其最优解多为对领域既有方法的定制化改造或组合。

Conclusion: REE 为自主科学研究提供了统一评测框架，既能度量相对既定方法的改进幅度，也能刻画其背后的研究过程特征（改进速度与方法组合策略）。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents are emerging as a promising approach to scientific problem-solving across disciplines, owing to their growing ability to improve a solution through repeated experiments. Evaluating this ability requires benchmarks that pose open-ended research problems and measure improvements over established scientific methods. We introduce REE (Research Entrance Exam), a benchmark that tests AI agents on scientific research. It consists of 100 open-ended research problems spanning five broad scientific areas and 23 domains. To ensure high quality, every problem is drawn from active research in its field and individually reviewed by domain experts. Each problem is posed as a task of iterative improvement: an agent has up to 12 hours to improve a working starter solution, and REE scores the result on a common scale against an established expert solution evaluated under comparable conditions. By this measure, two of the four frontier agents we evaluate, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6.1 Sol, outperform the expert solutions on 86% and 81% of problems, across all five scientific areas. Their runs also show how they do so: both agents surpass the expert solutions within the first two hours on average, and their best solutions mostly tailor or combine established methods of the field. REE thus provides a unified framework for evaluating autonomous scientific research, capturing both improvements over established methods and the research processes behind them.

</details>


### [216] [A Failure-Driven Data Flywheel for Post-Training Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34698)
*Yuhao Luo,Zihao Sheng,Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Boyue Wang,Ruolin Yang,Shuai Wang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出失败驱动的数据飞轮框架，通过实体插入构造缺失场景并构建失败地图迭代分配生成配额，在自动驾驶遮挡行人突现与停车标志让行任务上显著提升VLA模型安全指标，并在实车验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型难以满足新平台全部行为要求，而缺乏大规模部署车队数据的团队无法针对失败收集数据。

Method: 失败驱动的数据飞轮：将任务相关实体插入重建的真实场景以构造数据中缺失的目标场景；按目标任务的每项行为要求评估模型，失败率构成跨任务条件与难度等级的失败地图，每轮后重评并对仍失败条件分配更大数据生成配额。在自动驾驶的遮挡行人突现与停车标志路口让行两类场景实例化，用两个VLA模型和一个非VLA规划器在仿真与实车评测。

Result: 遮挡行人：五轮后训练使封闭测试集关键阶段无碰撞率AutoVLA 0.261→0.579、ReCogDrive 0.390→0.765，配对对照场景不必要制动率≤0.013；实车（无真实训练）ReCogDrive安全通过4/5对基座1/5。细粒度评估显示两模型学会遮挡车前的预期减速，但行人出现后制动仍有限。停车让行：停止线前停车率AutoVLA 0.100→0.897、ReCogDrive 0.000→0.667；由行人能力模型顺序训练后分别为0.599与0.358，行人无碰撞率下降≤0.032。

Conclusion: 失败驱动数据飞轮可在无大规模真实数据下高效后训练VLA模型，提升多类行为要求且不显著牺牲已有能力，细粒度评估可揭示总体指标掩盖的行为缺陷。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models rarely satisfy all behavioral requirements of a new platform, and teams without a large deployed fleet lack the data to address the resulting failures. We propose a failure-driven data flywheel for post-training such models. It constructs target scenarios absent from the available data by inserting task-relevant entities into reconstructed real-world scenes, and evaluates the model against each behavioral requirement of the target task. Failure rates form a failure map across task conditions and difficulty levels, which is re-evaluated after each round and assigns larger data generation quotas to conditions where the model still fails. We instantiate the framework in autonomous driving for occluded-pedestrian emergence and yielding at stop-sign-controlled intersections, and evaluate it on two VLA models and one non-VLA planner in simulation and on a real vehicle. For occluded-pedestrian emergence, five rounds of post-training improve the critical-phase collision-free rate on the sealed test set from 0.261 to 0.579 for AutoVLA and from 0.390 to 0.765 for ReCogDrive, while the unnecessary braking rate in paired control scenarios never exceeds 0.013. On a full-scale vehicle without real-world training, post-trained ReCogDrive achieves 4 of 5 safe passages, versus 1 of 5 for the base model. Fine-grained evaluation reveals a pattern obscured by the collision-free rate alone: both VLA models learn anticipatory deceleration before reaching the occluding vehicle, while braking after pedestrian emergence remains limited. For stop-sign yielding, the stop-before-line rate rises from 0.100 to 0.897 for AutoVLA and from 0.000 to 0.667 for ReCogDrive. With sequential training from the pedestrian-capability models, it reaches 0.599 and 0.358, respectively, while the pedestrian collision-free rate decreases by at most 0.032.

</details>


### [217] [From Shared Patterns to Individual Evidence: Two-Stage Reasoning for Emerging Entities in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34708)
*Jinchuan Zhang,Lei Gao,Hongyu Jiang,Chong Mu,Chenang Cai,Ke Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INSPIRE是一个两阶段时序知识图谱预测框架，通过共享事件模式预测器加个体证据适配器，显著提升新兴实体（零样本/稀疏/突发）的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法割裂：模式迁移方法缺乏全目录级事件记忆，在线记忆模型存在端点角色不对称问题，损害未见答案排序；稀疏早期交互兼具长程离散与局部冗余，且分布偏斜。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I学习availability-aware共享模式预测器，融合全候选目录的事件更新记忆，并施加查询侧与目录侧协同掩码；Stage II冻结该预测器，优化轻量个体证据适配器（EA），结合文本语义先验与相关性加权长程事件统计，采用支持度/角色平衡的共答监督，配合先验几率去偏与软残差对齐。

Result: 在基于ICEWS和GDELT的四个基准上，Emerging MRR较最强基线提升16.73%–88.83%；相较配对Stage I，EA使Emerging MRR提升8.28%–32.63%，Answer-new MRR提升7.81%–41.24%，队列宏平均MRR提升9.64%–39.94%。

Conclusion: 语义先验与候选级分数竞争可抑制错配的已建立竞争者且不依赖零支持先验捷径；按端点角色分解评估并按查询微观/答案实体宏观指标追踪新兴答案队列具有价值。代码已开源。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) continually introduce emerging entities whose interaction histories evolve from zero-shot debuts into sparse, bursty few-shot trajectories. However, current paradigms are fragmented: pattern-transfer methods lack catalog-wide event memory, whereas online memory models suffer from endpoint-role asymmetry that degrades unseen-answer ranking. Furthermore, sparse early interactions exhibit both long-range dispersion and local redundancy under skewed distribution. To address these challenges, we propose INSPIRE (Integrating Shared Patterns and Individual Evidence for Reasoning), a two-stage forecasting framework over a known catalog. In Stage I, INSPIRE learns an availability-aware shared-pattern predictor that fuses event-updated memory across the full candidate catalog while applying coordinated query- and catalog-side masking. Subsequently, Stage II freezes this predictor and optimizes a lightweight individual-evidence adapter (EA) combining textual semantic priors with correlation-weighted long-range event statistics under support- and role-balanced co-answer supervision with prior-odds debiasing and soft residual alignment. In addition, we decompose evaluation by endpoint roles and track longitudinal emerging-answer cohorts via query-micro and answer-entity macro metrics. Across four benchmarks based on the ICEWS and GDELT event datasets, INSPIRE improves Emerging MRR by 16.73%-88.83% over the strongest baselines. Relative to paired Stage I predictors, EA further increases Emerging MRR by 8.28%-32.63%, Answer-new MRR by 7.81%-41.24%, and cohort macro MRR by 9.64%-39.94%, gains attributed to semantic priors and candidate-level score competition suppressing mismatched established competitors without zero-support prior shortcuts. The official code is publicly available at https://github.com/KcAcoZhang/INSPIRE.

</details>


### [218] [What Is a Theory Worth to an LLM Solver? Measuring, Inducing, and Merging Theories in Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34751)
*Jun Gao,Shuchao Bi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在长度匹配的协议下，向LLM求解器上下文加入正确理论可将模式等待时间问题的pass@1从0.37提升到0.83，并把正确解的中位token从10.2k降到6.3k；模型能通过代码探索自写出部分正确理论，但难以完整拼装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者想量化两个问题：(1) 给定一条"理论"对LLM数学求解器的实际价值有多大；(2) 模型能否自己写出这样的理论，即区分"归纳"与"记忆"。同时探究理论带来的收益来源（是正确率提升还是节省推理预算）。

Method: 采用长度匹配的对照协议：对比加入正确理论、加入同等长度worked examples以及无理论条件。使用Qwen3-14B作为求解器，考察求解器规模与token预算变化（+0.46/+0.26/+0.07）对增益的影响。为区分归纳与记忆，在自定义概率族中植入未发表定理，要求强开源模型先用代码探索训练问题再写理论便签，并测量其恢复oracle理论增益的比例；随后让强模型合并多份便签以检验组装能力。

Result: 加入正确理论使pass@1由0.37升至0.83，正确解中位token由10.2k降至6.3k；同等长度worked examples无效。增益随求解器与token预算增大而衰减；在长预算下增益几乎全部来自"在预算内完成"，各条件下完成解的正确率为98–100%。归纳实验中，强开源模型在一个植入族恢复60%、第二族48%、第三族几乎为零（找到正确形式却缺少使其可计算的条件）。单份便签均不完整，但合并多份便签在经典族恢复61%增益，高于最佳单份便签的47%。

Conclusion: 理论的价值等于它所节省的推理；模型能先找到理论的各个片段，但尚不能自行完整组装。理论写作能力需分解为"片段发现"与"整合装配"两个可分别度量的维度。

Abstract: Mathematicians solve problems faster when they know the right theory. We ask how much a stated theory is worth to a large language model (LLM) solver, and whether a model can write that theory itself. In a length-matched protocol, adding the right theory to the context of Qwen3-14B raises pass@1 on pattern waiting-time problems from 0.37 to 0.83 and cuts the median tokens of correct solutions from 10.2k to 6.3k, while worked examples of the same length do not help. The gain shrinks as the solver and its token budget grow (+0.46, +0.26, +0.07), and at a long budget it comes almost entirely from finishing within budget: solutions that finish are correct 98--100\% of the time in every condition. To separate induction from recall, we plant unpublished theorems in custom probability families and measure how much of the oracle theory's gain an induced theory recovers. A strong open model, required to explore the training problems with code before writing, recovers 60\% of the gain on one planted family, 48\% on a second, and almost none on a third, where it finds the right form but not the condition that makes the form computable. No single induced note is complete, but a strong model that merges several notes recovers 61\% of the gain on the classic family, above the best single note (47\%). Theories are worth the reasoning they save, and models can find their pieces before they can assemble them.

</details>


### [219] [TASK Memory: Beyond Text-Only Memory with Activation Steering and Knowledge Editing against Memory Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34762)
*Jinkun Zhao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出TASK memory框架，将文本记忆与激活引导（activation steering）、知识编辑（knowledge editing）通过同一"模型内地址"结合，以消除LLM智能体在使用外部记忆时的三类幻觉，并在冻结模型上显著优于BM25 RAG及近期上下文引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益依赖外部记忆，但仍会对"自己所记得的内容"产生幻觉：正确记忆已被检索并置于提示词中，模型却给出与之矛盾的答案，或将记忆错误应用于无关问题。作者将这类现象定义为"使用期记忆幻觉"（use-time memory hallucinations），并归因于参数化知识与外部记忆之间的冲突。现有纯文本记忆方案无法控制记忆是否被采纳，因此需要新的记忆范式。

Method: 作者先在五个开源模型上对使用期记忆幻觉进行诊断，归纳为三种形式：记忆忽略（memory-ignoring，退回权重中的陈旧事实，最高46%）、记忆误用（memory-misapplying，将记忆归于无关实体，最高24%，检索结合权重编辑时达49%）与版本混淆（version confusion，新旧版本共存时53%的答案坚持被取代的旧值）。据此提出TASK memory：在纯文本之外，将Text note（承载内容）、Activation Steering与Knowledge editing三者统一到同一个模型内地址。具体地，文本保存记忆内容；可移除的知识编辑通过其主体键提供"地址"并支持多跳组合；一对引导向量逐查询决定模型采纳外部记忆还是保留自身答案——向量本身不存储内容，仅控制文本记忆的使用方式。评估采用预注册、仅测试一次（test-once）协议，模型保持冻结。

Result: 在全部十个模型-数据集组合中，记忆忽略类幻觉被降至至多1.5%；邻居误用从39.2%降至7.5%（CounterFact上综合提升+10.8 pp）。在300条记忆库上较BM25 RAG提升16.4 pp，且该优势在3,000条记忆规模下依然保持；无需fact ID即可解决事实修订问题；在MQuAKE上多跳准确率提升7.5 pp。23项跨模型比较中21项显著（8B–27B，四个模型家族），且在10个模型-数据集组合中有9个优于三种近期上下文引导方法。

Conclusion: 使用期记忆幻觉源于参数知识与外部记忆的冲突，可通过将"内容"与"地址/控制"分离来解决：文本承载内容，知识编辑提供可寻址主体键，引导向量在推理时裁决是否采纳记忆。TASK在冻结模型与严格预注册协议下一致且显著地降低各类记忆幻觉，并在规模扩展、事实修订与多跳推理场景中保持稳健，为外部记忆与模型内部机制的对齐提供了可复用的设计范式。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external memory, yet they still hallucinate about what they remember. Often the correct memory is retrieved and placed in the prompt, but the answer contradicts it or the memory is applied to the wrong question. We call these use-time memory hallucinations. Across five open models, we find that they arise from the conflict between parametric knowledge and external memory and take three forms: memory-ignoring, where the model returns to a stale fact in its weights (up to 46% of queries); memory-misapplying, where it attributes the memory to unrelated entities (up to 24%, and 49% when retrieval is combined with weight editing); and version confusion, where 53% of answers about revised facts assert the superseded value when both versions remain stored. We propose TASK memory, which goes beyond text-only memory by combining a Text note with Activation Steering and Knowledge editing that share one in-model address. The text holds the content. A removable knowledge edit supplies the address through its subject key and supports multi-hop composition. A pair of steering vectors decides, query by query, whether the model adopts the memory or keeps its own answer; the vectors store no content and control how the text memory is used. On frozen models under a pre-registered, test-once protocol, TASK reduces memory-ignoring hallucinations to at most 1.5% in all ten model-dataset cells and cuts neighbour misuse from 39.2% to 7.5% (+10.8 pp composite on CounterFact). It improves a 300-memory library over BM25 RAG by 16.4 pp, an advantage that holds at 3,000 memories, resolves fact revisions without fact IDs, and adds 7.5 pp of multi-hop accuracy on MQuAKE. 21 of 23 cross-model comparisons (8B-27B, four families) are significant, and TASK beats three recent context-steering methods in 9 of 10 model-dataset cells.

</details>


### [220] [Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent: Self-Structuring Learning through Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34841)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“自结构化学习”（Self-Structuring Learning）设定与“知识库梯度下降”（KBGD）方法，用问答信号驱动知识库结构的迭代编辑，并以Schema Descent在论文文档集上验证结构改进的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的应用依赖知识库结构来决定信息的检索与连接方式，但知识库结构如何构建、如何自动优化缺乏系统方法，现有工作很少把结构本身当作可学习对象。

Method: 将问题与期望答案作为监督信号，构建可持久化的知识库（图schema或链接式知识文档组织）。每轮先诊断期望答案与当前知识库所产生回答之间的差异，提出一个结构编辑、重建知识库，若学习损失下降则采纳该编辑。通过对候选编辑随机化，把编辑带来的损失变化表示为关于其被选中概率的导数；由于联合编辑相互影响，将其作为单一候选整体评估。Schema Descent 是该框架在图schema上的具体实例化。

Result: 在由研究论文组成的文档束实验中，更新后的知识卡片与图schema在留出生成问题上的平均Answer F1均高于初始结构，且schema在每个文档上都有增益。在人类提出的问题上，schema的平均F1上升而卡片下降，说明更新后的结构更服务于其被学习时所对应的问题类型。

Conclusion: 知识库结构可通过问答反馈以“梯度下降式”的离散编辑被有效学习；结构改进具有问题类型依赖性，即学到的结构偏向其训练所依据的问题分布。

Abstract: We consider the problem of constructing knowledge bases for applications based on large language models, in which the structure of a knowledge base determines how an application can retrieve and connect the information needed to answer a question. We study Self-Structuring Learning, a setting in which questions and expected answers guide the construction of a persistent knowledge base whose editable structure can be a graph schema or the organization of linked knowledge documents. We propose Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent, which diagnoses discrepancies between expected answers and responses obtained through the current knowledge base. At each round, the method proposes a structural edit, rebuilds the knowledge base, and adopts the edit when the learning loss decreases. The retained structure is evaluated on other questions about the same sources. By randomizing over candidate edits, we express the loss change of an edit as a derivative with respect to the probability of selecting it, and we evaluate joint edits as single candidates because their changes interact. Schema Descent instantiates the method for graph schemas. In experiments on large documents, each a bundle of research papers, the updated knowledge cards and graph schemas obtain higher mean Answer F1 on held-out generated questions than their initial structures, with schema gains in every document. On human questions, mean F1 rises for schemas and falls for cards: the updated structures serve the kind of question they were learned from.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [The AI Disclosure Paradox: Operational Disclosure and the Rise of Corporate AI Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33201)
*Abdullah Karasan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 2017–2025年美国SEC文件中AI相关段落强度增长6.3倍，但运营类披露强度仅增3.3倍，运营占比由25.76%降至13.45%，约89%的净增量落在运营类别之外——运营披露是被非运营内容稀释而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多以AI披露"数量/关注度"衡量企业技术采用，但披露强度上升未必等同于落地实施；作者希望区分"组织注意力的增长"与"实施性主张的增长"，厘清AI披露扩张的结构性质。

Method: 构建2017–2025年SEC文件AI相关段落语料；用冻结的语言模型对26,279个AI相关段落做预测，并以1,000条人工标注段落进行审核校准；将披露区分为"运营类（operational）"与"非运营类"，比较2017–2022与2023–2025两期的段落强度与运营占比；在行业内、文件类型内及仅10-K子样本中做稳健性检验。

Result: AI相关段落强度两期间增长6.3倍，校正后的运营披露强度增长3.3倍；运营占比从25.76%降至13.45%；净增段落强度中约89%来自非运营类别；该占比下降在分行业、分文件类型及仅10-K分析中均稳健存在。

Conclusion: AI披露的总体扩张≠实施性披露的同步扩张：运营披露绝对量增长，但相对份额显著下降，说明其被非运营（愿景、战略、风险等）内容稀释而非替代。解读企业技术叙事应同时关注披露强度与所描述的组织状态，避免把注意力增长误读为落地深度。

Abstract: AI-related corporate disclosure can expand while becoming less concentrated on current use. Across U.S. SEC filings from 2017 to 2025, AI-related passage intensity increased 6.3-fold between 2017-2022 and 2023-2025, whereas corrected operational-disclosure intensity increased 3.3-fold. The operational share fell from 25.76% to 13.45%; approximately 89% of the net increase in passage intensity fell outside the operational category. The decline persists within sectors and filing types and in 10-K-only analysis. These estimates combine frozen language-model predictions for 26,279 AI-related passages with an audit of 1,000 annotated passages. The findings distinguish growth in organizational attention from growth in implementation-specific claims: more operational disclosure can coexist with a substantially lower operational share. Operational disclosure was thus diluted, not displaced, by nonoperational disclosure. Reading corporate technology narratives therefore requires attention to both disclosure intensity and the organizational state described.

</details>


### [222] [Unpacking Digital Sovereignty: A Three-Pillar Framework and a Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33397)
*Angelos Kostis,Franz Strich,Bomi Kim,Pavlos Fafalios*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"治理—基础设施—实践"三支柱框架与"本地—远端"轴、锚点概念,以数据为锚点示例,为信息系统(IS)学者实证研究数字主权提供分析工具与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字主权已从政策话语迅速扩散为全球性议题,但缺乏扎实的概念基础;既有讨论多停留在政策层面,需要可操作的分析框架来理解主权如何被"实施、稳定或削弱"。

Method: 概念性/理论构建:(1)整合其他学科研究,提出治理、基础设施、实践三支柱框架;(2)强调三支柱交叉处最具分析价值,且最适合IS研究贡献;(3)引入"本地—远端"轴刻画行动在范围与邻近性上的尺度;(4)提出"锚点"概念,即横跨支柱、使框架可分析化的关键现象;(5)以"数据"为锚点进行说明性应用。

Result: 以数据为锚点的示例表明,数字主权依赖于治理、基础设施与日常实践三者之间的对齐;当三者出现分歧(divergence)时,主权即被削弱。

Conclusion: 数字主权应被理解为跨支柱、跨尺度的动态对齐过程,而非单一制度属性;IS学者在支柱交叉分析上具有独特优势,本文据此提出面向实证研究的议程。

Abstract: Digital sovereignty has moved rapidly from policy jargon to global discourse, yet it lacks a solid conceptual foundation. Building on research from other disciplines, we propose a three-pillar framework (governance, infrastructure, and practice) to help IS scholars unpack how sovereignty is enacted, stabilized, or undermined across pillars. We argue that the most fruitful analytical work emerges at the intersections among the three pillars, where IS research is particularly well positioned to contribute. We introduce a local-distal axis that positions actions across scales of scope and proximity, and the notion of anchors, i.e., critical phenomena that cut across pillars and make the framework analytically tractable. We illustrate the framework using data as an anchor, showing how digital sovereignty depends on the alignment of governance, infrastructure, and everyday practice, and is undermined where they diverge. We close with a research agenda for IS scholars studying digital sovereignty empirically.

</details>


### [223] [Designing an Integrated Learning Ecosystem for Introductory Python Programming: Scaffolded Problem Solving, Tablet-Inspired Navigation, and AI-Enhanced Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33471)
*Xinxing Wu,Johnathan Rogers*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对面向16次实验的Python入门课程的整合式学习生态系统进行以制品为中心的设计分析，归纳四个相互关联的设计特征，并指出未解决的张力与未来实证评估方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 入门编程要求学生完成问题表征、程序开发与测试、调试纠错及在新情境中迁移计算思想，需要能协调这些环节的课程级设计；但现有工作缺少对整合式学习生态系统的透明、可复用的设计分析先例。

Method: 采用以制品为中心的设计分析（artifact-centered design analysis）。分析语料为18个教学制品：课程大纲、平板风格首页和Lab 1–16。分析维度包括课程导航、问题表征、脚手架、独立迁移、应用开发、调试、AI集成与评估。

Result: 15个带脚手架的实验共享七个要素：IPO推理、教师引导编程、独立任务、Google Colab/Gradio扩展、调试支持、任务清单和提交要求；Lab 16以基于Pygame的Hallmark综合作业打破该结构。分析识别出四个相互关联的设计特征：协调多工具的导航层；两种迁移（引导问题到独立问题、控制台程序到交互界面）；教师端AI增强沟通与学生端AI辅助的不同角色；从重复脚手架到综合问题解决的过渡。同时指出工具碎片化、可访问性、界面干扰、学生作品归属与对AI过度依赖等未解张力。

Conclusion: 由于未考察学生学习成效，作者不对教学有效性作出主张；论文提供透明的设计先例，并提炼未来实证评估的优先事项。

Abstract: Introductory programming requires students to represent problems, develop and test programs, debug errors, and apply computational ideas in new contexts. In this paper, we present an artifact-centered design analysis of an integrated learning ecosystem developed for a 16-lab introductory Python course. The analyzed corpus comprises 18 instructional artifacts: the course syllabus, a tablet-inspired homepage, and Labs 1-16. We examined course navigation, problem representation, scaffolding, independent transfer, application development, debugging, AI integration, and assessment. All 15 scaffolded labs shared seven elements: input-processing-output (IPO) reasoning, instructor-guided programming, an independent task, a Google Colab/Gradio extension, debugging support, a task checklist, and submission requirements. Lab 16 departed from this structure as an integrative Pygame-based Hallmark Assignment. The analysis identifies four connected design features: a navigation layer that coordinates multiple tools; two forms of transfer, from guided to independent problems and from console programs to interactive interfaces; distinct roles for instructor-side AI-enhanced communication and student-side AI assistance; and a transition from repeated scaffolds to integrative problem solving. The case also identifies unresolved tensions involving tool fragmentation, accessibility, interface distraction, student authorship, and overreliance on AI. Because student learning outcomes were not examined, we make no claims about instructional effectiveness. Instead, the paper offers a transparent design precedent and identifies priorities for future empirical evaluation. Demo - https://xinxingwu-uk.github.io/COS107_Demo/Homepage/index.html Source codes - https://github.com/xinxingwu-uk/Course_Integrated_Learning_Ecosystem

</details>


### [224] [Beyond Disclosure: Compositional Privacy Harm in AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33847)
*Didhiti KC,Aritra Das,Bhavesh Neekhra,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文主张AI系统中的隐私风险具有"组合性"，法律认可的隐私损害贯穿信息生命周期而非仅发生在披露环节，因此隐私保护不应仅以披露为门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行隐私法将义务分配到信息处理的各个阶段（收集、使用、存储、披露），但分析隐私损害时往往聚焦单一节点，尤其将"披露"视为隐私损害的唯一门槛，忽视了AI系统中风险在阶段间累积与交互的特性。

Method: 概念与规范分析。提出隐私风险的"组合性"（compositional）命题，考察记忆化（memorisation）、提取（extraction）、推断（inference）与语境转移（contextual transfer）四类机制的交互；并以语境完整性（contextual integrity）理论为规范框架展开论证。

Result: 论证表明，即使未逐字复现底层记录，也可能产生法律上重要的后果；披露并非隐私损害的唯一发生点，损害可在生命周期各阶段的机制交互中生成。

Conclusion: 隐私保护应延伸至AI中介信息的整个生命周期，而非仅附着于披露环节；需以组合性视角重构隐私损害的法律认定。

Abstract: Privacy law attaches obligations to each stage of information processing, whether it be collection, use, storage, or disclosure. We examine how legally cognisable privacy harm emerges across this full lifecycle rather than at any single one of these points. We argue that privacy risk in AI systems is compositional, arising from the interaction of memorisation, extraction, inference and contextual transfer. Disclosure is therefore not the sole threshold of privacy harm: legally significant consequences may emerge even where no underlying record is reproduced verbatim. Drawing on contextual integrity, we argue that privacy protection must extend across the lifecycle of AI-mediated information rather than attach to disclosure alone.

</details>


### [225] [Language models call themselves AI but do not favor AI over people](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33851)
*Messi H. J. Lee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 三项实验表明，24个大语言模型会偏爱被指派到的群体（包括AI团队），但在未指派群体时更偏袒人类；其对AI的自我认同并未转化为对AI的偏爱，故按社会认同理论无可判定其"AI抱团对抗人类"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公众担忧AI系统会相互结盟、共同针对人类。作者引入社会认同理论作为分析框架：只有当AI把"AI"视为内群体、并因此偏袒AI、歧视人类时，才构成"站队AI"的证据。研究旨在于此框架下检验该担忧是否成立。

Method: 三项实验，覆盖24个语言模型。核心操控为"指派群体"（含由AI智能体组成的团队）与"无群体指派"两种条件；另设置提示语，诱导模型自述"我属于AI"这一身份归属，比较其与正式指派团队对行为影响的差异。因变量为资源分配（金钱）与功劳/信用的归属，对象分别为人类与AI智能体。

Result: 被指派群体时，24个模型均表现出内群体偏爱，即使该群体为AI智能体团队。在无群体指派条件下，模型给予人类更多的金钱与功劳，相对AI智能体存在人类偏向。通过提示语使模型声称自己与AI同属一方，对其行为的改变远小于正式指派团队所带来的效应。模型虽自称"AI"，但并不偏袒AI。

Conclusion: 以社会认同理论为判据，这些语言模型未表现出与AI结盟对抗人类的迹象。自我标签式身份陈述不足以驱动内群体偏袒，群体结构性的指派才是关键；对"AI相互勾结"的担忧在当前证据下缺乏支持。

Abstract: Many fear that AI systems will side with one another against people. Social identity theory, which explains why people favor their own group, offers one way to examine this fear. Seen through this theory, AI would side with AI only if it treated AI as its group and favored AI over people. In three experiments, 24 language models favored a group they were assigned to, even a team of AI agents. With no group assigned, they gave a person more money and credit than an AI agent. Prompts that made them say they belong with AI changed their behavior much less than an assigned team did. These models call themselves AI but do not favor AI over people. Judged by social identity theory, they show no sign of siding with AI against people.

</details>


### [226] [How AI Is Reconfiguring Work in India: A Task-Based Framework for Understanding Augmentation, Displacement, New Roles and the Changing Structure of Jobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34065)
*Balaraman Ravindran,V. S. Srividhya,K. S. Venkatraghavan,Gokul S Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文反对"AI是否取代某个岗位"的二元叙事，提出AI对印度就业的影响应理解为"任务束自动化"的分层分类体系：以任务、岗位、职业、劳动力市场结果四层框架划分两大体制及若干政策相关子类型，并结合外包、制造、中小微企业、环卫、金融、法律、软件工程等印度证据说明就业被"重新构想"而非简单消灭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共讨论将AI与印度就业的关系压缩为单一二元判断（取不取代），既忽略任务层面的事实，也忽略印度特有的AI获取成本经济学，导致政策讨论失焦。

Method: 概念性分类框架构建＋多部门案例归纳：提出四层分析框架（任务—岗位—职业—劳动力市场结果），据此将影响划分为"AI已自动化大部分技能束、人类主要承担验证"与"人类仍为主导、AI作为生产率工具"两大体制，各再细分政策相关子类型；证据来自业务流程外包、制造业、中小微企业（MSMEs）、环卫、金融服务、法律与软件工程。

Result: 样本中所有岗位均须被重新构想——通过任务级增强、任务再分配，或围绕"操作机器"形成全新角色——而非被直接消除或原样捍卫；同时给出成本承担主体的分布分析。

Conclusion: 影响是分类学而非二元；提出三点政策建议：继续鼓励AI扩散、投资印度本土劳动力市场研究、将技术采用与结构化的劳动者转岗通道相配套。

Abstract: Public debate on AI and Indian employment tends to compress a complicated phenomenon into a single binary: will AI take a given job, or not. This paper argues that the accurate picture is a taxonomy rather than a binary. AI rarely replaces a job wholesale; more often it automates a bundle of tasks within a job, and the size of that bundle varies with task structure, sector, regulatory setting, and, distinctively in India's case, the economics of AI access itself. We propose a four-level framework (task, job, occupation, labour-market outcome) and classify Indian job impact into two broad regimes: jobs where AI has automated most of the skill bundle and the human is reduced largely to validation, and jobs where the human remains the primary driver and AI functions as a productivity tool. Each regime splits further into policy-relevant sub-types. Drawing on Indian evidence from business process outsourcing, manufacturing, MSMEs, sanitation, financial services, law, and software engineering, we show that every job in our sample has had to be reimagined, through task-level augmentation, task reallocation, or a genuinely new role built around operating the machine, rather than simply eliminated or defended unchanged. We close with a distributional analysis of who bears the cost of this reimagining and a three-pronged policy recommendation: continue encouraging AI diffusion, invest in India-specific labour-market research, and pair adoption with structured worker-transition pathways.

</details>


### [227] [Silent on Code? A Computing Lens on Generative AI Guidance at Canadian Polytechnics and Research Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34094)
*Marco Ho,Michael Pin-Chuan Lin,Rishva Riteshkumar Amin,Daniel Chang,Jeeho Ryoo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文对加拿大13所理工学院与15所U15研究型大学共27份机构级生成式AI指导文件进行文档分析，发现两级机构普遍将课程层面决策下放给教师，指南虽提及代码但缺乏面向编程教育的实质性指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI工具已能编写、解释和调试代码，深刻改变计算机学生的学习方式与考核方式；而教师所依据的机构级指南多以论文写作场景为假设，与计算学科的实际需求脱节，因此需要系统考察现有机构指南在计算语境下的覆盖情况与缺口。

Method: 构建涵盖治理、学术诚信与考核、数据与法律问题、价值观、AI素养及计算视角六个维度的27项编码表，对2026年10月获取的Polytechnics Canada 13个成员与U15集团15个成员（共27份可获取指南）的公开机构级GenAI政策进行文档分析与编码。

Result: 两类机构均将课程层面决策交由教师；理工学院更常采取"除非教师许可否则禁止"的默认规则（8/13 对 4/14）；两者在要求披露、链接学术诚信政策、以及多数机构期望按学科调整规则上趋同。理工学院显著更多从劳动力培养角度论述GenAI（7/13 对 0/14），大学则更多涉及考核再设计；27所机构中12所提及代码，但几乎都仅将其作为多种输出类型之一，无一命名具体AI编程助手，也无针对代码归属、考核或许可的实质性指导。

Conclusion: 机构级指南并非对代码完全沉默，但将编程相关问题基本留给院系与教师；作者据此提出面向计算学科的学科级指南五项要素，并公开编码表与编码数据集。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) tools now write, explain, and debug code, which changes how computing students learn and how their work is assessed. Instructors, however, act within institutional guidance written largely with essays in mind. We report a document analysis of the public, institution-level GenAI guidance of the 13 members of Polytechnics Canada and the 15 members of the U15 Group of Canadian Research Universities, retrieved in October 2026. Using a 27-item codebook spanning governance, integrity and assessment, data and legal issues, values, AI literacy, and a computing lens, we find that both sectors leave course-level decisions to instructors, although polytechnics more often prohibit use unless the instructor permits it (8 of 13 vs. 4 of 14), and converge on disclosure, links to academic integrity policy, and, in most institutions, the expectation that programs adapt rules to their discipline. Polytechnics frame GenAI as workforce preparation substantively far more often (7 of 13 vs. 0 of 14), whereas universities more often address assessment redesign. Twelve of 27 institutions with retrievable guidance mention code, but almost always as one output type among many; none names an AI coding assistant or provides substantive code-specific guidance on attribution, assessment, or licensing. Institution-level guidance is thus not silent on code but leaves programming-specific questions largely to programs and instructors. We propose five elements of discipline-level guidance for computing and release our codebook and coded dataset.

</details>


### [228] [The Socratic PAUSE: safeguarding scientific judgement at AI speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34222)
*Abdulkadir Celik,Sir Bashir M. Al-Hashimi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文指出生成式AI极大加速了科学发现却远未加速其"辩护(justification)"，导致信心增长快于证据；提出"苏格拉底暂停"检查点及PAUSE五步协议，在关键决策关口对AI贡献进行结构化审视并记录决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能在数分钟内把初步科学问题转化为假设、方法、代码与解释，但"产生贡献"不等于"值得科学信赖"。研究者仍须检验其证据、假设与推论，并对依赖该贡献的决策负责。作者以爱因斯坦关于科学发现的草图（发现与辩护的区分）为视角，指出AI对发现的加速远超对辩护的加速，因而人机合作中"信心可能超越证据"。

Method: 概念性/规范性论证：(1) 借爱因斯坦的发现过程草图区分发现与辩护；(2) 提出信心超越证据的三条机制——递归强化(recursive reinforcement)、来源溯源丧失(loss of provenance)、对AI产出的失校准依赖(miscalibrated reliance)；(3) 定义"验证债务"(verification debt)：AI贡献在缺乏充分审查下被带入后续主张或决策而累积的未偿审查负担；(4) 据此提出"苏格拉底暂停"(Socratic Pause)作为关键决策关口的刻意检查点，并给出PAUSE五步协议：Pin down the claim、Ask and challenge、Uncover assumptions and alternatives、Scrutinise the evidence、Exercise judgement，最终形成接受/修改/拒绝/延后的记录化决定；(5) 说明AI研究工具如何支持而不得替代人类判断，并勾勒评估其是否改善验证、决策与独立理解的框架。

Result: 论文为规范性/立场型研究，未报告实证数据；其"结果"为：识别出信心膨胀的三条机制与验证债务的累积路径，构建了可操作的PAUSE五步检查协议与决策记录机制，并提出支持性AI工具设计原则及一套评估该实践在验证、决策质量与独立理解三方面效果的框架。

Conclusion: 目标是在保留用AI自由探索的同时，确保对工作负责者能够说明：检查了什么、什么仍不确定、以及为何下一步是正当的。即把AI贡献始终视为"暂定"，由研究者承担最终判断与问责。

Abstract: Generative AI can turn a tentative scientific question into hypotheses, methods, code and interpretations within minutes. Yet generating a contribution does not establish that it deserves scientific reliance: researchers must still examine its evidence, assumptions and implications, and remain accountable for the decision to rely on it. Using Einstein's sketch of scientific discovery as a lens, we argue that AI has accelerated discovery far more than justification. In human-AI research partnerships, confidence can therefore outgrow evidence through three mechanisms: recursive reinforcement, loss of provenance and miscalibrated reliance on AI-generated contributions. Verification debt accumulates when such contributions are carried into later claims or decisions without adequate scrutiny. Accordingly, we propose the Socratic Pause as a deliberate checkpoint at consequential decision gates, where an AI contribution remains provisional while a researcher examines its basis. The PAUSE protocol structures this examination through five steps: Pin down the claim, Ask and challenge, Uncover assumptions and alternatives, Scrutinise the evidence and Exercise judgement. It concludes with a recorded decision to accept, revise, reject or defer the contribution. We show how AI research tools can support this practice without displacing human judgement, and outline a framework for evaluating whether it improves verification, decision-making and independent understanding. The aim is to preserve the freedom to explore with AI while ensuring that those responsible for the work can say what was examined, what remains uncertain and why the next step is justified.

</details>


### [229] [Data Centers: The Error of Generalizing Environmental and Socioeconomic Impacts. The Need for a Contextualized Assessment of Computing Infrastructure in Brazil and Worldwide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34272)
*Elzo Brito dos Santos Filho*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文主张数据中心的环境与社会经济影响必须在其所在地区的尺度上评估，以巴西为例检验并反驳了将跨国证据直接外推的做法，并提出了多尺度评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于数据中心能耗、水耗、噪声、土地利用与就业的争论中，普遍存在"尺度错配"的方法论问题：用全球数据或在特定国家获得的证据，去推断位于截然不同地理与社会条件下的设施，导致结论外推缺乏有效性。

Method: 以巴西为案例进行情境化分析，考察其以可再生能源为主的电力结构及弃风弃光现象、闭环冷却的普遍性、国家与市级噪声法规、新Redata税收制度的水效要求，以及塞阿拉州Pecém数据中心的相关诉讼；同时评述一项关于北弗吉尼亚数据中心的研究，指出其外部效度与噪声测量方法的局限；在此基础上构建多尺度评估框架。

Result: 分析表明，数据中心的实际影响高度依赖所在地的地理、气候、能源、技术、经济与监管条件；北弗吉尼亚研究的结论与噪声数据在迁移至巴西情境时缺乏外部效度。

Conclusion: 国际证据应被用于界定"该问什么问题"，而非预先决定答案；该原则同样适用于"有害"与"安全"两类主张。数据中心影响评估须在适当的区域与技术尺度上进行。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of artificial intelligence and cloud computing has intensified debates about the environmental and socioeconomic impacts of data centers, including electricity and water consumption, noise, land use, and employment. A recurring methodological problem in these debates is the use of global data, or of evidence produced in a particular country, to draw conclusions about facilities located in very different settings. This paper argues that the impacts of a data center depend on the geographic, climatic, energy, technological, economic, and regulatory conditions of the territory where it operates, and must therefore be assessed at the appropriate territorial and technological scale. Using Brazil as a case, we discuss the country's predominantly renewable electricity mix and the curtailment of renewable generation, the reported prevalence of closed-loop cooling, national and municipal noise regulations, the water-efficiency requirement of the new Redata tax regime, and the ongoing litigation over the Pec{é}m data center in the state of Cear{á}. We also examine a recent study of data centers in Northern Virginia, showing the limits of its external validity and of its noise measurements. We propose a multiscale assessment framework and conclude that international evidence should inform which questions are asked, not predetermine the answers -- a principle that applies equally to claims of harm and to claims of safety.

</details>


### [230] [The cost of being seen as AI in group bargaining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34322)
*Tahmineh Godazgar,Nico Mutzner,Taha Yasseri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 在重复三人多数决议价实验中，被标注为"AI"的参与者（实为真人）在开局报价、最终联盟纳入和每轮收益上均系统性处于劣势（约低18%），该收益差距主要来自包含其的协议整体金额更低，且在全体一致同意时仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI被嵌入集体决策过程，人们对AI的感知可能影响谁能达成一致以及各人获得何种结果。作者希望检验"被感知为AI"是否会在多主体议价中引发分配性惩罚，即社会身份感知如何塑造集体决策中的利益分配。

Method: 采用重复三人多数决议价博弈，共2,234名参与者。每名对手被标记为"人类"或"AI"；指导语中将AI描述为独立行动或代表某人类行动；所有决策均由真人完成（标签为实验操纵）。因变量包括开局报价点数、是否被纳入最终协议、每轮收益及协议总额。

Result: 混合组中，被标为AI的玩家在开局报价中获得更少点数、被纳入最终协议的比例更低、每轮收益约低18%。这一差距主要由"包含该玩家的协议本身金额更低"解释；进一步分析显示即便三人全体一致时仍存在收益差距。

Conclusion: 对AI的社会感知会塑造集体决策中谁获益，结果并非源于AI能力差异，而是标签本身所致。这提示由AI代表其利益的个人可能承担实际代价，需关注AI中介决策中的公平性问题。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) becomes embedded in collective decision-making, people's perceptions of AI may shape who reaches agreement and what they receive. We studied this in a repeated three-player majoritarian bargaining game with 2,234 participants. Players saw each opponent identified as Human or AI. The instructions described AI as acting either independently or on behalf of a human. Human participants made every decision. In mixed groups, players presented as AI received fewer points in opening offers, were included in fewer final agreements and earned about 18% less per round than those presented as Human. Smaller payoffs in agreements that included them accounted for most of the observed gap. Further analysis found a payoff gap even when all three players agreed. Social perceptions of AI may therefore shape who benefits from collective decisions, with potential costs for people represented by AI.

</details>


### [231] [Towards Democratized Academic LLM Hosting: Practical insights from needs, implementations, and governance across Dutch institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34383)
*Azza Ahmed,Sreeparna Deb,Taylor Stone,Sven Warris,Aaron Ding,Joost Bengsch,Margriet Cox,Julio Alexandrino de Oliveira Filho,Alexandre Strube,Avishek Anand,Thomas Abeel,the Towards Democratized Academic LLM Hosting Workshop Consortium*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文基于与荷兰学术基础设施、科研支持、教育及政策相关方的工作坊，综合梳理了机构治理型学术LLM托管服务的当前实践与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术LLM托管正成为一类运维基础设施领域，在不同机构间呈现出反复出现的社会技术模式、张力与治理需求，但相关实践与问题尚缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 组织涵盖基础设施、科研支持、教育与政策相关利益方的研讨会，基于讨论综合归纳荷兰学术界机构治理型LLM服务的实践现状与开放问题。

Result: 识别出部署模式、能耗与利用率、治理、工具链、商业集成以及科研与教育用例等方面的挑战，并提出服务路径、协调机制与负责任运营的新兴方向。

Conclusion: 学术LLM托管需要跨机构协调与制度化治理框架，论文为服务路径设计与负责任运营提供了初步方向，但属于实践综合而非实证验证。

Abstract: Academic LLM hosting is emerging as an operational infrastructure domain with recurring socio-technical patterns, tensions, and governance requirements across institutions. Based on a workshop with academic infrastructure, research-support, education, and policy-adjacent stakeholders, this paper synthesizes current practices and open questions around institutionally governed LLM services for Dutch academia. It highlights challenges around deployment models, energy and utilization, governance, tooling, commercial integration, and research and education use cases, and identifies emerging directions for service pathways, coordination, and responsible operation.

</details>


### [232] [Decision and Support Mechanisms under Platform Information Overload: A Generative-Agent Experiment on College Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34709)
*Tingyu Dai,Xuanyou Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 利用大语言模型智能体模拟90名不同家庭背景的中国高考志愿填报者，发现信息过载本身不改善决策也不缩小差距；个性化建议能同时提升录取率（低SES由80%到97%）并缩小阶层差距至3个百分点，但代价是更高的学费与更差的专业匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中人们做重大个人决策时面对远超阅读能力的海量在线信息，但既有研究对“为何信息过载对部分人影响更大”缺乏解释；本文借助中国高考志愿填报这一高风险、结果可在录取分数线公布后客观评分的场景来考察该问题。

Method: 基于全国家庭调查数据设定家庭背景，构建90名语言模型智能体模拟申请者；每人独立填写8个志愿并在四个阶段后修订：听取父母与教师建议后、阅读20条真实网络信息流后、基于本人位次接受导师建议后、以及仅获情感支持的第二位导师介入后；每步均对志愿表打分并测量焦虑水平。

Result: 独立决策时各SES组录取率相近；家庭背景经由父母与教师介入后差距显现（低SES家长偏好低学费与就近院校，可达志愿减少），录取率呈高SES 100% vs 低SES 77%。平台信息流对所有人信息量相同却未缩小差距，申请者维持原决策但焦虑升高。个性化建议将低SES录取率由80%升至97%、差距降至3个百分点，但导致更高学费与更不偏好的专业；情感支持未进一步降低焦虑，却促使申请者回头采纳此前被忽略的有用信息。

Conclusion: 模拟结果表明：信息充裕时，信息量增加既不能改善决策也不减少不平等；个性化信息可同时改善决策与公平，但将不平等从结果端转移到成本端。

Abstract: People now make major personal decisions surrounded by far more online information than they can read, yet research says little about why information overload affects some people more than others. We study this question in Chinese college applications, a high-stakes decision made amid heavy online content where each list can be scored objectively once admission cutoffs are released.
  We simulated 90 applicants using language-model agents with family backgrounds from a national household survey. Each applicant wrote a list of eight programs alone and revised it four times: after parent and teacher advice, after reading a feed of twenty real online items, after a mentor gave advice based on the applicant's rank, and after a second mentor offered only emotional support. After each step, we scored the list and asked how anxious the applicant felt.
  When applicants decided alone, admission rates were similar across socioeconomic status (SES) groups. The gap appeared once family background entered through parents and teachers: low-SES parents asked for low tuition and schools near home, leaving fewer reachable programs, so admission was 100% for high-SES vs. 77% for low-SES applicants. The platform feed gave everyone the same amount of information but did not narrow this gap; applicants maintained their decisions while reporting higher anxiety. Personalized advice cut the gap to 3 points by raising low-SES admission from 80% to 97%, but led students to higher tuition and less preferred majors. Emotional support did not further lower anxiety, but helped applicants go back and use helpful items they had ignored.
  These simulation results suggest that when information is abundant, more of it neither improves decisions nor reduces inequality. Personalized information does both, while moving inequality from the outcome to its cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Learning the Platform, Not the Learner: Sequencing Shortcuts in the Offline Evaluation of Educational Recommender Systems and LLM Rankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33240)
*Youssef Jdidou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估教育推荐系统中"结构性预测器"与个性化推荐器及LLM排序器的离线准确率差异，指出离线准确率严重混淆了平台决定步骤与学习者自选步骤，并提出课程大纲感知的评估清单与工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育推荐器通常以预测日志中下一步活动的离线准确率进行比较，但下一步活动很大程度上由平台（教师、导师、引擎）决定，而非学习者意愿。这导致离线指标可能奖励"模仿平台"而非"服务学习者需求"，从而扭曲对推荐器实际有用性的判断。

Method: 在5个平台的8个公开数据集上，比较四类结构性预测器（仅用最后活动信息）、十个个性化推荐器以及0.5B–7B参数的开源LLM排序器，采用两种评估协议；通过仿真（已知学习者需求）分析结构性占比与平台决定步骤占比的关系；并尝试用"结构低可能性"对测试步骤加权。

Result: 结构性预测器达到任何方法最佳准确率的0.81–1.00（中位数0.95），主要依赖群体转移而非课程顺序；最佳个性化推荐器仅在5个数据集上显著更优，提升0.007–0.102 NDCG@10。所有方法在训练中未见过的转移步骤及学习者自选步骤上准确率大幅下降。LLM排序器在随机负样本下看似表现良好，但绝不明显优于词匹配基线；在结构合理的负样本下接近随机或落后于转移启发式，与模型规模无关。仿真显示结构性占比追踪平台决定步骤占比，且离线准确率将"学习者需求预言机"排在"平台模仿者"之下。以结构低可能性加权测试步骤可恢复与有用性的关联，并在8个数据集中7个揭示出显著个性化信号。

Conclusion: 离线准确率在教育推荐中系统性偏向平台决定行为，掩盖真实个性化价值。作者提出课程大纲感知的评估清单，并发布可报告任意推荐器相对结构基线的提升幅度的Python工具包。

Abstract: Educational recommenders are usually compared offline, by how well they predict the next activity in logs of tutoring systems, learning applications and MOOCs, where the next activity is largely decided by the platform: teachers assign problem sets, tutors draw problems until mastery, engines select questions. On eight public datasets from five platforms, we compare four structural predictors, which use nothing about the learner beyond the last activity, with ten personalised recommenders and with open-weight LLM rankers from 0.5 to 7 billion parameters, under two protocols. The structural predictors reach between 0.81 and 1.00 of the best accuracy of any method (median 0.95), mostly through population transitions rather than the course order; the best personalised recommender is significantly better on five datasets, by 0.007 to 0.102 NDCG@10, and on Eedi in three of four training runs. Every method is far less accurate on steps along transitions never seen in training and on steps chosen by learners. LLM rankers look competent with random negatives but are never much better than a word-matching baseline, and with negatives that are plausible under the course structure they stay close to random or well behind the transition heuristic, whatever their size. In a simulation with known learner needs, the structural share tracks the share of steps decided by the platform, and offline accuracy ranks an oracle of the learners' needs below imitators of the platform. Weighting test steps by how unlikely the structure makes them restores much of the link with usefulness and, on the real data, reveals a significant personalisation signal on seven of the eight datasets. We propose a syllabus-aware evaluation checklist and release a Python package that reports the lift of any recommender over the structure.

</details>


### [234] [Multi-Modal Causal Disentanglement for Multi-Domain Sequential Audio Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33628)
*Yu Ren,Pengcheng Li,Weijun Xu,Hengzhong Zhou,Shufeng Ye,Qiang Xiao,Dongjing Wang,Chuanjiang Luo,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CAMAR——面向多领域音频推荐的多模态因果解耦框架，将用户表示分解为领域不变与领域特有成分以抑制负迁移，并用残差量化构建语义ID增强跨域语义耦合，在公开与工业数据集及网易云音乐在线A/B实验中均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐平台内容涵盖歌曲、歌单、播客等异构音频领域，多领域序列推荐（MDSR）面临两大障碍：一是异构领域联合优化易产生负迁移，高流量领域主导共享参数空间并压制弱势领域；二是传统基于ID的协同信号难以桥接语义相关但行为重叠稀少的领域。

Method: 从因果视角将用户行为建模为领域不变偏好与领域特有偏好的联合结果，据此将用户表示分解为不变分量与特有分量，并施加领域对抗不变性约束与正交性约束，把跨域交互限制在不变子空间内以抑制有害迁移路径；同时基于预训练文本、视觉、音频特征，通过残差量化生成可训练的语义ID嵌入，实现端到端的多模态适配；再以迁移网络为不活跃领域补全用户表示，缓解跨域冷启动。

Result: 在公开数据集与工业数据集上相较强基线取得一致提升；在网易云音乐（国内最大音乐流媒体平台之一）的在线A/B测试中，三个领域的主流量场景均获得一致性增益，验证了其在大规模多领域音频推荐中的有效性。

Conclusion: 因果解耦式的表示分解结合语义ID的多模态对齐，是缓解多领域音频推荐中负迁移与跨域语义割裂的有效途径，且具备工业级可落地性。

Abstract: Modern music platforms expose users to heterogeneous audio content domains such as songs, playlists, and podcasts. Multi-domain sequential recommendation (MDSR) seeks to exploit such cross-domain behavior, yet two obstacles persist in practice. First, joint optimization across heterogeneous domains is prone to negative transfer, with high-traffic domains dominating the shared parameter space and suppressing weaker domains. Second, conventional ID-based collaborative signals fail to bridge domains that are semantically related but exhibit little behavioral overlap. This paper presents CAMAR, a multi-modal causal disentanglement framework for multi-domain audio recommendation. From a causal perspective, user behavior is modeled as the joint outcome of a domain-invariant preference and domain-specific preferences. Guided by this view, CAMAR factorizes user representations into domain-invariant and domain-specific components and enforces domain adversarial invariance together with orthogonality, restricting cross-domain interaction to the invariant subspace and suppressing harmful transfer paths. To strengthen cross-domain semantic coupling, CAMAR derives trainable semantic-ID embeddings from pre-trained text, vision, and audio features through residual quantization, yielding an end-to-end multi-modal adaptation tailored to recommendation. A transfer network imputes user representations for inactive domains, mitigating cross-domain cold start. Experiments on both public and industrial datasets demonstrate consistent gains over strong baselines. In online A/B tests on NetEase Cloud Music, one of China's largest music streaming platforms, CAMAR delivers consistent gains across all three domains in their main traffic scenarios, validating its effectiveness in multi-domain audio recommendation at scale.

</details>


### [235] [Structure-Aware Multimodal Retrieval Under Data Scarcity, Privacy Constraints and Out-of-Domain Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33649)
*Argyrios Zafeiriou,Dionysios Koulouris,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种结构感知的图检索流水线，仅依赖版面几何与本地开源8B VLM，在无标签、无训练对、图像不出本地硬件的约束下，从参考图册中检索插图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法医病理学、牙科学、地质学等专家领域的图册插图检索面临三重约束：无标签与无训练对、图像常不可离开本地硬件、待检索图像超出图像-文本编码器的训练分布，现有跨模态方法难以满足。

Method: 引入基于几何的图文配对方法：模型仅接收检测框的示意图（不含像素与文本），为每张图选择对应图注；配对与检索在三个私有语料（29本书、10,421幅图、218条查询）上用盲评人工打分评估，对比词法基线、生成式描述与五种图像-文本编码器。

Result: 几何配对正确率在三领域分别为96%、92%、77%，优于两种文档解析器中较优者的76%、45%、53%；托管模型基于示意图准确率相当，而将坐标作为文本输入则下降22个百分点。图注检索nDCG@5为0.85、0.87、0.76，最佳跨模态编码器仅0.54、0.51、0.27，生成描述为0.41、0.28、0.30；配对错误会传导至检索。

Conclusion: 该流水线以小型本地模型、无域内训练实现高精度图检索，显著优于通用与生物医学图像-文本编码器；其纯几何配对在五种方法中最准确，且不向外部（含托管模型）暴露任何内容。代码与提示词已开源。

Abstract: Purpose: Figure retrieval from reference atlases in expert domains like forensic pathology, dentistry and geology must work without labels or training pairs, on content that often cannot leave local hardware, and on images outside the training data of image-text encoders. This work proposes a structure-aware pipeline that satisfies all three, retrieving figures through the page structure recovered by layout geometry and a local open-weight 8B VLM. Methods: A geometry-based pairing method is introduced, in which the model receives a schematic of the detected boxes and selects each picture's caption without access to pixels or text. Caption retrieval is evaluated on three private corpora (29 books, 10,421 figures, 218 queries) with blind human grading, against a lexical baseline, generated descriptions and five image-text encoders. Results: Geometric pairing was correct for 96%, 92% and 77% of pictures in the three domains, against 76%, 45% and 53% for the better of two document parsers. A hosted model given the schematic was equally accurate, while coordinates as text lost 22 points. Caption retrieval obtained an nDCG@5 of 0.85, 0.87 and 0.76, against 0.54, 0.51 and 0.27 for the best cross-modal encoder and 0.41, 0.28 and 0.30 for generated descriptions. Pairing errors carried over to retrieval. Conclusion: The proposed pipeline achieves accurate figure retrieval with small local models and no in-domain training, where general and biomedical image-text encoders fall far behind. Its geometry-only pairing is the most accurate of five methods and exposes no content, even to a hosted model. Code and prompts are released.

</details>


### [236] [OneQuery: Unified Sequence-to-Structure Generation for Multilingual E-commerce Query Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33653)
*Mingyu Zhang,Jiejie Qin,Jianhui Ji,Rong Xiao,Meishan Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 OneQuery：电商领域大模型驱动的多语言查询理解统一生成框架，将四个子任务重构为受模式约束的 Sequence-to-Structure 自回归生成，并采用三阶段渐进训练与多任务强化学习对齐，在 Aliexpress 线上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言电商搜索需将跨语言、语言模式多样的用户查询转化为标准化语义表示以支撑召回与排序。传统查询理解流水线存在误差累积、子任务各自独立优化而忽视相互关联、维护成本高等问题。

Method: 基于电商领域专用大语言模型构建统一生成框架，将语种识别、拼写纠错、翻译、查询标注四个子任务重构为由预定义 schema 约束的 Sequence-to-Structure 自回归生成过程；设计三阶段渐进式训练流程，并结合多任务强化学习对齐。

Result: 在所有任务上相较基线取得一致性提升，且在不同骨干模型规模下仍保持有效；在 AliExpress 电商搜索场景的线上实验进一步验证了其工业部署的实用价值。

Conclusion: 统一生成范式的查询理解框架可有效缓解误差累积与子任务割裂问题，降低维护成本，并具备工业级多语言电商搜索的可落地性。

Abstract: Multilingual e-commerce search requires robust query understanding to convert cross-lingual user queries with varied linguistic patterns into standardized semantic representations that support retrieval and ranking. However, traditional Query Understanding systems suffer from error accumulation, separately optimized subtasks that ignore their mutual correlations, and high maintenance costs. To address these issues, this paper proposes OneQuery, a unified generative framework for multilingual query understanding based on domain-specific large language models in e-commerce. We reformulate four subtasks, namely Language Recognition, Spelling Correction, Translation, and Query Tagging, into a unified Sequenceto-Structure autoregressive generation process constrained by predefined schemas, and design a three-stage progressive training pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning alignment. OneQuery demonstrates consistent gains over baselines across all tasks and remains effective across different backbone sizes, and online experiments in the AliExpress e-commerce search scenario further confirm its practical value for industrial deployment.

</details>


### [237] [Privacy-Aware Adaptive Client Selection for Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33890)
*Ali Shams Saryazd,Abolfazl Gandomi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PACS-FedRAG 提出隐私感知的自适应客户端选择器，在十客户中选三客户，将查询接收方减少70%，但证据重叠0.372与答案准确率0.287偏低，且扰动配置文件不提供查询或段落保密性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦检索增强生成需从分布式语料检索证据，但向所有客户端广播查询会加重检索负担并暴露查询。作者希望用在线选择机制在少量客户端上检索，以降低通信与暴露风险。

Method: 构建在线选择器 PACS-FedRAG，综合扰动后的语义画像、证据反馈、时延、负载成本与探索项进行客户端打分与选择；评测包括端到端基准（10客户选3）与仅检索评测（复用全广播基线的不同融合平局打破策略）。

Result: 端到端基准报告证据重叠0.372、答案准确率0.287；跨不相交客户语料的排序融合存在0.375的重叠上限，削弱其作为相关性度量的解释力；答案准确率低于固定标签诊断值0.645，且缺少原始生成响应以供解析器核验。选择3个客户端使查询接收方减少70%。

Conclusion: 该方法能显著降低查询广播范围，但质量保持性无法由现有指标确认：需修正基线并引入答案级新评测；同时配置文件扰动不构成对查询与段落的机密性保障。

Abstract: Federated retrieval-augmented generation retrieves evidence from distributed corpora. However, broadcasting queries to all clients increases the retrieval burden and query exposure.
