We present Privacy-Aware Adaptive Client Selection for Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation (PACS-FedRAG), an online selector that takes into account perturbed semantic profiles along with evidence feedback, latency, payload cost, and exploration. In a ten-client setting where three clients are chosen, the archived end-to-end benchmark reports an evidence overlap of 0.372 and an answer accuracy of 0.287. The retrieval-only evaluation, on the other hand, reuses a full-broadcast baseline with a different fusion tie-breaking policy. Rank fusion across disjoint client corpora imposes an overlap ceiling of 0.375, limiting its interpretation as a relevance measure. Answer accuracy is below the fixed-label diagnostic of 0.645, and raw generator responses are unavailable for parser verification. Corrected baselines and a new answer-level evaluation are needed to assess whether quality is preserved. Selecting three clients reduces the number of query recipients by 70%. Profile perturbation does not provide confidentiality of queries or passages.

</details>


### [238] [MulCLIP: A Multi-level Alignment Framework for Enhancing Fine-grained Long-context CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34083)
*Chau Truong,Hieu Ta Quang,Zhenzhen Liu,Dung D. Le*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MulCLIP 是一个端到端的多层级对齐框架，无需区域提议即可利用自然长文本结构实现视觉-语言对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 等视觉-语言模型因训练于短标题而难以处理长而详细的描述；现有方法借助区域提议对齐视觉区域与句子，但部署成本高昂。

Method: 通过对不同文本粒度施加兼容的训练信号实现多层对齐：长标题与短摘要的全局对比对齐；在局部校准特征上进行词-块重建（Word-Patch Reconstruction）以捕捉样本内词-块语义；以及子标题聚合的块对齐（Subcaption-Aggregated Patch）实现上下文丰富的子标题定位。

Result: 在覆盖不同标题长度的基准上取得一致提升；消融实验证实各目标互补并共同提升整体框架性能。

Conclusion: MulCLIP 证明无需区域提议即可有效建模长文本的多粒度语义，为长描述场景下的视觉-语言对齐提供了低成本方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP excel at image-text alignment but struggle with long, detailed descriptions due to training on short captions. Recent methods address this limitation using region proposals to align visual regions with sentences, but at substantial deployment cost. We present MulCLIP, an end-to-end multi-level alignment framework that directly exploits natural long-text structure without region proposals. Instead of simply stacking objectives, MulCLIP aligns different textual granularities through compatible training signals: global contrastive alignment for long captions and short summaries, Word-Patch Reconstruction over locally calibrated features for within-sample word-patch semantics, and Subcaption-Aggregated Patch alignment for context-rich subcaption grounding. Experiments on benchmarks spanning varying caption lengths show consistent gains, and ablations confirm that the proposed objectives are complementary and jointly improve the overall framework. Code will be released at https://github.com/truongchau2602/MulCLIP.

</details>


### [239] [RecField: Embedding Recommendations into Spatial Movie Organization as Affordances for Serendipitous Encounters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34330)
*Kenta Oku*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出 RecField——一种空间化的电影组织界面，把推荐作为环境可供性（environmental affordance）嵌入用户自发的整理活动中，并通过实验表明嵌入式推荐既能直接通过条目采纳、也能间接通过激发用户自主探索与重新评估来促进机缘性体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有机缘性推荐研究主要聚焦于算法层面（推荐意外但有用的条目），但机缘性日益被视为由算法输出与系统特征共同塑造的用户体验。作者希望重新定位推荐的角色：不是用户直接追求的主要目标，而是嵌入其他活动之中的环境可供性，从而在用户从事前景任务时为其创造发现机会。

Method: 提出 RecField 空间化电影组织界面，用户可自由将电影排列到"收藏区"（collection zones）；每个收藏区提供基于属性的推荐列表，并按推荐视角加注标签。采用被试间实验（N=57），前景任务为创建与组织个人电影画像，考察推荐对发现体验的影响，并在环境层面与单个电影层面分别测量机缘性及相遇相关评价。

Result: 推荐对"环境层面"的机缘性感知无显著影响，但相遇相关维度的评分略高，效应量偏小。在单个电影层面，推荐条件下的收藏条目在"激发观看欲望"和"拓宽偏好"上评分显著更高，而在新颖性与意外性上无显著差异。值得注意的是，推荐条件下被评价的收藏条目中仅 14% 直接来自推荐列表；即便仅分析通过其他途径添加的收藏条目，评分差异的效应量仍相近。

Conclusion: 嵌入式推荐可同时通过直接条目采纳与间接激发用户自主探索和重新评估两条路径贡献于机缘性体验；研究强调应在环境层面与条目层面双重评估机缘性，并指出仅依赖直接推荐采纳率来评价机缘性存在局限。

Abstract: Research on serendipity-oriented recommender systems has focused on algorithms that recommend unexpected yet useful items. However, serendipity is increasingly viewed as a user experience shaped by both algorithmic outputs and system features. This study positions recommendations not as the primary goal users pursue but as an environmental affordance that creates opportunities for discovery during other activities. We propose RecField, a spatial movie organization interface where users freely arrange movies into collection zones. For each collection, RecField provides attribute-based recommendation lists labeled according to their recommendation perspectives. A between-subjects experiment ($N$ = 57) examined the effects of recommendations on discoveries made during a foreground task of creating and organizing personal movie profiles. Although recommendations did not significantly affect environment-level perceived serendipity, ratings of encounter-related aspects tended to be higher, with small effect sizes. At the individual-movie level, favorites in the recommendation condition received significantly higher ratings for stimulating the desire to watch and broadening preferences, but not for novelty or unexpectedness. Notably, only 14\% of evaluated favorites in the recommendation condition were added directly from recommendation lists. Differences in ratings showed similar effect sizes even when the analysis was restricted to favorites added through other routes. These findings suggest that embedded recommendations can contribute to serendipitous experiences both directly through item acceptance and indirectly by prompting users' own exploration and re-evaluation. They also highlight the value of evaluating serendipity at both the environment and item levels and the limitations of relying solely on direct recommendation acceptance rates.

</details>


### [240] [ConTra: A Training Framework for Effective Contextual Retrieval via Prefix Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34366)
*Jiajun Hou,Jingbang Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 ConTra，一种用强化学习训练轻量前缀生成器的框架，在索引时为每个 chunk 生成携带上下文的文本前缀，训练奖励融合冻结的嵌入检索与重排模型反馈、事实性校验和长度控制；同时发布 RealContextBench 基准。2B 模型在 ConTEB 与 RealContextBench 上超过提示式 9B 基线和 PPLX-4B 上下文编码器最多 3.15 分，短文档索引速度快 7.74 倍和 2.15 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档需切分为独立索引的 chunk，但切分会剥离背景与标识信息，导致上下文丢失。现有方案两难：把上下文压进 chunk 向量则文本重排器无法使用；用提示生成文本前缀虽可同时服务向量检索与重排，但索引时生成代价高，且效果不及上下文化编码器。

Method: 以强化学习训练轻量前缀生成器：每个 chunk 生成一个上下文前缀，构成增强文本并在索引时一次性存储文本与向量，供多查询复用；奖励由冻结的 embedding 模型与 reranker 的反馈、事实性校验、长度约束共同构成。另构建 RealContextBench，含 5 个应用集合与 3 种受控条件以隔离干扰文本影响。

Result: 在 ConTEB 与 RealContextBench 上，ConTra-2B 优于 Anthropic 方法的提示式 9B 基线及 PPLX-4B 上下文化编码器，整体得分最高领先 3.15 分；短文档索引速度分别为其 7.74 倍与 2.15 倍。仅在 ConTEB 上后训练，经重排后在 RealContextBench 上仍领先各独立方法，且对随机与主题相近干扰项的敏感度低于提示式 2B 基线。

Conclusion: 将上下文以可复用文本前缀形式"随 chunk 旅行"，能在保持检索与重排兼容性的同时显著降低索引成本，小模型通过 RL 后训练即可超越更大提示式基线并具备更好的抗干扰鲁棒性。

Abstract: Data management systems organize large text collections for tasks such as finding relevant studies and extracting contract clauses. Systems make long documents searchable by splitting them into independently indexed chunks. Chunking can separate text from its identifying details. The indexing challenge is to keep each chunk independently searchable while preserving the context needed to find and rank it. Existing methods either encode that context in chunk vectors or attach it as a text prefix. Context encoded only in vectors is unavailable to text rerankers. Prompted prefixes serve both vector search and reranking, but are costly to generate at indexing time and can underperform contextual encoders. We propose ConTra (Context That Travels), a reinforcement learning framework for training lightweight prefix generators. Its reward combines feedback from frozen embedding and reranking models with factual verification and length control. ConTra generates one prefix per chunk during index construction and stores the augmented text and its embedding for reuse across queries. To evaluate contextual retrieval across workloads and under interference, we introduce RealContextBench, a benchmark with five application collections and three controlled conditions that isolate the effect of distracting text. On ConTEB and RealContextBench, ConTra-2B outperforms the prompted 9B baseline using Anthropic's method and the PPLX-4B contextual encoder, with a margin of up to 3.15 points over their best overall scores, while indexing short documents 7.74 times and 2.15 times as fast as those baselines, respectively. Post-trained only on ConTEB, it leads the standalone methods on RealContextBench after reranking and is less sensitive than the prompted 2B baseline to random and topically similar distractors. Code, datasets, and trained models are available at https://github.com/SStan1/ConTra.

</details>


### [241] [Grounding User Preferences in Shared Multimodal Concepts for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34436)
*Ao Xu,Jinhui Huang,Wenqiang Tang,Tiehua Mei,Hongru Hou,Hengrui Chen,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MKGE 是一个多模态知识图谱推荐框架，将视觉与文本中的反复出现的语义因素表示为规范可寻址的概念，并联合建模用户-物品直接证据与概念中介的偏好信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐虽借助图像和文本提升了物品理解，但用户偏好仍被编码为与单个物品绑定的隐式信号，反复出现的语义因素缺乏可跨物品共享的持久身份，导致不同物品上的证据难以累积到同一偏好维度。

Method: 从物品图像与标题中归纳出领域自适应的概念词表；将每个用户的交互历史锚定到有支撑的概念上；在知识图谱上联合建模直接的用户-物品证据与概念中介的偏好信号，使反复语义成为共享坐标；并进行消融与受控实验（移除或重连用户图、检验概念身份规范化与个性化用户-概念对应关系）。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，MKGE 在全部六项报告指标上均稳定优于协同过滤、知识图谱、多模态及近期 LLM/MLLM 推荐方法；移除或重连用户图时性能下降明显，尤其在低活跃用户上增益最显著。

Conclusion: 规范化的多模态概念是进行偏好建模与迁移的有效单元；概念身份规范化与个性化用户-概念对应均不可或缺。代码、构建的知识图谱与提示词已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation has substantially advanced item understanding by incorporating rich visual and textual content. Yet user preferences are still commonly encoded as implicit signals tied to individual items, leaving recurring semantic factors without persistent identities that can be shared across items. We introduce MKGE, a multimodal knowledge graph framework that represents these factors as canonical, addressable concepts. MKGE induces a domain-adaptive concept vocabulary from item images and titles, grounds each user's interaction history in supported concepts, and jointly models direct user-item evidence and concept-mediated preference signals. By turning recurring semantics into shared coordinates, MKGE enables evidence from different items to accumulate around the same preference factors. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate that MKGE consistently outperforms collaborative, knowledge-graph, multimodal, and recent LLM/MLLM-based recommenders across all six reported metrics. Controlled studies further demonstrate the importance of canonical concept identities and personalized user-concept correspondence, with especially pronounced gains over removing or rewiring the user graph for low-activity users. These results establish canonical multimodal concepts as effective units for preference modeling and transfer. Code, constructed knowledge graphs, and prompts are available at https://github.com/Garden1a-X/MKGE.

</details>
